<script>
/* ===== FIRE-BABYS-NET: multiplayer client library (inlined) ===== */
/* Fire Babys multiplayer client — fire-babys-net.js
 *
 * Classic script (no modules, no dependencies). Exposes window.FireBabysNet.
 *
 *   const net = new FireBabysNet({ server: "wss://<your-worker>.workers.dev" });
 *   const { code } = await net.createLobby({ mode: "4v4" });
 *   await net.connect(code, { name: "Jeff" });
 *   net.on("lobby", (state) => renderPlayerList(state));
 *   net.on("pos", (p) => moveRemoteDot(p.id, p.x, p.y));
 *   net.setReady(true);
 *   // host: net.start();
 *   net.sendMove({ x: 100, y: 200, dir: 90 });   // throttled to 12Hz internally
 *   net.sendAction({ kind: "extinguish", x, y });
 *
 * Protocol constants mirror worker/src/protocol.js — keep in sync.
 */
(function (global) {
  "use strict";

  var MAX_PLAYERS = 30;
  var MODES = { "2v2": 4, "4v4": 8, "6v6": 12, "7v7": 14, "15v15": 30, "catch": 30 };
  var MOVE_HZ = 12;
  var PING_INTERVAL_MS = 20000;
  var RECONNECT_DELAYS = [1000, 2000, 5000, 10000]; // backoff steps

  function isValidMode(m) {
    return m === "coop" || Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(MODES, m);
  }

  function FireBabysNet(opts) {
    opts = opts || {};
    this.server = (opts.server || "").replace(/\/$/, "");
    this.moveHz = opts.moveHz || MOVE_HZ;
    this.handlers = {};
    this.ws = null;
    this.code = null;
    this.playerId = null;
    this.token = null;
    this.isHost = false;
    this.state = null; // last lobby snapshot
    this.connected = false;
    this.reconnectAttempt = 0;
    this.reconnectTimer = null;
    this.shouldReconnect = false;
    this.lastMoveSent = 0;
    this.lastPing = 0;
    this.latencyMs = null;
    this._name = "";
  }

  // ------------------------------------------------------------ events
  FireBabysNet.prototype.on = function (evt, fn) {
    (this.handlers[evt] = this.handlers[evt] || []).push(fn);
    return this;
  };
  FireBabysNet.prototype.off = function (evt, fn) {
    var h = this.handlers[evt];
    if (!h) return this;
    this.handlers[evt] = h.filter(function (f) { return f !== fn; });
    return this;
  };
  FireBabysNet.prototype.emit = function (evt, data) {
    var h = this.handlers[evt] || [];
    for (var i = 0; i < h.length; i++) {
      try { h[i](data); } catch (e) { /* never let a handler kill the socket */ }
    }
  };

  FireBabysNet.prototype.httpBase = function () {
    return this.server.replace(/^wss:/, "https:").replace(/^ws:/, "http:");
  };

  // ------------------------------------------------------------ REST
  FireBabysNet.prototype.createLobby = function (opts) {
    var self = this;
    opts = opts || {};
    var mode = isValidMode(opts.mode) ? opts.mode : "coop";
    return fetch(this.httpBase() + "/api/lobby", {
      method: "POST",
      headers: { "content-type": "application/json" },
      body: JSON.stringify({ mode: mode }),
    })
      .then(function (r) { return r.json(); })
      .then(function (j) {
        if (!j.ok) throw new Error(j.error || "create failed");
        return j.code;
      });
  };

  FireBabysNet.listLobbies = function (server) {
    var base = String(server || "").replace(/\/$/, "")
      .replace(/^wss:/, "https:").replace(/^ws:/, "http:");
    return fetch(base + "/api/lobbies")
      .then(function (r) { return r.json(); })
      .then(function (j) { return j.ok ? j.lobbies : []; });
  };

  FireBabysNet.prototype.getLobby = function (code) {
    return fetch(this.httpBase() + "/lobby/" + encodeURIComponent(code))
      .then(function (r) { return r.json(); })
      .then(function (j) { return j.ok ? j.state : null; });
  };

  // ------------------------------------------------------------ websocket
  FireBabysNet.prototype.connect = function (code, opts) {
    var self = this;
    opts = opts || {};
    this.code = String(code || "").toUpperCase();
    this._name = String(opts.name || "Player").slice(0, 16);
    this.shouldReconnect = true;
    this.reconnectAttempt = 0;
    return this._open(this._name, opts.token || this.token);
  };

  FireBabysNet.prototype._open = function (name, token) {
    var self = this;
    return new Promise(function (resolve, reject) {
      var url = self.server + "/lobby/" + encodeURIComponent(self.code) +
        "/ws?name=" + encodeURIComponent(name) +
        (token ? "&token=" + encodeURIComponent(token) : "");
      var ws;
      try { ws = new WebSocket(url); } catch (e) { reject(e); return; }
      var settled = false;
      var timeout = setTimeout(function () {
        if (!settled) { settled = true; try { ws.close(); } catch (e) {} reject(new Error("connect timeout")); }
      }, 10000);

      ws.onopen = function () { /* wait for welcome */ };
      ws.onmessage = function (ev) { self._onMessage(ev.data, resolve, reject, function(){ settled = true; clearTimeout(timeout); }); };
      ws.onerror = function () {
        if (!settled) { settled = true; clearTimeout(timeout); reject(new Error("websocket error")); }
      };
      ws.onclose = function () { self._onClose(); };
      self.ws = ws;
    });
  };

  FireBabysNet.prototype._onMessage = function (data, resolve, reject, settle) {
    var msg;
    try { msg = JSON.parse(data); } catch (e) { return; }
    if (!msg || typeof msg.t !== "string") return;

    // First message must be welcome (resolves connect()).
    if (msg.t === "welcome") {
      this.playerId = msg.playerId;
      this.token = msg.token;
      this.isHost = !!msg.isHost;
      this.state = msg.state || null;
      this.connected = true;
      this.reconnectAttempt = 0;
      this._startPing();
      try { localStorage.setItem("fbmp:" + this.code, this.token); } catch (e) {}
      settle();
      resolve({ playerId: this.playerId, isHost: this.isHost, reconnected: !!msg.reconnected });
      this.emit("welcome", msg);
      if (msg.state) this.emit("lobby", msg.state);
      return;
    }
    if (msg.t === "error") { this.emit("error", msg.msg || "error"); return; }

    switch (msg.t) {
      case "lobby":
        this.state = msg.state || this.state;
        this.emit("lobby", msg.state);
        break;
      case "joined": case "rejoined": case "left":
        this.emit("presence", msg);
        break;
      case "host":
        if (this.state) this.state.hostId = msg.hostId;
        this.isHost = (msg.hostId === this.playerId);
        this.emit("host", msg);
        break;
      case "pos": this.emit("pos", msg); break;
      case "action": this.emit("action", msg); break;
      case "start":
        this.state = msg.state || this.state;
        this.emit("start", msg);
        break;
      case "end": this.emit("end", msg); break;
      case "pong":
        if (msg.ts) this.latencyMs = Date.now() - msg.ts;
        this.emit("pong", { latencyMs: this.latencyMs });
        break;
      default: this.emit("message", msg);
    }
  };

  FireBabysNet.prototype._send = function (obj) {
    if (this.ws && this.ws.readyState === 1) {
      try { this.ws.send(JSON.stringify(obj)); return true; } catch (e) {}
    }
    return false;
  };

  FireBabysNet.prototype._startPing = function () {
    var self = this;
    this._stopPing();
    this._pingTimer = setInterval(function () {
      self._send({ t: "ping", ts: Date.now() });
    }, PING_INTERVAL_MS);
  };
  FireBabysNet.prototype._stopPing = function () {
    if (this._pingTimer) clearInterval(this._pingTimer);
    this._pingTimer = null;
  };

  FireBabysNet.prototype._onClose = function () {
    this.connected = false;
    this._stopPing();
    this.emit("close", {});
    if (!this.shouldReconnect || !this.code) return;
    var self = this;
    var delay = RECONNECT_DELAYS[Math.min(this.reconnectAttempt, RECONNECT_DELAYS.length - 1)];
    this.reconnectAttempt++;
    this.emit("reconnecting", { attempt: this.reconnectAttempt, delayMs: delay });
    this.reconnectTimer = setTimeout(function () {
      var token = self.token;
      try { token = token || localStorage.getItem("fbmp:" + self.code); } catch (e) {}
      self._open(self._name, token).catch(function () { /* will retry via onclose */ });
    }, delay);
  };

  FireBabysNet.prototype.disconnect = function () {
    this.shouldReconnect = false;
    if (this.reconnectTimer) clearTimeout(this.reconnectTimer);
    this._stopPing();
    try { if (this.ws) this.ws.close(); } catch (e) {}
    this.ws = null;
    this.connected = false;
  };

  // ------------------------------------------------------------ game actions
  FireBabysNet.prototype.setReady = function (ready) {
    return this._send({ t: "ready", ready: !!ready });
  };
  FireBabysNet.prototype.setMode = function (mode) {
    if (!isValidMode(mode)) return false;
    return this._send({ t: "mode", mode: mode });
  };
  FireBabysNet.prototype.start = function () { return this._send({ t: "start" }); };
  FireBabysNet.prototype.end = function (results) {
    return this._send({ t: "end", results: results || null });
  };

  /** Throttled movement send. Call every frame; library caps to moveHz. */
  FireBabysNet.prototype.sendMove = function (move) {
    var now = Date.now();
    var minInterval = 1000 / this.moveHz;
    if (now - this.lastMoveSent < minInterval) return false;
    this.lastMoveSent = now;
    return this._send({
      t: "move",
      x: +move.x || 0, y: +move.y || 0,
      dir: +move.dir || 0,
      anim: String(move.anim || "").slice(0, 16),
      ts: now,
    });
  };

  /** Unthrottled action (attack, extinguish, etc.). Keep data small (<512B JSON). */
  FireBabysNet.prototype.sendAction = function (action) {
    return this._send({
      t: "action",
      kind: String(action.kind || "").slice(0, 24),
      x: +action.x || 0, y: +action.y || 0,
      data: action.data || null,
    });
  };

  FireBabysNet.MAX_PLAYERS = MAX_PLAYERS;
  FireBabysNet.MODES = MODES;

  global.FireBabysNet = FireBabysNet;
})(typeof window !== "undefined" ? window : this);

</script>
<script>

/* ---- PHONE ERROR REPORTER (2026-10-03): shows JS errors on-screen so phone-only bugs can be diagnosed via screenshot ---- */
(function(){
  var errBox=null, errCount=0;
  function showErr(msg){
    try{
      errCount++;
      if(errCount>8) return; // don't flood the screen
      if(!errBox){
        errBox=document.createElement('div');
        errBox.id='phoneerr';
        errBox.style.cssText='position:fixed;left:8px;right:8px;bottom:8px;z-index:99999;background:rgba(180,20,20,.93);color:#fff;font:12px/1.4 monospace;padding:8px 10px;border-radius:8px;max-height:140px;overflow:auto;white-space:pre-wrap;';
        document.body.appendChild(errBox);
      }
      errBox.style.display='block';
      errBox.textContent='\u26A0\uFE0F '+msg+'\n'+errBox.textContent;
    }catch(e){}
  }
  window.addEventListener('error', function(e){
    showErr('ERR: '+(e.message||'unknown')+' @ '+String(e.filename||'').split('/').pop()+':'+(e.lineno||'?'));
  });
  window.addEventListener('unhandledrejection', function(e){
    var r=e.reason; showErr('PROMISE: '+((r&&r.message)||String(r||'?')).slice(0,160));
  });
  window.__showErr=showErr;
})();
'use strict';
/* ================= PERFORMANCE: graphics quality (FBQ) =================
   FBQ.level: 'high' | 'medium' | 'low', persisted in fb_quality.
   Mobile auto-defaults to medium. Changing quality in Settings reloads
   the page (antialias can only be set at renderer creation). */
var FBQ=(function(){
  var q=''; try{ q=localStorage.getItem('fb_quality')||''; }catch(e){}
  var mob=/Android|iPhone|iPad|iPod|Mobile|webOS|BlackBerry/i.test(navigator.userAgent||'');
  if(q!=='high'&&q!=='medium'&&q!=='low') q=mob?'medium':'high';
  var dpr=window.devicePixelRatio||1;
  return {
    level:q, isMobile:mob,
    antialias:(q==='high'),
    pixelRatio:(q==='high'?Math.min(dpr,2):q==='medium'?Math.min(dpr,1.5):1),
    shadows:(q!=='low'),
    shadowSoft:(q==='high'),
    shadowSize:(q==='high'?2048:1024),
    particleScale:(q==='high'?1:q==='medium'?0.6:0.35),
    emberCount:(q==='high'?260:q==='medium'?150:60),
    botHz:(q==='low'?10:20)
  };
})();

/* ---------------- tiny canvas-texture helpers ---------------- */
function cnv(w,h){ var c=document.createElement('canvas'); c.width=w; c.height=h; return c; }
function tex(c){ var t=new THREE.CanvasTexture(c); t.anisotropy=2; return t; }
function shade(hex, f){ // darken/lighten #rrggbb by factor f
  var n=parseInt(hex.slice(1),16), r=(n>>16)&255, g=(n>>8)&255, b=n&255;
  r=Math.max(0,Math.min(255,Math.round(r*f))); g=Math.max(0,Math.min(255,Math.round(g*f))); b=Math.max(0,Math.min(255,Math.round(b*f)));
  return 'rgb('+r+','+g+','+b+')';
}

/* Face painter: expressive mouths, zero bird traits. */
function drawFace(ctx, S, kind){
  var cx=S/2, cy=S/2, u=S/100;
  ctx.lineCap='round';
  function eye(x, y, r, style){
    ctx.fillStyle='#1a0a05';
    ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(x, y, r, 0, Math.PI*2); ctx.fill();
    ctx.fillStyle='#ff2e1a';
    ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(x, y, r*0.45, 0, Math.PI*2); ctx.fill();
    ctx.fillStyle='rgba(255,255,255,.85)';
    ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(x-r*0.15, y-r*0.2, r*0.18, 0, Math.PI*2); ctx.fill();
    if(style==='brow'){
      ctx.strokeStyle='#1a0a05'; ctx.lineWidth=2.4*u;
      ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(x-1.6*r, y-1.7*r); ctx.lineTo(x+1.5*r, y-0.9*r); ctx.stroke();
    } else if(style==='heavy'){
      ctx.strokeStyle='rgba(26,10,5,.9)'; ctx.lineWidth=2.2*u;
      ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(x-1.5*r, y-1.1*r); ctx.lineTo(x+1.5*r, y-1.1*r); ctx.stroke();
    } else if(style==='sharp'){
      ctx.strokeStyle='#1a0a05'; ctx.lineWidth=2*u;
      ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(x-1.7*r, y-0.6*r); ctx.lineTo(x+1.7*r, y-0.6*r); ctx.stroke();
    }
  }
  function mouth(style){
    ctx.strokeStyle='#1a0a05'; ctx.lineWidth=2.6*u;
    ctx.beginPath();
    if(style==='smirk'){ ctx.arc(cx+4*u, cy+11*u, 9*u, 0.15*Math.PI, 0.8*Math.PI); }
    else if(style==='grin'){ ctx.arc(cx, cy+9*u, 10*u, 0.1*Math.PI, 0.9*Math.PI); }
    else if(style==='open'){ ctx.fillStyle='#5a1408'; ctx.beginPath(); ctx.ellipse(cx, cy+12*u, 6*u, 7.5*u, 0, 0, Math.PI*2); ctx.fill();
      ctx.fillStyle='#ff6a4a'; ctx.beginPath(); ctx.ellipse(cx, cy+15*u, 3.4*u, 3.4*u, 0, 0, Math.PI*2); ctx.fill(); return; }
    else if(style==='jag'){
      ctx.beginPath(); var x0=cx-10*u;
      ctx.moveTo(x0, cy+12*u);
      for(var i=0;i<5;i++){ ctx.lineTo(x0+(i*4+2)*u, cy+(i%2? 8:14)*u); }
      ctx.lineTo(x0+20*u, cy+12*u); }
    else { ctx.arc(cx, cy+10*u, 8*u, 0.2*Math.PI, 0.8*Math.PI); }
    ctx.stroke();
  }
  var er=7*u, ey=cy-6*u, ex=13*u;
  if(kind==='cocky'){ eye(cx-ex,ey,er,'brow'); eye(cx+ex,ey,er,'brow'); mouth('smirk'); }
  else if(kind==='calm'){ eye(cx-ex,ey,er,'heavy'); eye(cx+ex,ey,er,'heavy'); mouth('smile'); }
  else if(kind==='sharp'){ eye(cx-ex,ey,er,'sharp'); eye(cx+ex,ey,er,'sharp'); mouth('grin'); }
  else if(kind==='fierce'){ eye(cx-ex,ey,er,'brow'); eye(cx+ex,ey,er,'brow'); mouth('open'); }
  else if(kind==='eager'){ eye(cx-ex,ey,er*1.15); eye(cx+ex,ey,er*1.15); mouth('open'); }
  else if(kind==='villain'){ eye(cx-ex,ey,er,'brow'); eye(cx+ex,ey,er,'brow'); mouth('jag'); }
  else if(kind==='happy'){
    ctx.strokeStyle='#1a0a05'; ctx.lineWidth=2.6*u;
    ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(cx-ex, ey+3*u, er*0.95, Math.PI*1.12, Math.PI*1.88); ctx.stroke();
    ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(cx+ex, ey+3*u, er*0.95, Math.PI*1.12, Math.PI*1.88); ctx.stroke();
    mouth('grin');
  }
  else if(kind==='cool'){
    ctx.fillStyle='#16161f';
    ctx.beginPath(); ctx.ellipse(cx-ex, ey, er*1.3, er, 0, 0, Math.PI*2); ctx.fill();
    ctx.beginPath(); ctx.ellipse(cx+ex, ey, er*1.3, er, 0, 0, Math.PI*2); ctx.fill();
    ctx.strokeStyle='#16161f'; ctx.lineWidth=1.8*u;
    ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(cx-ex+er*1.15, ey-er*0.2); ctx.lineTo(cx+ex-er*1.15, ey-er*0.2); ctx.stroke();
    ctx.fillStyle='rgba(255,255,255,.4)';
    ctx.beginPath(); ctx.ellipse(cx-ex-er*0.35, ey-er*0.35, er*0.42, er*0.2, -0.5, 0, Math.PI*2); ctx.fill();
    ctx.beginPath(); ctx.ellipse(cx+ex-er*0.35, ey-er*0.35, er*0.42, er*0.2, -0.5, 0, Math.PI*2); ctx.fill();
    mouth('smirk');
  }
  else if(kind==='angry'){
    eye(cx-ex,ey,er,'brow'); eye(cx+ex,ey,er,'brow');
    ctx.strokeStyle='#1a0a05'; ctx.lineWidth=2.6*u;
    ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(cx, cy+19*u, 8*u, Math.PI*1.2, Math.PI*1.8); ctx.stroke();
  }
  else if(kind==='sleepy'){
    ctx.strokeStyle='#1a0a05'; ctx.lineWidth=2.2*u;
    ctx.fillStyle='#1a0a05';
    [-1,1].forEach(function(s){
      var exx=cx+s*ex;
      ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(exx-er*1.2, ey+er*0.5); ctx.lineTo(exx+er*1.2, ey+er*0.5); ctx.stroke();
      ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(exx, ey+er*0.65, er*0.32, 0, Math.PI*2); ctx.fill();
    });
    ctx.fillStyle='#5a1408';
    ctx.beginPath(); ctx.ellipse(cx, cy+12*u, 3.5*u, 4.5*u, 0, 0, Math.PI*2); ctx.fill();
  }
  else if(kind==='star'){
    [-1,1].forEach(function(s){
      var exx=cx+s*ex;
      ctx.fillStyle='#fff';
      ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(exx, ey, er*1.05, 0, Math.PI*2); ctx.fill();
      ctx.fillStyle='#ffd76a';
      ctx.beginPath();
      for(var i=0;i<10;i++){
        var rr=(i%2===0)?er*0.72:er*0.3, aa=-Math.PI/2+i*Math.PI/5;
        var px=exx+Math.cos(aa)*rr, py=ey+Math.sin(aa)*rr;
        if(i===0) ctx.moveTo(px,py); else ctx.lineTo(px,py);
      }
      ctx.closePath(); ctx.fill();
    });
    mouth('open');
  }
  else { eye(cx-ex,ey,er); eye(cx+ex,ey,er); mouth('smile'); }
}

/* Body texture: bright core -> body color -> dark edge, with face. */
function bodyTexture(spec, faceKind){
  var S=256, c=cnv(S,S), x=c.getContext('2d');
  var g=x.createRadialGradient(S/2,S*0.38,S*0.06, S/2,S/2,S*0.52);
  g.addColorStop(0, spec.core); g.addColorStop(0.45, spec.body); g.addColorStop(1, shade(spec.dark,0.85));
  x.fillStyle=g; x.fillRect(0,0,S,S);
  x.globalAlpha=0.16;
  for(var i=0;i<26;i++){
    x.fillStyle = i%2 ? spec.core : spec.dark;
    x.beginPath();
    x.arc(Math.random()*S, Math.random()*S, 6+Math.random()*16, 0, Math.PI*2); x.fill();
  }
  x.globalAlpha=1;
  drawFace(x, S, faceKind);
  return tex(c);
}

/* ---------------- shared FX textures ---------------- */
function flameTexture(){
  var S=128, c=cnv(S,S), x=c.getContext('2d');
  var g=x.createLinearGradient(0,S,0,0);
  g.addColorStop(0,'rgba(255,120,20,0)'); g.addColorStop(0.35,'rgba(255,140,30,0.55)');
  g.addColorStop(0.75,'rgba(255,200,90,0.9)'); g.addColorStop(1,'rgba(255,246,200,0)');
  x.fillStyle=g;
  x.beginPath(); x.moveTo(S*0.5,0);
  x.bezierCurveTo(S*0.95,S*0.35, S*0.8,S*0.7, S*0.5,S);
  x.bezierCurveTo(S*0.2,S*0.7, S*0.05,S*0.35, S*0.5,0);
  x.fill();
  return tex(c);
}
function glowTexture(){
  var S=128, c=cnv(S,S), x=c.getContext('2d');
  var g=x.createRadialGradient(S/2,S/2,2, S/2,S/2,S/2);
  g.addColorStop(0,'rgba(255,205,105,0.9)'); g.addColorStop(0.32,'rgba(255,140,45,0.42)');
  g.addColorStop(0.68,'rgba(255,95,25,0.14)'); g.addColorStop(1,'rgba(255,90,20,0)');
  x.fillStyle=g; x.fillRect(0,0,S,S);
  return tex(c);
}
var FLAME_TEX=null, GLOW_TEX=null;

/* ---------------- fire baby factory ---------------- */
function makeBaby(spec, faceKind, scale){
  scale = scale||1;
  var g = new THREE.Group();
  var R = 1.0*scale;

  var bodyMat = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ map: bodyTexture(spec, faceKind), roughness:0.28, metalness:0.0 });
  var body = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.SphereGeometry(R, 22, 16), bodyMat);
  body.rotation.y = -Math.PI/2;
  body.castShadow=true;
  g.add(body);

  var glow = new THREE.Sprite(new THREE.SpriteMaterial({
    map:GLOW_TEX, blending:THREE.AdditiveBlending, depthWrite:false, transparent:true, opacity:0.85 }));
  glow.scale.set(R*4.5, R*4.5, 1);
  g.add(glow);

  var licks=[];
  var lickGeo = new THREE.ConeGeometry(R*0.34, R*1.15, 7);
  var lm = new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({ map:FLAME_TEX, transparent:true,
    blending:THREE.AdditiveBlending, depthWrite:false, side:THREE.DoubleSide, opacity:0.95 });
  for(var i=0;i<7;i++){
    var cone = new THREE.Mesh(lickGeo, lm);
    var a = (i/7)*Math.PI*2 + Math.random()*0.5;
    cone.position.set(Math.cos(a)*R*0.72, Math.sin(a)*R*0.72, R*0.35);
    cone.rotation.z = a - Math.PI/2;
    cone.rotation.x = (Math.random()-0.5)*0.6;
    cone.userData.phase = Math.random()*Math.PI*2;
    cone.userData.base = 0.85+Math.random()*0.3;
    g.add(cone); licks.push(cone);
  }

  var shC=cnv(64,64), shX=shC.getContext('2d');
  var sg=shX.createRadialGradient(32,32,2,32,32,30);
  sg.addColorStop(0,'rgba(0,0,0,0.42)'); sg.addColorStop(1,'rgba(0,0,0,0)');
  shX.fillStyle=sg; shX.fillRect(0,0,64,64);
  var blob = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.PlaneGeometry(R*2.6,R*2.6),
    new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({ map:tex(shC), transparent:true, depthWrite:false }));
  blob.rotation.x=-Math.PI/2; blob.position.y=-R*1.52;
  g.add(blob);

  var dark = new THREE.MeshLambertMaterial({ color:new THREE.Color(spec.dark) });
  if(spec.extra==='headband'){
    var band = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.TorusGeometry(R*1.0, R*0.10, 8, 24), dark);
    band.rotation.x = Math.PI/2 - 0.35; band.position.y = R*0.42;
    g.add(band);
    var knot = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(R*0.16,R*0.34,R*0.06), dark);
    knot.position.set(-R*0.95, R*0.5, -R*0.25); knot.rotation.z=0.5;
    g.add(knot);
  } else if(spec.extra==='crown'){
    var gold = new THREE.MeshLambertMaterial({ color:0xffd76a, emissive:0x664400 });
    var circ = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.CylinderGeometry(R*0.52,R*0.58,R*0.30,10), gold);
    circ.position.y = R*1.02; g.add(circ);
    for(var s=0;s<5;s++){
      var spike = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.ConeGeometry(R*0.11,R*0.34,6), gold);
      var sa=(s/5)*Math.PI*2;
      spike.position.set(Math.cos(sa)*R*0.48, R*1.28, Math.sin(sa)*R*0.48);
      g.add(spike);
    }
  } else if(spec.extra==='buns'){
    var bunMat = new THREE.MeshLambertMaterial({ map:bodyTexture(
      {body:spec.body, dark:spec.dark, core:spec.core}, 'none') });
    [-1,1].forEach(function(sgn){
      var bun = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.SphereGeometry(R*0.34,12,10), bunMat);
      bun.position.set(sgn*R*0.62, R*0.88, 0);
      g.add(bun);
    });
  } else if(spec.extra==='bow'){
    var wood = new THREE.MeshLambertMaterial({ color:0x8a5a2e });
    var arc = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.TorusGeometry(R*0.85, R*0.07, 8, 20, Math.PI*1.2), wood);
    arc.position.set(R*1.25, R*0.1, 0); arc.rotation.z = -Math.PI*0.1; arc.rotation.y = 0.5;
    g.add(arc);
    var strMat = new THREE.LineBasicMaterial({ color:0xfff2cc });
    var p1 = new THREE.Vector3(R*1.25 + Math.cos(-Math.PI*0.1+Math.PI*0.6)*R*0.85, R*0.1 + Math.sin(-Math.PI*0.1+Math.PI*0.6)*R*0.85, 0);
    var p2 = new THREE.Vector3(R*1.25 + Math.cos(-Math.PI*0.1-Math.PI*0.6)*R*0.85, R*0.1 + Math.sin(-Math.PI*0.1-Math.PI*0.6)*R*0.85, 0);
    g.add(new THREE.Line(new THREE.BufferGeometry().setFromPoints([p1,p2]), strMat));
  }

  g.userData = { licks:licks, R:R, body:body, glow:glow, blob:blob,
    bobPhase:Math.random()*Math.PI*2, flash:0, baseY:0 };
  addLimbs(g, R, spec.body, spec.dark);
  return g;
}
function updateBaby(g, t, dt){
  var u=g.userData;
  g.position.y = u.baseY + Math.sin(t*2.2+u.bobPhase)*0.16;
  g.rotation.y += dt*0.0; // facing handled by callers
  for(var i=0;i<u.licks.length;i++){
    var L=u.licks[i], s=L.userData.base*(0.85+0.3*Math.sin(t*9+L.userData.phase));
    var hx=L.userData.hsx||1, hy=L.userData.hsy||1, hz=L.userData.hsz||1;
    L.scale.set(s*hx, s*(1+0.25*Math.sin(t*11+L.userData.phase*2))*hy, s*hz);
  }
  u.glow.material.opacity = 0.78+0.2*Math.sin(t*7+u.bobPhase);
  if(u.flash>0){ u.flash-=dt*4; u.body.material.emissive.setRGB(Math.max(0,u.flash)*0.9, Math.max(0,u.flash)*0.35, Math.max(0,u.flash)*0.1); }
  // limb walk-swing from real displacement (chunky toy waddle)
  var lpx=(u.lastPX===undefined)?g.position.x:u.lastPX, lpz=(u.lastPZ===undefined)?g.position.z:u.lastPZ;
  var mdx=g.position.x-lpx, mdz=g.position.z-lpz;
  var moved=Math.sqrt(mdx*mdx+mdz*mdz);
  u.walkPhase=(u.walkPhase||0)+moved*2.6;
  u.lastPX=g.position.x; u.lastPZ=g.position.z;
  var swT=moved>0.0008?1:0;
  u.swingAmp=(u.swingAmp||0)+(swT-(u.swingAmp||0))*Math.min(1,dt*7);
  var sw=Math.sin(u.walkPhase)*0.6*(u.swingAmp||0);
  if(u.armL){ u.armL.rotation.x=sw; u.armR.rotation.x=-sw; u.legL.rotation.x=-sw; u.legR.rotation.x=sw; }
}

/* ---------------- arms & legs: chunky toy-like limbs, walk-swung ---------------- */
var LIMB_GEO_CACHE={}, LIMB_MAT_CACHE={};
function limbGeo(kind, R, len){
  var key=kind+'_'+R.toFixed(2);
  if(!LIMB_GEO_CACHE[key]){
    var rad=(kind==='arm'?0.18:0.22)*R;
    var g=new THREE.CapsuleGeometry(rad, len, 4, 10);
    g.translate(0, -len/2, 0); // pivot at the joint
    LIMB_GEO_CACHE[key]=g;
  }
  return LIMB_GEO_CACHE[key];
}
function limbMat(color){
  if(!LIMB_MAT_CACHE[color]) LIMB_MAT_CACHE[color]=new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({color:new THREE.Color(color), roughness:0.4, metalness:0.0});
  return LIMB_MAT_CACHE[color];
}
function addLimbs(g, R, bodyColor, darkColor){
  var armLen=R*0.70, legLen=R*0.73;
  var armL=new THREE.Mesh(limbGeo('arm',R,armLen), limbMat(bodyColor));
  armL.position.set(-R*1.02, R*0.25, 0); armL.rotation.z=-0.12; armL.castShadow=true; g.add(armL);
  var armR=new THREE.Mesh(limbGeo('arm',R,armLen), limbMat(bodyColor));
  armR.position.set(R*1.02, R*0.25, 0); armR.rotation.z=0.12; armR.castShadow=true; g.add(armR);
  var legL=new THREE.Mesh(limbGeo('leg',R,legLen), limbMat(darkColor));
  legL.position.set(-R*0.38, -R*0.60, 0); legL.castShadow=true; g.add(legL);
  var legR=new THREE.Mesh(limbGeo('leg',R,legLen), limbMat(darkColor));
  legR.position.set(R*0.38, -R*0.60, 0); legR.castShadow=true; g.add(legR);
  g.userData.armL=armL; g.userData.armR=armR; g.userData.legL=legL; g.userData.legR=legR;
}

/* ---------------- graphics glow-up: Fall Guys gloss world ---------------- */
var __embers=null, __volcGlow=null;
function buildSceneryFX(){
  // distant volcano with a glowing crater on the horizon
  var volc=new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.ConeGeometry(30,46,9),
    new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({color:0x3a2a28, roughness:0.9}));
  volc.position.set(6,14,-152); scene.add(volc);
  var craterC=cnv(128,128), craterX=craterC.getContext('2d');
  var cg=craterX.createRadialGradient(64,64,4,64,64,62);
  cg.addColorStop(0,'rgba(255,220,150,1)'); cg.addColorStop(0.4,'rgba(255,140,40,0.85)');
  cg.addColorStop(1,'rgba(255,80,20,0)');
  craterX.fillStyle=cg; craterX.fillRect(0,0,128,128);
  __volcGlow=new THREE.Sprite(new THREE.SpriteMaterial({map:tex(craterC),blending:THREE.AdditiveBlending,depthWrite:false,transparent:true,fog:false}));
  __volcGlow.scale.set(46,26,1); __volcGlow.position.set(6,36,-150); scene.add(__volcGlow);

  // candy trees lining the path
  var trunkM=new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({color:0x8a5a3a, roughness:0.8});
  var leafCols=[0x37d67a, 0x2bb8a8, 0xff7ab8, 0xffd23e, 0x7a9bff];
  for(var i=0;i<10;i++){
    var tg=new THREE.Group();
    var trunk=new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.CylinderGeometry(0.35,0.5,2.2,8), trunkM.clone());
    trunk.position.y=1.1; trunk.castShadow=true; tg.add(trunk);
    var lm=new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({color:leafCols[i%leafCols.length], roughness:0.5});
    var c1=new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.ConeGeometry(2.0,3.0,10), lm);
    c1.position.y=3.4; c1.castShadow=true; tg.add(c1);
    var c2=new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.ConeGeometry(1.4,2.2,10), lm);
    c2.position.y=5.0; c2.castShadow=true; tg.add(c2);
    var side=i%2?1:-1;
    tg.position.set(side*(15+Math.random()*9), 0, 44-i*15+Math.random()*6);
    tg.rotation.y=Math.random()*3;
    scene.add(tg);
    FBF.register(tg.position.x,tg.position.z,{r:2.2,fuel:18,size:2.6,kind:'A',group:tg,mats:[trunk.material,lm],y:4.2});
  }

  // drifting ember motes everywhere
  var N=FBQ.emberCount, pos=new Float32Array(N*3), spd=new Float32Array(N);
  for(var e=0;e<N;e++){
    pos[e*3]=-30+Math.random()*60; pos[e*3+1]=Math.random()*14; pos[e*3+2]=-115+Math.random()*165;
    spd[e]=0.6+Math.random()*1.6;
  }
  var eg=new THREE.BufferGeometry();
  eg.setAttribute('position', new THREE.BufferAttribute(pos,3));
  var em=new THREE.PointsMaterial({size:0.5, map:GLOW_TEX, transparent:true,
    blending:THREE.AdditiveBlending, depthWrite:false, color:0xffb050, opacity:0.95});
  __embers=new THREE.Points(eg, em);
  __embers.userData={spd:spd, N:N};
  __embers.frustumCulled=false;
  scene.add(__embers);
}
function updateEmbers(dt){
  if(__volcGlow) __volcGlow.material.opacity=0.75+0.25*Math.sin(clock.elapsedTime*3.1);
  if(!__embers) return;
  var p=__embers.geometry.attributes.position, u=__embers.userData, t=clock.elapsedTime;
  for(var i=0;i<u.N;i++){
    var y=p.getY(i)+u.spd[i]*dt;
    if(y>15) y=0;
    p.setY(i,y);
    p.setX(i, p.getX(i)+Math.sin(t*1.3+i)*dt*0.7);
  }
  p.needsUpdate=true;
}

/* =====================================================================
   OPEN WORLD — one connected region, six biomes along the path (-Z).
   z bands: ember +52→+18 | grease +18→-2 | volt -2→-22 | slag -22→-42
            smoke -42→-62 | volcano -62→-118
   ===================================================================== */
var scene, camera, renderer, clock;
var torches=[], colliders=[], gates=[], triggers=[];
var stronghold=null, strongMats=[], strongFire=null;

/* biome ground: painted bands on one canvas, v=1 (canvas top) = far -Z */
var BIOMES = [
  { id:'ember',   z0:52,  z1:18,  base:'#9a7858', fog:0x47261a },
  { id:'grease',  z0:18,  z1:-2,  base:'#8a815f', fog:0x473a1e },
  { id:'volt',    z0:-2,  z1:-22, base:'#54689a', fog:0x1e3050 },
  { id:'slag',    z0:-22, z1:-42, base:'#9c703c', fog:0x472c18 },
  { id:'smoke',   z0:-42, z1:-62, base:'#6e6e7c', fog:0x2c2c36 },
  { id:'volcano', z0:-62, z1:-118,base:'#7e452c', fog:0x4d1810 }
];
function skyTexture(){
  var S=512, c=cnv(256,S), x=c.getContext('2d');
  var g=x.createLinearGradient(0,0,0,S);
  g.addColorStop(0,'#2f7fe0');
  g.addColorStop(0.34,'#8fd0ff');
  g.addColorStop(0.52,'#ffe3b3');
  g.addColorStop(0.58,'#ffc98a');
  g.addColorStop(0.64,'#ff9a52');
  g.addColorStop(0.74,'#c65a28');
  g.addColorStop(0.86,'#5a2818');
  g.addColorStop(1,'#2a120a');
  x.fillStyle=g; x.fillRect(0,0,256,S);
  // puffy candy clouds
  for(var i=0;i<16;i++){
    var cx=Math.random()*256, cy=40+Math.random()*200, cw=30+Math.random()*70;
    x.fillStyle='rgba(255,255,255,'+(0.35+Math.random()*0.35)+')';
    for(var p=0;p<5;p++){
      x.beginPath();
      x.ellipse(cx+(Math.random()-0.5)*cw, cy+(Math.random()-0.5)*18,
        cw*0.28*(0.7+Math.random()*0.6), 10+Math.random()*10, 0, 0, Math.PI*2);
      x.fill();
    }
  }
  // warm glow hugging the horizon
  var hg=x.createLinearGradient(0,S*0.5,0,S*0.8);
  hg.addColorStop(0,'rgba(255,170,80,0)');
  hg.addColorStop(1,'rgba(255,170,80,0.35)');
  x.fillStyle=hg; x.fillRect(0,S*0.5,256,S*0.3);
  // low sun glow pooled on the horizon (premium pass)
  var sg2=x.createRadialGradient(184,S*0.62,4, 184,S*0.62,120);
  sg2.addColorStop(0,'rgba(255,228,165,0.55)'); sg2.addColorStop(1,'rgba(255,228,165,0)');
  x.fillStyle=sg2; x.fillRect(64,Math.round(S*0.62)-120,240,240);
  return tex(c);
}
function worldGroundTexture(){
  var W=512, H=1024, c=cnv(W,H), x=c.getContext('2d');
  function zToY(z){ return (52-z)/170*H; }
  BIOMES.forEach(function(b){
    var y0=zToY(b.z0), y1=zToY(b.z1);
    x.fillStyle=b.base; x.fillRect(0,y0,W,y1-y0);
  });
  // speckle + scorch everywhere (denser + warm flecks for a richer ground read)
  for(var i=0;i<7000;i++){
    var v=Math.random();
    x.fillStyle = v<0.45 ? 'rgba(28,20,16,0.22)' : (v<0.72 ? 'rgba(130,110,90,0.16)' : (v<0.88 ? 'rgba(16,12,10,0.3)' : 'rgba(255,190,120,0.10)'));
    var s=1+Math.random()*4;
    x.fillRect(Math.random()*W, Math.random()*H, s, s);
  }
  // soft warm light pools in the ember village band (torchlight feel)
  var py0=zToY(52), py1=zToY(18);
  for(var p9=0;p9<10;p9++){
    var px2=Math.random()*W, py2=py0+Math.random()*(py1-py0), pr2=26+Math.random()*44;
    var pg2=x.createRadialGradient(px2,py2,2,px2,py2,pr2);
    pg2.addColorStop(0,'rgba(255,170,80,0.13)'); pg2.addColorStop(1,'rgba(255,170,80,0)');
    x.fillStyle=pg2; x.beginPath(); x.arc(px2,py2,pr2,0,Math.PI*2); x.fill();
  }
  // grease slicks (yellow blobs in grease band)
  var gy0=zToY(18), gy1=zToY(-2);
  for(var g2=0;g2<26;g2++){
    x.fillStyle='rgba(255,210,60,'+(0.18+Math.random()*0.25)+')';
    x.beginPath(); x.ellipse(Math.random()*W, gy0+Math.random()*(gy1-gy0),
      12+Math.random()*30, 8+Math.random()*18, Math.random()*3, 0, Math.PI*2); x.fill();
  }
  // ember cracks (orange lines in slag band)
  x.strokeStyle='rgba(255,106,30,0.75)'; x.lineWidth=3;
  var sy0=zToY(-22), sy1=zToY(-42);
  for(var e2=0;e2<16;e2++){
    x.beginPath();
    var ex=Math.random()*W, ey=sy0+Math.random()*(sy1-sy0);
    x.moveTo(ex,ey);
    for(var s3=0;s3<4;s3++){ ex+=(Math.random()-0.5)*70; ey+=(Math.random()-0.5)*30; x.lineTo(ex,ey); }
    x.stroke();
  }
  // lava glow patches (volcano band)
  var vy0=zToY(-62), vy1=zToY(-118);
  for(var l2=0;l2<18;l2++){
    var lx=Math.random()*W, ly=vy0+Math.random()*(vy1-vy0), lr=14+Math.random()*36;
    var lg=x.createRadialGradient(lx,ly,2,lx,ly,lr);
    lg.addColorStop(0,'rgba(255,110,30,0.5)'); lg.addColorStop(1,'rgba(255,110,30,0)');
    x.fillStyle=lg; x.beginPath(); x.arc(lx,ly,lr,0,Math.PI*2); x.fill();
  }
  // center path: faint warm dashes down the middle
  x.strokeStyle='rgba(255,180,90,0.16)'; x.lineWidth=8; x.setLineDash([30,26]);
  x.beginPath(); x.moveTo(W/2,0); x.lineTo(W/2,H); x.stroke();
  var t=tex(c); t.anisotropy=4;
  return t;
}

function addCollider(x,z,r){ colliders.push({x:x,z:z,r:r}); }

function prop(geo, mat, x, y, z, ry, colliderR){
  var m=new THREE.Mesh(geo, mat);
  m.position.set(x,y,z);
  m.castShadow=true;
  if(ry) m.rotation.y=ry;
  scene.add(m);
  if(colliderR) addCollider(x,z,colliderR);
  return m;
}

/* =====================================================================
   FBF — Fire Babys Fire: flammable world props, ignition, wind spread,
   burning damage, and hero-tool extinguish / flare-up (fire-safety tie-in).
   LOCAL visual-only system. Does not touch netcode or auth.
   ===================================================================== */
var FBF={
  list:[],
  wind:{x:1,z:0,speed:1},
  windT:12, windArrow:'\u2192 E', windDirty:true, lastFlareToast:0,
  /* register a flammable prop. opts: r, fuel, size, kind('A'|'B'), group,
     mats, y, torch, burning, flames, spreadMult */
  register:function(x,z,opts){
    if(this.list.length>=60) return null;
    var o=opts||{};
    var it={
      x:x, z:z,
      r:o.r||2, fuel:(o.fuel===Infinity?Infinity:(o.fuel||20)),
      maxFuel:(o.fuel===Infinity?Infinity:(o.fuel||20)),
      size:o.size||2, kind:o.kind||'A',
      group:o.group||null, mats:o.mats||[], y:o.y||2,
      spreadMult:o.spreadMult||1,
      torch:!!o.torch,
      burning:false, saved:false, burned:false,
      flames:[], embT:Math.random()*0.15, spreadT:0.3+Math.random()*0.4,
      dmgT:Math.random()*0.5, spreadBoostT:0, reigniteT:0
    };
    if(!it.group){ it.group=new THREE.Group(); it.group.position.set(x,0,z); scene.add(it.group); }
    if(o.flames&&o.flames.length){
      it.flames=o.flames;
      for(var f=0;f<it.flames.length;f++){ it.flames[f].userData.bx=0; it.flames[f].userData.bz=0; }
    }
    if(o.burning){ it.burning=true; if(it.flames.length===0) this.attachFlames(it); }
    this.list.push(it);
    return it;
  },
  attachFlames:function(it){
    if(typeof FLAME_TEX==='undefined'||!FLAME_TEX||!GLOW_TEX) return;
    var g=it.group, s=it.size;
    var fl=new THREE.Sprite(new THREE.SpriteMaterial({map:FLAME_TEX,blending:THREE.AdditiveBlending,depthWrite:false,transparent:true}));
    fl.scale.set(s*0.9,s*1.3,1); fl.position.y=it.y;
    fl.userData.bx=0; fl.userData.bz=0; g.add(fl);
    var gl=new THREE.Sprite(new THREE.SpriteMaterial({map:GLOW_TEX,blending:THREE.AdditiveBlending,depthWrite:false,transparent:true,opacity:0.6}));
    gl.scale.set(s*2.4,s*2.4,1); gl.position.y=it.y;
    gl.userData.bx=0; gl.userData.bz=0; g.add(gl);
    it.flames=[fl,gl];
  },
  /* ignite every unburned flammable within radius of (x,z) */
  ignite:function(x,z,radius){
    for(var i=0;i<this.list.length;i++){
      var it=this.list[i];
      if(it.burning||it.saved||it.burned) continue;
      var dx=it.x-x, dz=it.z-z;
      if(dx*dx+dz*dz<=radius*radius) this.igniteItem(it);
    }
  },
  igniteItem:function(it){
    if(it.burning||it.saved||it.burned) return;
    it.burning=true;
    if(it.flames.length===0) this.attachFlames(it);
    else for(var i=0;i<it.flames.length;i++) it.flames[i].visible=true;
    it.spreadT=0.3+Math.random()*0.4;
    if(typeof spawnBurst==='function')
      spawnBurst(new THREE.Vector3(it.x,it.y+0.5,it.z),0xffa040,6,4);
  },
  charItem:function(it,prog){
    if(prog<0) prog=0; if(prog>1) prog=1;
    for(var i=0;i<it.mats.length;i++){
      var m=it.mats[i];
      if(!m||!m.color) continue;
      if(m.userData._fbfOrig===undefined) m.userData._fbfOrig=m.color.getHex();
      var o=m.userData._fbfOrig;
      var or=(o>>16)&255, og=(o>>8)&255, ob=o&255;
      m.color.setRGB((or+(0x1a-or)*prog)/255,(og+(0x12-og)*prog)/255,(ob+(0x10-ob)*prog)/255);
    }
  },
  burnout:function(it){
    it.burning=false; it.burned=true;
    for(var i=0;i<it.flames.length;i++) it.flames[i].visible=false;
    this.charItem(it,1);
  },
  extinguish:function(it,byPlayer){
    if(!it.burning) return;
    it.burning=false; it.saved=true;
    for(var i=0;i<it.flames.length;i++) it.flames[i].visible=false;
    if(it.torch) it.reigniteT=20; // decorative torches relight themselves
    if(byPlayer){
      if(typeof showToast==='function') showToast('\uD83E\uDDEF EXTINGUISHED! +50',1400);
      if(typeof beep==='function') beep(880,0.15,'sine',0.08);
      try{
        if(window.FBMultiplayer&&typeof window.FBMultiplayer.sendExtinguish==='function')
          window.FBMultiplayer.sendExtinguish(it.x,it.z);
      }catch(e){}
    }
  },
  reignite:function(it){
    it.saved=false; it.burning=true; it.reigniteT=0;
    for(var i=0;i<it.flames.length;i++) it.flames[i].visible=true;
  },
  /* hero attack near fire: right tool extinguishes, wrong tool flares up */
  combatHit:function(pos,src){
    var heroId=src&&src.spec?src.spec.id:null;
    if(!heroId||typeof HERO_COUNTER==='undefined') return;
    var counter=HERO_COUNTER[heroId], weak=HERO_WEAK[heroId];
    for(var i=0;i<this.list.length;i++){
      var it=this.list[i];
      if(!it.burning) continue;
      var dx=it.x-pos.x, dz=it.z-pos.z;
      if(dx*dx+dz*dz>36) continue; // within 6
      if(counter&&counter===it.kind){
        this.extinguish(it,true);
      }else if(weak&&weak===it.kind){
        it.spreadBoostT=6; // doubles spread radius + chance for 6s
        var now=(typeof performance!=='undefined')?performance.now():0;
        if(now-this.lastFlareToast>1500){
          this.lastFlareToast=now;
          if(typeof showToast==='function') showToast('\uD83D\uDD25 FLARE-UP! Wrong tool!',1400);
          if(typeof beep==='function') beep(140,0.25,'sawtooth',0.09);
        }
      }
      /* blaze (no counter/weak): does nothing to fires */
    }
  },
  newWind:function(){
    var a=Math.random()*Math.PI*2;
    this.wind.x=Math.cos(a); this.wind.z=Math.sin(a);
    this.wind.speed=0.5+Math.random()*1.0;
    var dirs=['\u2192 E','\u2198 SE','\u2193 S','\u2199 SW','\u2190 W','\u2196 NW','\u2191 N','\u2197 NE'];
    this.windArrow=dirs[Math.round(a/(Math.PI/4))%8];
    this.windT=25+Math.random()*20;
    this.windDirty=true;
  },
  updateWindPill:function(){
    if(!this.windDirty) return;
    this.windDirty=false;
    var el=document.getElementById('windpill');
    if(el) el.textContent='\uD83D\uDCA8 WIND '+this.windArrow;
  },
  spreadFrom:function(src){
    var boost=src.spreadBoostT>0?2:1;
    var baseR=7*src.spreadMult*boost;
    for(var i=0;i<this.list.length;i++){
      var it=this.list[i];
      if(it.burning||it.saved||it.burned||it===src) continue;
      var dx=it.x-src.x, dz=it.z-src.z;
      var d2=dx*dx+dz*dz;
      if(d2>baseR*baseR||d2<0.01) continue;
      var d=Math.sqrt(d2);
      var align=(dx/d)*this.wind.x+(dz/d)*this.wind.z; // -1..1, downwind>0
      var chance=(0.35+0.4*align)*0.5*boost;
      if(Math.random()<chance) this.igniteItem(it);
    }
    if(src.spreadBoostT>0) src.spreadBoostT-=0.5;
  },
  burnFoes:function(it){
    if(typeof units==='undefined') return;
    var rr=(it.r+3)*(it.r+3);
    for(var i=0;i<units.length;i++){
      var u=units[i];
      if(!u||u.side!=='foe'||u.dead||!u.group) continue;
      var dx=u.group.position.x-it.x, dz=u.group.position.z-it.z;
      if(dx*dx+dz*dz>rr) continue;
      u.hp-=3;
      u.group.userData.flash=1;
      if(u.hpbar&&u.maxHp) u.hpbar.set(Math.max(0,u.hp/u.maxHp),true);
      if(typeof dmgNumber==='function')
        dmgNumber(u.group.position.clone().add(new THREE.Vector3(0,2,0)),3,'#ff6a5a');
      if(u.hp<=0&&!u.dead){
        u.dead=true; u.state='dying'; u.deathT=0;
        if(typeof fbExtinguishPoof==='function') fbExtinguishPoof(u.group);
        if(typeof spawnBurst==='function')
          spawnBurst(u.group.position.clone().add(new THREE.Vector3(0,1,0)),0xff8c2e,16,6);
        if(typeof updateFoeCount==='function') updateFoeCount();
      }
    }
  },
  update:function(dt){
    this.windT-=dt;
    if(this.windT<=0) this.newWind();
    for(var i=0;i<this.list.length;i++){
      var it=this.list[i];
      if(it.burning&&!it.saved){
        if(it.fuel!==Infinity) it.fuel-=dt;
        it.embT-=dt;
        if(it.embT<=0){
          it.embT=0.15;
          if(typeof spawnBurst==='function')
            spawnBurst(new THREE.Vector3(it.x,it.y+1,it.z),0xff8c2e,2,3);
        }
        var prog=(it.fuel===Infinity)?0:(1-it.fuel/it.maxFuel);
        this.charItem(it,prog);
        /* wind tilts the flames */
        for(var f=0;f<it.flames.length;f++){
          var flm=it.flames[f];
          flm.position.x=(flm.userData.bx||0)+this.wind.x*0.5*this.wind.speed;
          flm.position.z=(flm.userData.bz||0)+this.wind.z*0.5*this.wind.speed;
        }
        it.spreadT-=dt;
        if(it.spreadT<=0){ it.spreadT=0.5; this.spreadFrom(it); }
        it.dmgT-=dt;
        if(it.dmgT<=0){ it.dmgT=0.5; this.burnFoes(it); }
        if(it.fuel<=0) this.burnout(it);
      }else if(it.reigniteT>0){
        it.reigniteT-=dt;
        if(it.reigniteT<=0) this.reignite(it);
      }
    }
    this.updateWindPill();
  }
};

function buildOpenWorld(){
  // sky + sun
  var sky=new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.SphereGeometry(160,16,12),
    new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({ map:skyTexture(), side:THREE.BackSide, fog:false, toneMapped:false }));
  scene.add(sky);
  var sunC=cnv(128,128), sunX=sunC.getContext('2d');
  var sg=sunX.createRadialGradient(64,64,4,64,64,62);
  sg.addColorStop(0,'rgba(255,230,170,1)'); sg.addColorStop(0.35,'rgba(255,160,60,0.9)');
  sg.addColorStop(1,'rgba(255,120,30,0)');
  sunX.fillStyle=sg; sunX.fillRect(0,0,128,128);
  var sun=new THREE.Sprite(new THREE.SpriteMaterial({map:tex(sunC),blending:THREE.AdditiveBlending,depthWrite:false,fog:false,transparent:true}));
  sun.scale.set(44,44,1); sun.position.set(-50,34,-130); scene.add(sun);

  // ground: one big plane, biome bands painted on
  var ground=new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.PlaneGeometry(64,170),
    new THREE.MeshLambertMaterial({ map:worldGroundTexture() }));
  ground.rotation.x=-Math.PI/2; ground.position.set(0,0,-33);
  ground.receiveShadow=true;
  scene.add(ground);

  // side cliffs so the world feels enclosed (dark silhouettes)
  var cliffMat=new THREE.MeshLambertMaterial({ color:0x1e120a });
  var cliffGeo=new THREE.ConeGeometry(10,16,6);
  for(var i=0;i<22;i++){
    var side=i%2?1:-1;
    var h=new THREE.Mesh(cliffGeo, cliffMat);
    h.position.set(side*(34+Math.random()*10), 2, 46-i*8+Math.random()*4);
    h.rotation.y=Math.random()*3;
    scene.add(h);
  }

  var wood=new THREE.MeshLambertMaterial({color:0x7a5a36});
  var darkWood=new THREE.MeshLambertMaterial({color:0x4a3018});
  var stoneM=new THREE.MeshLambertMaterial({color:0x5a5a66});
  var rustM=new THREE.MeshLambertMaterial({color:0x6e4a30});

  /* ---- EMBER VILLAGE (z 18..52): huts, torches, dummies, fences ---- */
  function hut(px,pz){
    var grp=new THREE.Group();
    var walls=new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(3.2,2.2,2.8), new THREE.MeshLambertMaterial({color:0x8a6a48}));
    walls.position.y=1.1; grp.add(walls);
    var roof=new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.ConeGeometry(2.9,1.7,4), darkWood.clone());
    roof.position.y=3.0; roof.rotation.y=Math.PI/4; grp.add(roof);
    grp.position.set(px,0,pz); grp.rotation.y=(Math.random()-0.5)*0.6;
    scene.add(grp); addCollider(px,pz,2.6);
    FBF.register(px,pz,{r:2.8,fuel:30,size:4,kind:'A',group:grp,mats:[walls.material,roof.material],y:3.4});
  }
  hut(-13,32); hut(13,28); hut(-12,22);
  function torch(px,pz){
    var grp=new THREE.Group();
    var pole=new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.CylinderGeometry(0.12,0.16,3.2,8), darkWood);
    pole.position.y=1.6; grp.add(pole);
    var fl=new THREE.Sprite(new THREE.SpriteMaterial({ map:FLAME_TEX, blending:THREE.AdditiveBlending, depthWrite:false, transparent:true }));
    fl.scale.set(1.5,2.2,1); fl.position.y=4.2; grp.add(fl);
    var gl=new THREE.Sprite(new THREE.SpriteMaterial({ map:GLOW_TEX, blending:THREE.AdditiveBlending, depthWrite:false, transparent:true, opacity:0.7 }));
    gl.scale.set(5,5,1); gl.position.y=4.2; grp.add(gl);
    grp.position.set(px,0,pz); scene.add(grp);
    torches.push({fl:fl, gl:gl, phase:Math.random()*9});
    FBF.register(px,pz,{r:1.5,fuel:Infinity,kind:'A',group:grp,mats:[],y:4.2,torch:true,burning:true,flames:[fl,gl]});
  }
  torch(-9,38); torch(9,38); torch(-9,24); torch(9,24); torch(-9,-64); torch(9,-64);
  function dummy(px,pz){
    var grp=new THREE.Group();
    var post=new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.CylinderGeometry(0.22,0.26,3.4,8),wood.clone());
    post.position.y=1.7; grp.add(post);
    var bar=new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.CylinderGeometry(0.14,0.14,2.2,8),wood.clone());
    bar.rotation.z=Math.PI/2; bar.position.y=2.6; grp.add(bar);
    grp.position.set(px,0,pz); scene.add(grp); addCollider(px,pz,0.8);
    FBF.register(px,pz,{r:1.2,fuel:12,size:2,kind:'A',group:grp,mats:[post.material,bar.material],y:2.8});
  }
  dummy(-6,34); dummy(6,33); dummy(0,27);
  // fences flanking the path
  var fenceM=new THREE.MeshLambertMaterial({color:0x5e4226});
  [-9.5,9.5].forEach(function(fx){
    for(var fz=42;fz>=20;fz-=3){
      var fpm=prop(new THREE.BoxGeometry(0.28,1.4,0.28), fenceM.clone(), fx, 0.7, fz);
      var fbfg=new THREE.Group(); fbfg.position.set(fx,0,fz); scene.add(fbfg);
      FBF.register(fx,fz,{r:1.0,fuel:8,size:1.6,kind:'A',group:fbfg,mats:[fpm.material],y:1.5});
    }
    var rail=new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(0.14,0.14,22), fenceM);
    rail.position.set(fx,1.05,31); scene.add(rail);
  });

  /* ---- GREASEWORKS (z -2..18): factories, chimneys, slicks, pipes ---- */
  function factory(px,pz,w){
    var f=prop(new THREE.BoxGeometry(w,3.4,3), stoneM, px, 1.7, pz, 0.2, w*0.7);
    prop(new THREE.CylinderGeometry(0.5,0.6,5,8), new THREE.MeshLambertMaterial({color:0x44444e}), px+w*0.3, 4.2, pz-1, 0, 0.9);
    return f;
  }
  factory(-14,10,4); factory(14,6,5); factory(-13,-1,3.4);
  // yellow oil slicks (flat, glossy)
  var slickM=new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({color:0xffd23c, transparent:true, opacity:0.42});
  [[-4,12,2.2],[5,8,1.6],[-6,2,2.6],[3,-1,1.4]].forEach(function(s){
    var sm=slickM.clone();
    var m=new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.CircleGeometry(s[2],16), sm);
    m.rotation.x=-Math.PI/2; m.position.set(s[0],0.03,s[1]); scene.add(m);
    var sgp=new THREE.Group(); sgp.position.set(s[0],0,s[1]); scene.add(sgp);
    FBF.register(s[0],s[1],{r:s[2]+0.8,fuel:25,size:2.2,kind:'B',group:sgp,mats:[sm],y:0.7,spreadMult:1.6});
  });
  // pipes along the ground
  var pipeM=new THREE.MeshLambertMaterial({color:0x707078});
  prop(new THREE.CylinderGeometry(0.4,0.4,20,8), pipeM, -11, 0.4, 8, 0).rotation.z=Math.PI/2;
  prop(new THREE.CylinderGeometry(0.4,0.4,20,8), pipeM, 11, 0.4, 4, 0).rotation.z=Math.PI/2;

  /* ---- VOLT TOWN (z -22..-2): tesla coils, neon ---- */
  window.__voltOrbs=[];
  [[-11,-6],[11,-8],[-10,-16],[10,-18]].forEach(function(p,k){
    prop(new THREE.CylinderGeometry(0.5,0.7,3.6,8),
      new THREE.MeshLambertMaterial({color:0x2a3444, emissive:0x0a2a55}), p[0], 1.8, p[1], 0, 1.1);
    var orb=new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.SphereGeometry(0.7,12,10),
      new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({color:0x3ca9ff}));
    orb.position.set(p[0],4.1,p[1]); scene.add(orb);
    window.__voltOrbs.push({m:orb, ph:k*1.7});
    // arc: jagged line from orb to ground, flickers in loop
    var pts=[]; for(var a2=0;a2<6;a2++) pts.push(new THREE.Vector3(p[0]+(Math.random()-0.5)*1.2, 3.8-a2*0.7, p[1]+(Math.random()-0.5)*1.2));
    var arc=new THREE.Line(new THREE.BufferGeometry().setFromPoints(pts),
      new THREE.LineBasicMaterial({color:0x7dd7ff, transparent:true, opacity:0.8}));
    scene.add(arc);
    window.__voltOrbs.push({m:arc, ph:k*2.3, arc:true, bx:p[0], bz:p[1]});
  });
  // neon sign plane
  var neonC=cnv(256,96), nx=neonC.getContext('2d');
  nx.font='900 44px system-ui'; nx.textAlign='center'; nx.fillStyle='#3ca9ff';
  nx.shadowColor='#3ca9ff'; nx.shadowBlur=18; nx.fillText('VOLT TOWN',128,60);
  var neon=new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.PlaneGeometry(7,2.6),
    new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({map:tex(neonC), transparent:true}));
  neon.position.set(0,6.5,-12); scene.add(neon);

  /* ---- SLAGHEAP (z -42..-22): canyon walls, scrap, molten river ---- */
  var wallM=new THREE.MeshLambertMaterial({color:0x3a2c20});
  [[-15,-32],[15,-32]].forEach(function(p){
    var _wm = prop(new THREE.BoxGeometry(6,9,20), wallM, p[0], 4.5, p[1], 0.15, 4.5);
    try { if(_wm){ _wm.userData.slagWall = true; } } catch(e){}
  });
  for(var s2=0;s2<8;s2++){
    var sx=(Math.random()<0.5?-1:1)*(10+Math.random()*4), sz=-24-Math.random()*16;
    var scrap=prop(new THREE.BoxGeometry(1+Math.random(),0.8+Math.random()*0.8,1+Math.random()*0.6), rustM,
      sx, 0.5, sz, Math.random()*3, 1.1);
    scrap.rotation.x=(Math.random()-0.5)*0.3;
  }
  // molten river strip
  var river=new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.PlaneGeometry(3.2,20),
    new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({color:0xff6a1e}));
  river.rotation.x=-Math.PI/2; river.position.set(11.5,0.04,-32); scene.add(river);
  window.__river=river;

  /* ---- SMOKESTACK CITY (z -62..-42): towers, villain banners, smog ---- */
  var towerM=new THREE.MeshLambertMaterial({color:0x3c3c48});
  [[-12,-46,7],[-6,-52,10],[2,-47,6],[8,-54,9],[13,-48,7],[-13,-58,8]].forEach(function(tw){
    prop(new THREE.BoxGeometry(2.6,tw[2],2.6), towerM, tw[0], tw[2]/2, tw[1], 0, 2);
    var win=new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.PlaneGeometry(1.6,0.8),
      new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({color:0xffd76a}));
    win.position.set(tw[0], tw[2]*0.7, tw[1]+1.32); scene.add(win);
  });
  // villain banners
  ['TWIGGY','SLICK','BUZZ','SLAG'].forEach(function(vn,bi){
    var bc=cnv(128,192), bx=bc.getContext('2d');
    bx.fillStyle='#1c0a06'; bx.fillRect(0,0,128,192);
    bx.fillStyle='#ff5a1e'; bx.font='900 24px system-ui'; bx.textAlign='center';
    bx.fillText(vn,64,40);
    bx.fillStyle='#e8352c';
    bx.beginPath(); bx.moveTo(24,180); bx.lineTo(64,120); bx.lineTo(104,180); bx.closePath(); bx.fill();
    var ban=new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.PlaneGeometry(2.2,3.3),
      new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({map:tex(bc), side:THREE.DoubleSide}));
    var bxx=-8+bi*5.4;
    ban.position.set(bxx,4.4,-60); scene.add(ban);
    window.__banners=window.__banners||[]; window.__banners.push({m:ban, ph:bi*1.3});
  });
  // drifting smog sprites
  window.__smog=[];
  for(var sm=0;sm<14;sm++){
    var sc=cnv(64,64), sx2=sc.getContext('2d');
    var sgg=sx2.createRadialGradient(32,32,2,32,32,30);
    sgg.addColorStop(0,'rgba(120,120,130,0.35)'); sgg.addColorStop(1,'rgba(120,120,130,0)');
    sx2.fillStyle=sgg; sx2.fillRect(0,0,64,64);
    var sp=new THREE.Sprite(new THREE.SpriteMaterial({map:tex(sc), transparent:true, depthWrite:false, opacity:0.5}));
    sp.position.set((Math.random()-0.5)*40, 3+Math.random()*6, -44-Math.random()*16);
    var ssc=3+Math.random()*4; sp.scale.set(ssc,ssc,1);
    scene.add(sp);
    window.__smog.push({s:sp, vx:0.4+Math.random()*0.5});
  }

  /* ---- VOLCANO (z -118..-62): crater fortress, lava, arena ---- */
  var cone=new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.ConeGeometry(17,30,14,1,true),
    new THREE.MeshLambertMaterial({color:0x4a2620, side:THREE.DoubleSide}));
  cone.position.set(0,15,-104); scene.add(cone); addCollider(0,-104,13);
  var lava=new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.CircleGeometry(6,16),
    new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({color:0xff5a1e}));
  lava.rotation.x=-Math.PI/2; lava.position.set(0,29.4,-104); scene.add(lava);
  window.__lava=lava;
  // graduation arena: stone disc
  var arena=new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.CylinderGeometry(9,9.6,0.7,20),
    new THREE.MeshLambertMaterial({color:0x5a4a3c}));
  arena.position.set(0,0.35,-88); scene.add(arena);
  // lava glow sprites around crater
  for(var lg2=0;lg2<6;lg2++){
    var lgs=new THREE.Sprite(new THREE.SpriteMaterial({map:GLOW_TEX, color:0xff5a1e,
      blending:THREE.AdditiveBlending, depthWrite:false, transparent:true, opacity:0.6}));
    var la=lg2/6*Math.PI*2;
    lgs.position.set(Math.cos(la)*13, 4, -104+Math.sin(la)*13);
    lgs.scale.set(8,8,1); scene.add(lgs);
  }
  torch(-6,-80); torch(6,-80);

  /* ---- gates between biomes (rock pillars + energy shimmer) ---- */
  var GATE_Z=[18,-2,-22,-42,-62];
  var rockM=new THREE.MeshLambertMaterial({color:0x2c2018});
  GATE_Z.forEach(function(gz,gi){
    var grp=new THREE.Group();
    [-7.5,7.5].forEach(function(px){
      var pil=new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(2.4,7,2.4), rockM);
      pil.position.set(px,3.5,0); grp.add(pil);
    });
    var shimC=cnv(64,128), shx=shimC.getContext('2d');
    var shg=shx.createLinearGradient(0,0,0,128);
    shg.addColorStop(0,'rgba(255,120,30,0)'); shg.addColorStop(0.5,'rgba(255,150,50,0.55)');
    shg.addColorStop(1,'rgba(255,120,30,0)');
    shx.fillStyle=shg; shx.fillRect(0,0,64,128);
    var shim=new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.PlaneGeometry(13,6.4),
      new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({map:tex(shimC), transparent:true, side:THREE.DoubleSide, depthWrite:false}));
    shim.position.y=3.2; grp.add(shim);
    grp.position.set(0,0,gz);
    scene.add(grp);
    gates.push({z:gz, grp:grp, shim:shim, locked:true, idx:gi});
  });

  buildStronghold();
}

/* ---------------- town stronghold (repositionable, re-tintable) ---------------- */
function buildStronghold(){
  stronghold=new THREE.Group();
  var stone=new THREE.MeshLambertMaterial({color:0x3a3a44});
  var darkStone=new THREE.MeshLambertMaterial({color:0x26262e});
  strongMats.push(stone, darkStone);
  var tower=new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(6,8,6), stone);
  tower.position.y=4; stronghold.add(tower);
  for(var b=0;b<6;b++){
    var merlon=new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(1.1,1.1,0.7), darkStone);
    var ba=(b/6)*Math.PI*2;
    merlon.position.set(Math.cos(ba)*2.7, 8.5, Math.sin(ba)*2.7);
    stronghold.add(merlon);
  }
  var door=new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(1.8,3,0.3),
    new THREE.MeshLambertMaterial({color:0x1a0e08, emissive:0x661a00}));
  door.position.set(0,1.5,3.02); stronghold.add(door);
  for(var w=0;w<3;w++){
    var win=new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.PlaneGeometry(0.7,1.0),
      new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({color:0xff5a1e}));
    win.position.set(-1.8+w*1.8, 5.6, 3.01); stronghold.add(win);
  }
  stronghold.visible=false;
  scene.add(stronghold);
  strongFire=new THREE.Group();
  for(var f=0;f<10;f++){
    var cf=new THREE.Sprite(new THREE.SpriteMaterial({ map:FLAME_TEX,
      blending:THREE.AdditiveBlending, depthWrite:false, transparent:true }));
    cf.position.set((Math.random()-0.5)*5, 8.6+Math.random()*1.5, (Math.random()-0.5)*5);
    cf.scale.set(1.6,2.4,1); cf.userData.ph=Math.random()*9;
    strongFire.add(cf);
  }
  strongFire.visible=false; stronghold.add(strongFire);
}
var STRONG_TINTS=[0x3a3a44, 0x3f4a3a, 0x2c3444, 0x4a3226, 0x33333e, 0x2a1410];
function placeStronghold(townIdx, x, z){
  stronghold.position.set(x,0,z);
  stronghold.visible=true;
  strongMats[0].color.setHex(STRONG_TINTS[townIdx]);
  strongFire.visible=false;
}
function hideStronghold(){ stronghold.visible=false; strongFire.visible=false; }

/* ---------------- particles, damage numbers, hp bars ---------------- */
var particles=[];
/* pooled particles: bounded sprite reuse, zero per-frame alloc/dispose.
   Two pools (additive bursts vs normal smoke) so blending never toggles. */
var particlePoolAdd=[], particlePoolNorm=[];
function poolGet(additive){
  var pool=additive?particlePoolAdd:particlePoolNorm;
  for(var i=0;i<pool.length;i++) if(!pool[i].visible) return pool[i];
  if(pool.length>=(additive?160:80)) return null;
  var s=new THREE.Sprite(new THREE.SpriteMaterial({ map:GLOW_TEX, depthWrite:false, transparent:true,
    blending:additive?THREE.AdditiveBlending:THREE.NormalBlending }));
  s.visible=false; s.userData={}; scene.add(s); pool.push(s); return s;
}
function poolSpawn(pos, colorHex, additive, n, spd, smoke){
  n=Math.max(1,Math.round((n||10)*FBQ.particleScale)); spd=spd||4;
  for(var i=0;i<n;i++){
    var s=poolGet(additive); if(!s) return;
    s.visible=true; s.material.color.setHex(colorHex); s.material.opacity=0.9;
    s.position.copy(pos);
    var spread=smoke?1.4:0.6;
    s.position.x+=(Math.random()-0.5)*spread; s.position.z+=(Math.random()-0.5)*spread;
    var sc=smoke?(0.7+Math.random()*0.9):(0.5+Math.random()*0.9); s.scale.set(sc,sc,1);
    var u=s.userData, vspd=smoke?1.6:spd;
    u.vx=(Math.random()-0.5)*vspd; u.vy=smoke?(1.6+Math.random()*2.4):(Math.random()*spd*0.9+1); u.vz=(Math.random()-0.5)*vspd;
    u.life=smoke?(0.9+Math.random()*0.7):(0.55+Math.random()*0.35); u.age=0; u.smoke=!!smoke;
    particles.push(s);
  }
}
function spawnBurst(pos, colorHex, n, spd){ poolSpawn(pos, colorHex, true, n, spd, false); }
function updateParticles(dt){
  for(var i=particles.length-1;i>=0;i--){
    var p=particles[i], u=p.userData;
    u.age+=dt;
    if(u.age>=u.life){ p.visible=false; particles.splice(i,1); continue; }
    p.position.x+=u.vx*dt; p.position.y+=u.vy*dt; p.position.z+=u.vz*dt;
    var k=1-u.age/u.life;
    if(u.smoke){
      u.vy+=1.2*dt;
      p.material.opacity=k*0.8; p.scale.multiplyScalar(1+dt*1.7);
    } else {
      u.vy-=6*dt;
      p.material.opacity=k; p.scale.multiplyScalar(1-dt*0.6);
    }
  }
}

var dmgPool=[];
function dmgNumber(pos, amount, color){
  var d=null;
  for(var i=0;i<dmgPool.length;i++) if(!dmgPool[i].visible){ d=dmgPool[i]; break; }
  if(!d){
    if(dmgPool.length>24) return;
    var c=cnv(128,64), t=tex(c);
    var sp=new THREE.Sprite(new THREE.SpriteMaterial({ map:t, depthWrite:false, transparent:true }));
    sp.scale.set(2.4,1.2,1); sp.visible=false; sp.userData={c:c,t:t};
    scene.add(sp); dmgPool.push(sp); d=sp;
  }
  var x=d.userData.c.getContext('2d');
  x.clearRect(0,0,128,64);
  x.font='900 40px system-ui,sans-serif'; x.textAlign='center'; x.textBaseline='middle';
  x.lineWidth=7; x.strokeStyle='rgba(20,5,0,0.9)';
  var txt='-'+amount;
  x.strokeText(txt,64,32); x.fillStyle=color||'#ffd76a'; x.fillText(txt,64,32);
  d.userData.t.needsUpdate=true;
  d.position.copy(pos); d.position.y+=1.6;
  d.visible=true; d.userData.age=0; d.userData.life=0.9;
}
function updateDmg(dt){
  for(var i=0;i<dmgPool.length;i++){
    var d=dmgPool[i]; if(!d.visible) continue;
    d.userData.age+=dt;
    if(d.userData.age>=d.userData.life){ d.visible=false; continue; }
    d.position.y+=dt*2.4;
    d.material.opacity=1-d.userData.age/d.userData.life;
  }
}

function makeHpBar(w){
  var grp=new THREE.Group();
  var bg=new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.PlaneGeometry(w,0.22),
    new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({ color:0x1a0a06, depthWrite:false, transparent:true, opacity:0.85 }));
  var fg=new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.PlaneGeometry(w,0.22),
    new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({ color:0x7dff6a, depthWrite:false, transparent:true }));
  fg.position.z=0.01;
  grp.add(bg); grp.add(fg);
  grp.userData={fg:fg,w:w};
  grp.set=function(frac, hurt){
    frac=Math.max(0,Math.min(1,frac));
    grp.userData.fg.scale.x=frac;
    grp.userData.fg.position.x=-(1-frac)*grp.userData.w/2;
    grp.userData.fg.material.color.setHex(hurt?0xff5a3c:(frac>0.5?0x7dff6a:(frac>0.25?0xffd23c:0xff5a3c)));
  };
  return grp;
}

function classBadge(cls){
  var c=cnv(64,64), x=c.getContext('2d');
  x.fillStyle='rgba(10,5,2,0.85)';
  x.beginPath(); x.arc(32,32,28,0,Math.PI*2); x.fill();
  x.lineWidth=5; x.strokeStyle=CLASSES[cls].color; x.stroke();
  x.font='900 34px system-ui,sans-serif'; x.textAlign='center'; x.textBaseline='middle';
  x.fillStyle=CLASSES[cls].color; x.fillText(cls,32,34);
  var sp=new THREE.Sprite(new THREE.SpriteMaterial({ map:tex(c), depthWrite:false, transparent:true }));
  sp.scale.set(1.1,1.1,1);
  return sp;
}
function nameTag(text, color){
  var c=cnv(256,56), x=c.getContext('2d');
  x.font='900 30px system-ui,sans-serif'; x.textAlign='center'; x.textBaseline='middle';
  x.lineWidth=6; x.strokeStyle='rgba(15,5,0,0.9)'; x.strokeText(text,128,28);
  x.fillStyle=color||'#ffe9d6'; x.fillText(text,128,28);
  var sp=new THREE.Sprite(new THREE.SpriteMaterial({ map:tex(c), depthWrite:false, transparent:true }));
  sp.scale.set(3.4,0.75,1);
  return sp;
}

    villain:'inferno', vcls:'A',
    foes:[['Twiggy','A',85,11],['Twiggy','A',85,11],['Slick','B',100,13],['Slick','B',100,13],['Buzz','C',95,13],['Buzz','C',95,13],['Slag','D',135,13],['Slag','D',135,13]] },
  { id:'volcano', name:"Inferno's Volcano", sub:'Crater fortress',   trig:{x:0,z:-70,r:9}, gate:null,
    villain:'inferno', vcls:'D', boss:true,
    foes:[['LORD INFERNO','D',650,22,'boss'],['Twiggy','A',90,12],['Slick','B',105,13],['Buzz','C',100,14],['Slag','D',140,13]] }
];
var VILLAIN_NAMES = { twiggy:'TWIGGY', slick:'SLICK', buzz:'BUZZ', slag:'SLAG', inferno:'LORD INFERNO' };

/* ---------------- villain factory ---------------- */
function makeVillain(name, cls, scale){
  scale=scale||1;
  var g=new THREE.Group();
  var R=1.05*scale;
  var spec={ body:'#3a241a', dark:'#120806', core:CLASSES[cls].color };
  var body=new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.SphereGeometry(R,20,14),
    new THREE.MeshLambertMaterial({ map:bodyTexture(spec,'villain') }));
  body.rotation.y = -Math.PI/2;
  g.add(body);
  var glow=new THREE.Sprite(new THREE.SpriteMaterial({ map:GLOW_TEX, color:CLASSES[cls].hex,
    blending:THREE.AdditiveBlending, depthWrite:false, transparent:true, opacity:0.7 }));
  glow.scale.set(R*4,R*4,1); g.add(glow);
  var licks=[];
  var lm=new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({ map:FLAME_TEX, color:CLASSES[cls].hex, transparent:true,
    blending:THREE.AdditiveBlending, depthWrite:false, side:THREE.DoubleSide });
  for(var i=0;i<6;i++){
    var cone=new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.ConeGeometry(R*0.3,R*1.0,6), lm);
    var a=(i/6)*Math.PI*2;
    cone.position.set(Math.cos(a)*R*0.7, Math.sin(a)*R*0.7, R*0.3);
    cone.rotation.z=a-Math.PI/2;
    cone.userData.phase=Math.random()*9; cone.userData.base=0.85+Math.random()*0.3;
    g.add(cone); licks.push(cone);
  }
  if(name==='inferno'){
    var hornMat=new THREE.MeshLambertMaterial({color:0x1c0a05});
    [-1,1].forEach(function(s){
      var horn=new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.ConeGeometry(R*0.22,R*0.8,7), hornMat);
      horn.position.set(s*R*0.55, R*0.95, 0); horn.rotation.z=-s*0.45;
      g.add(horn);
    });
  }
  var badge=classBadge(cls); badge.position.y=R*1.9; g.add(badge);
  var tag=nameTag(VILLAIN_NAMES[name]||name, CLASSES[cls].color); tag.position.y=R*2.6; g.add(tag);
  var blobC=cnv(64,64), blobX=blobC.getContext('2d');
  var bg=blobX.createRadialGradient(32,32,2,32,32,30);
  bg.addColorStop(0,'rgba(0,0,0,0.42)'); bg.addColorStop(1,'rgba(0,0,0,0)');
  blobX.fillStyle=bg; blobX.fillRect(0,0,64,64);
  var blob=new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.PlaneGeometry(R*2.6,R*2.6),
    new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({ map:tex(blobC), transparent:true, depthWrite:false }));
  blob.rotation.x=-Math.PI/2; blob.position.y=-R*1.52; g.add(blob);
  g.userData={ licks:licks, R:R, body:body, glow:glow, blob:blob,
    bobPhase:Math.random()*9, flash:0, baseY:0, name:name };
  addLimbs(g, R, spec.body, spec.dark);
  return g;
}

var cine=null, castList=[], villainGroups=[];
(function buildDlgUI(){
  var st=document.createElement('style');
  st.textContent='#dlgov{position:fixed;inset:0;z-index:20;display:none;cursor:pointer;touch-action:manipulation;}'

    +'border:1px solid rgba(255,150,60,.5);border-radius:12px;font:800 14px system-ui;padding:10px 18px;cursor:pointer;}'
