  document.body.appendChild(ov);
  var tb=document.createElement('div'); tb.id='townbanner'; tb.innerHTML='<div></div><span></span>';
  document.body.appendChild(tb);
  var lastDlgTap=0;
  function dlgTapGuard(){ var n=Date.now(); if(n-lastDlgTap<350) return false; lastDlgTap=n; return true; }
  ov.addEventListener('pointerdown', function(e){ e.stopPropagation(); if(dlgTapGuard()) cineAdvance(); });
  ov.addEventListener('click', function(e){ e.stopPropagation(); if(dlgTapGuard()) cineAdvance(); });
  var skipBtn=document.getElementById('dlgskip');
  function dlgDoSkip(e){ if(e){ e.stopPropagation(); e.preventDefault(); } cineFinish(); }
  skipBtn.addEventListener('pointerdown', function(e){ if(dlgTapGuard()) dlgDoSkip(e); });
  skipBtn.addEventListener('click', function(e){ if(dlgTapGuard()) dlgDoSkip(e); });
})();

var SPEAKER_NAMES={ narrator:'—', ruko:'RUKO', sizzle:'SIZZLE', cinder:'CINDER', boulder:'BOULDER', blaze:'BLAZE',
  twiggy:'TWIGGY', slick:'SLICK', buzz:'BUZZ', slag:'SLAG', inferno:'LORD INFERNO' };

function clearCast(){
  for(var i=0;i<castList.length;i++) scene.remove(castList[i].group);
  castList=[]; villainGroups=[];
}
/* stage the 5 heroes + optional villains around (x,z), facing +Z (camera side) */
function stageLineupAt(x, z, villains){
  clearCast();
  var xs=[-6,-3,0,3,6];
  for(var i=0;i<HEROES.length;i++){
    var grp=makeBaby(HEROES[i], HEROES[i].face, 1);
    grp.position.set(x+xs[i], 1.55, z);
    grp.userData.baseY=1.55;
    grp.rotation.y=Math.PI; // face +Z toward camera
    scene.add(grp);
    castList.push({who:HEROES[i].id, group:grp});
  }
  if(villains) stageVillains(villains, x, z-9);
  return castList;
}
/* stage villains in an arc facing the player; squad must already be in castList */
function stageVillains(villains, x, z){
  villains.forEach(function(v, k){
    var sc=v.boss?1.7:1.15;
    var vg=makeVillain(v.name, v.cls, sc);
    vg.position.set(x+(k-(villains.length-1)/2)*3.6, sc*1.55, z-k*1.2);
    vg.userData.baseY=sc*1.55;
    vg.rotation.y=0; // face +Z toward heroes/camera
    scene.add(vg);
    castList.push({who:v.name, group:vg});
    villainGroups.push(vg);
  });
}
function clearVillains(){
  for(var i=0;i<villainGroups.length;i++){
    scene.remove(villainGroups[i]);
    for(var j=castList.length-1;j>=0;j--)
      if(castList[j].group===villainGroups[i]) castList.splice(j,1);
  }
  villainGroups=[];
}
function castWho(who){
  for(var i=0;i<castList.length;i++) if(castList[i].who===who) return castList[i].group;
  return null;
}

function playCine(lines, onDone){
  cine={ lines:lines, i:-1, onDone:onDone||null, chars:0, full:'', done:true,
    camPos:new THREE.Vector3(), camTgt:new THREE.Vector3(0,1.5,0), init:false };
  document.getElementById('dlgov').style.display='block';
  cineAdvance();
}
function cineRender(){
  var line=cine.lines[cine.i];
  var nm=document.getElementById('dlgname'), tx=document.getElementById('dlgtext');
  if(line.who==='narrator'){
    nm.textContent='— WEST EMBER HIGH —'; nm.style.color='#8a6a5a';
    tx.className='narr';
  } else {
    nm.textContent=SPEAKER_NAMES[line.who]||line.who.toUpperCase(); nm.style.color='#ffd76a';
    tx.className='';
  }
  cine.full=line.text; cine.chars=0; cine.done=false; tx.textContent='';
  var spk=castWho(line.who);
  var tp=new THREE.Vector3(), pp=new THREE.Vector3();
  if(spk){
    tp.copy(spk.position); tp.y+=1.2;
    // camera in front of speaker (speaker faces +Z, so camera sits at +Z)
    pp.copy(spk.position).add(new THREE.Vector3(2.8, 2.2, 6.4));
  } else { tp.set(0,1.6,30); pp.set(0,5.5,44); }
  cine.camTgt.copy(tp); cine.camPos.copy(pp);
  if(!cine.init){ camera.position.copy(pp); cine.init=true; }
  for(var i=0;i<castList.length;i++){
    var g2=castList[i].group, isSpk=(castList[i].who===line.who);
    g2.userData.glow.material.opacity=isSpk?1.0:0.4;
    g2.scale.setScalar(isSpk?1.14:1.0);
  }
}
function cineAdvance(){
  if(!cine) return;
  var tx=document.getElementById('dlgtext');
  if(!cine.done){ tx.textContent=cine.full; tx.className = cine.lines[cine.i].who==='narrator'?'narr':''; cine.done=true; return; }
  cine.i++;
  if(cine.i>=cine.lines.length){ cineFinish(); return; }
  cineRender();
}
function cineFinish(){
  if(!cine) return;
  document.getElementById('dlgov').style.display='none';
  for(var i=0;i<castList.length;i++){
    castList[i].group.scale.setScalar(1.0);
    castList[i].group.userData.glow.material.opacity=0.85;
  }
  var cb=cine.onDone; cine=null;
  if(cb) cb();
}
var cineLook=new THREE.Vector3(0,1.5,0);
function updateCine(t, dt){
  if(!cine) return;
  if(!cine.done){
    cine.chars+=dt*62;
    var n=Math.min(cine.full.length, Math.floor(cine.chars));
    var tx=document.getElementById('dlgtext');
    if(tx.textContent.length!==n) tx.textContent=cine.full.slice(0,n);
    if(n>=cine.full.length) cine.done=true;
  }
  camera.position.lerp(cine.camPos, 1-Math.pow(0.02, dt));
  cineLook.lerp(cine.camTgt, 1-Math.pow(0.02, dt));
  camera.lookAt(cineLook);
  for(var i=0;i<castList.length;i++) updateBaby(castList[i].group, t, dt);
}

function townBanner(name, sub, hold, cb){
  var tb=document.getElementById('townbanner');
  tb.querySelector('div').textContent=name.toUpperCase();
  tb.querySelector('span').textContent=sub.toUpperCase();
  tb.style.display='block';
  setTimeout(function(){ tb.style.display='none'; if(cb) cb(); }, hold||1800);
}

/* =====================================================================
   SQUAD + EXPLORATION — Blaze is player-driven, the crew follows
   ===================================================================== */
var state='TITLE';
var units=[], player=null, followers=[];
var currentTown=-1, battleTime=0, arenaCenter=new THREE.Vector3();
var liberated=[false,false,false,false,false,false], seenIntro=false;
try{ var sv=localStorage.getItem('fb3d_openworld');
  if(sv){ var p=JSON.parse(sv);
    if(p&&p.liberated instanceof Array) for(var li=0;li<6;li++) liberated[li]=!!p.liberated[li];
    if(p&&p.seenIntro) seenIntro=true; } }catch(e){}
function saveProgress(){ try{ localStorage.setItem('fb3d_openworld',
  JSON.stringify({liberated:liberated, seenIntro:seenIntro})); }catch(e){} }

var FOLLOW_OFF=[ // ruko, boulder, sizzle, cinder — behind Blaze (+Z)
  {id:'ruko', dx:-2.6, dz:2.8}, {id:'boulder', dx:2.6, dz:2.8},
  {id:'sizzle', dx:-1.3, dz:5.0}, {id:'cinder', dx:1.3, dz:5.0}
];
function createSquad(x, z){
  for(var i=units.length-1;i>=0;i--) scene.remove(units[i].group);
  units=[]; followers=[]; clearCast();
  var saved=loadLevels();
  HEROES.forEach(function(h){
    var g=makeBaby(h, h.face, 1);
    g.userData.baseY=1.55;
    var tag=nameTag(h.name,'#ffd76a'); tag.position.y=2.9; g.add(tag);
    var hp=makeHpBar(2.2); hp.position.y=2.35; g.add(hp); hp.set(1);
    scene.add(g);
    var u={ group:g, spec:h, side:'hero', hp:h.hp, maxHp:h.hp, atk:h.atk,
      speed:h.speed, range:h.range, cd:0, hpbar:hp, state:'idle', dead:false,
      lunge:0, lungeDir:null, isBlaze:(h.id==='blaze'),
      level:1, exp:0, expNext:100 };
    if(saved&&saved[h.id]){
      var s=saved[h.id];
      u.level=s.level||1; u.exp=s.exp||0; u.expNext=s.expNext||100*u.level;
      u.maxHp=s.maxHp||u.maxHp; u.atk=s.atk||u.atk; u.hp=u.maxHp;
    }
    units.push(u);
    castList.push({who:h.id, group:g});
    if(u.isBlaze){ player=u; g.position.set(x,1.05,z); }
    else followers.push(u);
  });
  // formation around start
  followers.forEach(function(f){
    for(var k=0;k<FOLLOW_OFF.length;k++) if(FOLLOW_OFF[k].id===f.spec.id){
      f.group.position.set(x+FOLLOW_OFF[k].dx, 1.05, z+FOLLOW_OFF[k].dz);
    }
  });
  stepAcc=0; nextEncounter=(7+Math.random()*8)*2.5;
  lastPX=x; lastPZ=z;
  updateExpBar();
}
function setObjective(txt){
  var ob=document.getElementById('objbanner');
  if(!txt){ ob.style.display='none'; return; }
  ob.textContent=txt; ob.style.display='block';
}
function nextObjective(){
  for(var i=0;i<6;i++) if(!liberated[i]){ setObjective('➤ HEAD TO '+TOWNS[i].name.toUpperCase()); return; }
  setObjective('🎓 GRADUATED — THE LAND IS SAFE!');
}

/* ---------------- input: virtual joystick + WASD ---------------- */
var joy={dx:0, dy:0, active:false, id:null};
var keys={};
function bindInput(){
  var joyEl=document.getElementById('joy'), knob=document.getElementById('knob');
  function setKnob(dx,dy){
    knob.style.transform='translate(calc(-50% + '+dx+'px), calc(-50% + '+dy+'px))';
  }
  joyEl.addEventListener('pointerdown', function(e){
    joy.active=true; joy.id=e.pointerId;
    try { joyEl.setPointerCapture(e.pointerId); } catch(_e){}
    joyMove(e);
    e.preventDefault();
    e.stopPropagation();
  }, {passive:false});
  function joyMove(e){
    if(!joy.active||e.pointerId!==joy.id) return;
    var r=joyEl.getBoundingClientRect();
    var cx=r.left+r.width/2, cy=r.top+r.height/2;
    var dx=e.clientX-cx, dy=e.clientY-cy;
    var max=r.width/2-14, len=Math.hypot(dx,dy);
    if(len>max){ dx=dx/len*max; dy=dy/len*max; }
    joy.dx=dx/max; joy.dy=dy/max;
    setKnob(dx,dy);
  }
  joyEl.addEventListener('pointermove', joyMove);
  function joyEnd(e){
    if(e.pointerId!==joy.id) return;
    joy.active=false; joy.id=null; joy.dx=0; joy.dy=0; setKnob(0,0);
  }
  joyEl.addEventListener('pointerup', joyEnd);
  joyEl.addEventListener('pointercancel', joyEnd);

  window.addEventListener('keydown', function(e){
    var k=(e.key||'').toLowerCase();
    if(cine){
      if(k===' '||k==='enter'){ cineAdvance(); e.preventDefault(); }
      else if(k==='escape'||k==='s'){ cineFinish(); e.preventDefault(); }
      return;
    }
    if(k==='w'||k==='a'||k==='s'||k==='d'||k==='arrowup'||k==='arrowdown'||k==='arrowleft'||k==='arrowright'){
      keys[k]=true; e.preventDefault();
    }
  });
  window.addEventListener('keyup', function(e){ keys[(e.key||'').toLowerCase()]=false; });

  var cv=renderer.domElement;
  cv.addEventListener('wheel', function(e){
    camDistMul=Math.max(0.75,Math.min(1.35,camDistMul+e.deltaY*0.001));
    e.preventDefault();
  }, {passive:false});

  window.addEventListener('resize', function(){
    camera.aspect=innerWidth/innerHeight; camera.updateProjectionMatrix();
    renderer.setSize(innerWidth,innerHeight);
  });

  /* TAP TO START is wired by the standalone script before </body> (independent of init/bindInput) */
  document.getElementById('pstart').addEventListener('click', function(){ saveCreation(); });
  document.getElementById('againbtn').addEventListener('click', function(){
    document.getElementById('victoryov').style.display='none';
    if(onVictoryNext) onVictoryNext(); else showHub();
  });
  document.getElementById('retrybtn').addEventListener('click', function(){
    document.getElementById('defeatov').style.display='none';
    if(onDefeatNext) onDefeatNext(); else showHub();
  });
  document.getElementById('vhubbtn').addEventListener('click', function(){
    document.getElementById('victoryov').style.display='none'; showHub();
  });
  document.getElementById('dhubbtn').addEventListener('click', function(){
    document.getElementById('defeatov').style.display='none'; showHub();
  });
  document.getElementById('lbback').addEventListener('click', function(){
    document.getElementById('lbov').style.display='none'; showHub();
  });
  document.getElementById('tab_top').addEventListener('click', function(){ renderLB('top'); });
  document.getElementById('tab_champ').addEventListener('click', function(){ renderLB('champions'); });
}

function resolveColliders(u, nx, nz){
  for(var i=0;i<colliders.length;i++){
    var c=colliders[i];
    var dx=nx-c.x, dz=nz-c.z, d=Math.hypot(dx,dz), min=c.r+0.9;
    if(d<min&&d>0.001){ nx=c.x+dx/d*min; nz=c.z+dz/d*min; }
  }
  return {x:nx, z:nz};
}
function gateBlocked(ox, oz, nx, nz){
  for(var i=0;i<gates.length;i++){
    var g=gates[i];
    if(!g.locked) continue;
    if(Math.abs(nx)>9.5) continue; // can walk around the sides? no — corridor walls; keep simple
    if((oz-g.z)*(nz-g.z)<0){ return g.z; }
  }
  return null;
}

var camDistMul=1;
function moveInput(){
  var vx=0, vz=0;
  if(joy.active){ vx+=joy.dx; vz+=joy.dy; }
  if(keys['w']||keys['arrowup']) vz-=1;
  if(keys['s']||keys['arrowdown']) vz+=1;
  if(keys['a']||keys['arrowleft']) vx-=1;
  if(keys['d']||keys['arrowright']) vx+=1;
  var l=Math.hypot(vx,vz);
  if(l>1){ vx/=l; vz/=l; }
  return {x:vx, z:vz, on:l>0.08};
}

function updateExplore(dt, t){
  var mv=moveInput();
  var bp=player.group.position;
  if(mv.on){
    var sp=7.5*dt;
    var nx=bp.x+mv.x*sp, nz=bp.z+mv.z*sp;
    // gates
    var gz=gateBlocked(bp.x, bp.z, nx, nz);
    if(gz!==null){ nz=gz+(bp.z>gz?0.6:-0.6); nx=bp.x; }
    // world bounds
    nx=Math.max(-26,Math.min(26,nx)); nz=Math.max(-108,Math.min(44,nz));
    var rc=resolveColliders(player, nx, nz);
    bp.x=rc.x; bp.z=rc.z;
    player.group.rotation.y=Math.atan2(mv.x, mv.z);
    beepTick+=dt;
  }
  updateBaby(player.group, t, dt);

  // followers: drift to formation slots
  followers.forEach(function(f){
    var off=null;
    for(var k=0;k<FOLLOW_OFF.length;k++) if(FOLLOW_OFF[k].id===f.spec.id) off=FOLLOW_OFF[k];
    var tx=bp.x+off.dx, tz=bp.z+off.dz;
    var fp=f.group.position;
    var dx=tx-fp.x, dz=tz-fp.z, d=Math.hypot(dx,dz);
    if(d>0.7){
      var fs=Math.min(f.speed, d*3.2)*dt;
      fp.x+=dx/d*fs; fp.z+=dz/d*fs;
      f.group.rotation.y=Math.atan2(dx,dz);
    }
    updateBaby(f.group, t, dt);
    f.hpbar.quaternion.copy(camera.quaternion);
  });
  player.hpbar.quaternion.copy(camera.quaternion);

  // follow camera
  var cx=bp.x*0.55, cz=bp.z+13.5*camDistMul, cy=10.5*camDistMul;
  camera.position.lerp(new THREE.Vector3(cx,cy,cz), 1-Math.pow(0.01,dt));
  camera.lookAt(bp.x*0.6, 1.5, bp.z-5);

  // fog follows biome
  var pz=bp.z, fogT=BIOMES[0].fog;
  for(var b=0;b<BIOMES.length;b++) if(pz<=BIOMES[b].z0&&pz>BIOMES[b].z1) fogT=BIOMES[b].fog;
  scene.fog.color.lerp(new THREE.Color(fogT), 1-Math.pow(0.2,dt));
  document.getElementById('stagepill').textContent='🔥 '+biomeNameAt(pz).toUpperCase();

  // town trigger zones (story mode only)
  if(!cine && state==='EXPLORE' && gameMode==='story'){
    for(var i=0;i<TOWNS.length;i++){
      if(liberated[i]) continue;
      var tr=TOWNS[i].trig;
      var ddx=bp.x-tr.x, ddz=bp.z-tr.z;
      if(ddx*ddx+ddz*ddz<tr.r*tr.r){ enterTown(i); break; }
    }
  }
  // random encounters: wild fire-monsters every 7-15 steps of travel
  if(!cine && state==='EXPLORE' && (gameMode==='story'||gameMode==='free')){
    var moved=Math.hypot(bp.x-lastPX, bp.z-lastPZ);
    stepAcc+=moved; lastPX=bp.x; lastPZ=bp.z;
    if(stepAcc>nextEncounter){
      stepAcc=0; nextEncounter=(7+Math.random()*8)*2.5;
      startEncounter();
    }
  }
}
var beepTick=0;
function biomeNameAt(z){
  for(var b=0;b<BIOMES.length;b++) if(z<=BIOMES[b].z0&&z>BIOMES[b].z1) return BIOMES[b].id==='ember'?'Ember Village':TOWNS[b].name;
  return TOWNS[0].name;
}

/* =====================================================================
   BATTLE (arena spawns around the town trigger) + TOWN FLOW
   ===================================================================== */
var strongHP=0, strongMax=0;

function spawnFoes(comp, cx, cz){
  comp.forEach(function(f, i){
    var boss=f[4]==='boss';
    var key=f[0].toLowerCase();
    if(key.indexOf('inferno')>=0) key='inferno';
    var g=makeVillain(key, f[1], boss?1.7:1.0);
    var R=g.userData.R;
    var a=(i/comp.length)*Math.PI*2+0.4, rr=8+Math.random()*2;
    g.position.set(cx+Math.cos(a)*rr, R*1.55, cz+Math.sin(a)*rr*0.7);
    g.userData.baseY=R*1.55;
    var hp=makeHpBar(boss?3.4:2.0); hp.position.y=R*2.1+0.9; g.add(hp); hp.set(1);
    scene.add(g);
    units.push({ group:g, side:'foe', name:f[0], cls:f[1], hp:f[2], maxHp:f[2],
      atk:f[3], speed:3.4+Math.random()*0.8, range:2.3, cd:Math.random()*0.8, hpbar:hp,
      state:'seek', dead:false, lunge:0, lungeDir:null, boss:boss });
  });
  updateFoeCount();
}
function updateFoeCount(){
  var n=0; for(var i=0;i<units.length;i++) if(units[i].side==='foe'&&!units[i].dead) n++;
  document.getElementById('foecount').textContent=n;
}
function nearestFoe(u){
  var best=null, bd=1e9;
  for(var i=0;i<units.length;i++){ var v=units[i];
    if(v.side==='foe'&&!v.dead){ var d=u.group.position.distanceTo(v.group.position);
      if(d<bd){ bd=d; best=v; } } }
  return best?{u:best,d:bd}:null;
}
function nearestHero(u){
  var best=null, bd=1e9;
  for(var i=0;i<units.length;i++){ var v=units[i];
    if(v.side==='hero'&&!v.dead){ var d=u.group.position.distanceTo(v.group.position);
      if(d<bd){ bd=d; best=v; } } }
  return best?{u:best,d:bd}:null;
}
var HERO_COUNTER={ruko:'A', boulder:'D', sizzle:'C', cinder:'B'};
var HERO_WEAK={ruko:'B', boulder:'A', sizzle:'D', cinder:'C'};
var lastMatchToast=0;
function dealDamage(src, tgt, amount){
  if(tgt.dead) return;
  // Extinguish Matchup Matrix: correct counter = big damage, wrong = backfire
  if(src && src.side==='hero' && tgt.cls && src.spec && src.spec.id){
    var id=src.spec.id, mult=1, note=null;
    if(id==='blaze'){ mult=1.25; }
    else if(HERO_COUNTER[id]===tgt.cls){ mult=2; note='⚡ SUPER EFFECTIVE! '+src.spec.name+' counters Class '+tgt.cls; }
    else if(HERO_WEAK[id]===tgt.cls){ mult=0.5; note='💦 BACKFIRE! Wrong match for '+src.spec.name;
      src.hp=Math.max(1, src.hp-6); src.hpbar.set(src.hp/src.maxHp); }
    if(note && performance.now()-lastMatchToast>1500){ lastMatchToast=performance.now(); showToast(note, 1400); }
    amount=Math.round(amount*mult);
  }
  /* FBF fire spread: hero attacks ignite nearby flammables; hero tools vs fire */
  if(src && src.side==='hero' && typeof FBF!=='undefined' && tgt.group){
    FBF.ignite(tgt.group.position.x, tgt.group.position.z, 5);
    FBF.combatHit(tgt.group.position, src);
  }
  tgt.hp-=amount;
  tgt.group.userData.flash=1;
  tgt.hpbar.set(tgt.hp/tgt.maxHp, true);
  var p=tgt.group.position.clone();
  dmgNumber(p, Math.round(amount), src.side==='hero'?'#ffd76a':'#ff6a5a');
  spawnBurst(p.clone().add(new THREE.Vector3(0,1,0)), src.side==='hero'?0xffb347:0xff3a2a, 8, 4);
  beep(src.side==='hero'?300+Math.random()*120:180, 0.07, 'square', 0.05);
  if(tgt.hp<=0){
    tgt.dead=true; tgt.state='dying'; tgt.deathT=0;
    fbExtinguishPoof(tgt.group);
    spawnBurst(p.clone().add(new THREE.Vector3(0,1,0)), 0xff8c2e, 16, 6);
    if(tgt.side==='foe') updateFoeCount();
  }
}

/* wild encounters: scale with squad level + depth into the world */
function startEncounter(){
  state='BATTLE'; battleKind='encounter'; battleTime=0;
  var bp=player.group.position;
  arenaCenter.set(bp.x, 0, bp.z);
  var n=2+Math.floor(Math.random()*3);
  var comp=[];
  var clss=['A','B','C','D'];
  var names={A:'WILD TWIGGY',B:'WILD SLICK',C:'WILD BUZZ',D:'WILD SLAG'};
  var lv=avgSquadLevel();
  for(var i=0;i<n;i++){
    var c=clss[Math.floor(Math.random()*4)];
    comp.push([names[c], c, Math.round(50*lv)+20, Math.round(7*lv)+3]);
  }
  spawnFoes(comp, bp.x, bp.z);
  document.getElementById('foepill').style.display='block';
  setObjective('⚔️ WILD FIRE-MONSTERS ATTACK!');
  beep(180,0.2,'sawtooth',0.08); setTimeout(function(){beep(140,0.25,'sawtooth',0.08);},150);
}

function enterTown(i){
  currentTown=i;
  state='CINE';
  var t=TOWNS[i];
  setObjective(null);
  document.getElementById('stagepill').textContent='🔥 '+t.name.toUpperCase();
  townBanner('CHAPTER '+(i+1)+' \u2014 '+t.name, t.sub, 2500, function(){
    var bp=player.group.position;
    // stage villains in front of the squad
    stageVillains([{name:t.villain, cls:t.vcls, boss:!!t.boss}], bp.x, bp.z-7);
    playCine(DIALOGUE.towns[t.id], function(){
      clearVillains();
      startTownBattle(i);
    });
  });
}

function startTownBattle(i){
  var t=TOWNS[i], bp=player.group.position;
  state='BATTLE'; battleKind='town'; battleTime=0;
  arenaCenter.set(bp.x, 0, bp.z-8);
  // stronghold rises ahead
  placeStronghold(i, bp.x, bp.z-20);
  strongMax=strongHP=260+i*70+(t.boss?260:0);
  spawnFoes(t.foes, arenaCenter.x, arenaCenter.z);
  document.getElementById('foepill').style.display='block';
  setObjective('⚔️ LIBERATE '+t.name.toUpperCase()+'!');
  beep(220,0.25,'sawtooth',0.08); setTimeout(function(){beep(330,0.25,'sawtooth',0.08);},160);
}

function hitStronghold(amount){
  strongHP-=amount;
  var sp=stronghold.position;
  stronghold.position.x+=(Math.random()-0.5)*0.3;
  spawnBurst(new THREE.Vector3(sp.x+(Math.random()-0.5)*5, 5+Math.random()*3, sp.z), 0xffa040, 10, 5);
  dmgNumber(new THREE.Vector3(sp.x, 9, sp.z), Math.round(amount), '#ffd76a');
  beep(140,0.12,'sawtooth',0.07);
  if(typeof FBF!=='undefined') FBF.ignite(sp.x, sp.z, 6); // stronghold hits start fires
  if(strongHP<=strongMax*0.5) strongFire.visible=true;
  if(strongHP<=0){ strongHP=0; victory(); }
}
function heroesAlive(){ var n=0; for(var i=0;i<units.length;i++) if(units[i].side==='hero'&&!units[i].dead) n++; return n; }

function updateBattle(dt, t){
  battleTime+=dt;
  var anyFoe=false;
  for(var i=0;i<units.length;i++){ var u=units[i]; if(u.side==='foe'&&!u.dead){anyFoe=true;break;} }

  for(var k=units.length-1;k>=0;k--){
    var u=units[k], g=u.group;
    if(u.dead){
      if(u.state==='dying'){
        u.deathT+=dt;
        g.position.y-=dt*2.2; g.scale.multiplyScalar(1-dt*1.6);
        if(u.deathT>0.7){ scene.remove(g); u.state='gone'; }
      }
      continue;
    }
    updateBaby(g, t, dt);
    u.cd-=dt;

    var tgt=null, dist=1e9, tgtPos=null;
    if(u.side==='hero'){
      var nf=nearestFoe(u);
      if(nf){ tgt=nf.u; dist=nf.d; tgtPos=tgt.group.position; }
      else if(strongHP>0){ tgtPos=new THREE.Vector3(stronghold.position.x,2,stronghold.position.z); dist=g.position.distanceTo(tgtPos); tgt='stronghold'; }
    } else {
      var nh=nearestHero(u);
      if(nh){ tgt=nh.u; dist=nh.d; tgtPos=tgt.group.position; }
    }
    if(tgtPos){
      var dir=new THREE.Vector3().subVectors(tgtPos, g.position); dir.y=0;
      var dlen=dir.length(); if(dlen>0.001) dir.normalize();
      var reach=(tgt==='stronghold')?5.2:u.range;
      var driven=false;
      // Blaze can still be steered in battle (dodge!)
      if(u.isBlaze){
        var mv=moveInput();
        if(mv.on){
          g.position.x+=mv.x*u.speed*dt; g.position.z+=mv.z*u.speed*dt;
          g.rotation.y=Math.atan2(mv.x,mv.z);
          driven=true;
        }
      }
      if(!driven){
        if(dlen>reach){
          g.position.addScaledVector(dir, u.speed*dt);
          g.rotation.y=Math.atan2(dir.x, dir.z);
          u.state='seek';
        } else {
          g.rotation.y=Math.atan2(dir.x, dir.z);
          u.state='attack';
          if(u.cd<=0){
            u.cd=u.side==='hero'?(u.spec.id==='sizzle'?1.5:1.1):1.4;
            u.lunge=0.28; u.lungeDir=dir.clone();
            var dmg=u.atk*(0.85+Math.random()*0.3);
            if(tgt==='stronghold'){ hitStronghold(dmg); }
            else {
              if(u.side==='hero'&&u.spec.id==='cinder'){
                for(var j=0;j<units.length;j++){ var v=units[j];
                  if(v.side==='foe'&&!v.dead&&v.group.position.distanceTo(tgt.group.position)<3.4)
                    dealDamage(u, v, dmg*(v===tgt?1:0.6));
                }
              } else dealDamage(u, tgt, dmg);
            }
          }
        }
      } else if(dlen<=reach){
        // steering but in range: still attack
        g.rotation.y=Math.atan2(dir.x, dir.z);
        if(u.cd<=0){
          u.cd=1.1; u.lunge=0.28; u.lungeDir=dir.clone();
          var dmg2=u.atk*(0.85+Math.random()*0.3);
          if(tgt==='stronghold') hitStronghold(dmg2); else dealDamage(u, tgt, dmg2);
        }
      }
      if(u.lunge>0&&u.lungeDir){
        u.lunge-=dt;
        var k2=Math.sin((0.28-u.lunge)/0.28*Math.PI)*1.1;
        g.position.addScaledVector(u.lungeDir, k2*dt*8);
      }
      // stay in the arena
      var ax=g.position.x-arenaCenter.x, az=g.position.z-arenaCenter.z;
      var ad=Math.hypot(ax,az);
      if(ad>26){ g.position.x=arenaCenter.x+ax/ad*26; g.position.z=arenaCenter.z+az/ad*26; }
    }
    u.hpbar.quaternion.copy(camera.quaternion);
  }

  if(strongFire.visible){
    for(var f=0;f<strongFire.children.length;f++){
      var cf=strongFire.children[f];
      var s=0.85+0.35*Math.sin(t*10+cf.userData.ph);
      cf.scale.set(1.6*s,2.4*s,1);
    }
    if(Math.random()<dt*8){
      var sp2=stronghold.position;
      spawnBurst(new THREE.Vector3(sp2.x+(Math.random()-0.5)*5,8.6,sp2.z),0xff6a1e,2,2);
    }
  }

  // battle camera: 3/4 view over the arena
  var dist=21*camDistMul, h=13.5*camDistMul;
  camera.position.set(arenaCenter.x, h, arenaCenter.z+dist);
  camera.lookAt(arenaCenter.x, 1, arenaCenter.z-2);

  if(heroesAlive()===0 && state==='BATTLE') defeat();
  // victory when every foe is down (encounter / practice / tournament: no stronghold)
  if(state==='BATTLE' && battleKind!=='town' && battleKind!=='rescue' && !(battleKind==='survival'&&survBetween)){
    var anyFoeLeft=false;
    for(var i2=0;i2<units.length;i2++) if(units[i2].side==='foe' && !units[i2].dead){ anyFoeLeft=true; break; }
    if(!anyFoeLeft) victory();
  }
}

function victory(){
  if(state!=='BATTLE') return;
  state='VICTORY';
  var t=TOWNS[currentTown];
  var lib=DIALOGUE.liberate[Math.floor(Math.random()*DIALOGUE.liberate.length)][0];
  var fxPos = battleKind==='town' ? stronghold.position : new THREE.Vector3(arenaCenter.x, 3, arenaCenter.z);
  for(var i=0;i<5;i++)
    spawnBurst(new THREE.Vector3(fxPos.x+(Math.random()-0.5)*6, 4+Math.random()*5, fxPos.z), [0xff8c2e,0xffd76a,0xff3a2a][i%3], 18, 8);
  strongFire.visible=true;
  beep(523,0.16,'square',0.08); setTimeout(function(){beep(659,0.16,'square',0.08);},170);
  setTimeout(function(){beep(784,0.3,'square',0.08);},340);
  setTimeout(function(){
    if(battleKind==='encounter'){
      // quick wild battle: EXP, then straight back to roaming
      var exp=Math.round(20+Math.abs(arenaCenter.z)/6);
      grantExpAll(exp);
      lbPost('top', totalExp);
      reviveSquad(); clearArena();
      state='EXPLORE'; showJoy(true);
      document.getElementById('foepill').style.display='none';
      setObjective('✅ WILD DEFEATED! +'+exp+' EXP');
      setTimeout(function(){ if(gameMode==='story') nextObjective(); else setObjective('🧭 FREE ROAM — keep exploring!'); }, 2400);
      return;
    }
    if(battleKind==='practice'){
      // drills: no EXP, no fail — all learning
      reviveSquad(); clearArena();
      showVictoryPanel('🎯','DRILL COMPLETE','“Right tool, right fire. That\'s the job.” — BOULDER',
        'Dummies extinguished: <b>4/4</b> · no EXP, all learning 🔥',
        'DRILL AGAIN 🎯', function(){ document.getElementById('victoryov').style.display='none'; startPractice(); });
      return;
    }
    if(battleKind==='tournament'){ clearArena(); tournamentRoundWin(); return; }
    if(battleKind==='survival'){
      var wv=survWave, wexp=15+wv*5;
      grantExpAll(wexp); addTotalExp(wexp*5);
      units.forEach(function(u){ if(u.side==='hero'&&!u.dead){ u.hp=Math.min(u.maxHp, u.hp+u.maxHp*0.25); u.hpbar.set(u.hp/u.maxHp); } });
      showToast('\uD83D\uDC80 WAVE '+wv+' CLEARED! +'+wexp+' EXP', 1500);
      state='BATTLE'; survBetween=true;
      setTimeout(function(){
        if(state==='BATTLE'&&battleKind==='survival'){
          townBanner('WAVE '+(wv+1), 'THEY KEEP COMING!', 1400, function(){ spawnSurvWave(); });
        }
      }, 1400);
      return;
    }
    if(battleKind==='rescue'){
      var secs=Math.max(0, Math.ceil(rescueTime));
      var rexp=60+secs*2;
      grantExpAll(rexp); addTotalExp(rexp*5);
      lbPost('top', totalExp);
      reviveSquad(); clearArena(); clearRescueProps();
      showVictoryPanel('\uD83D\uDEA8','RESCUE COMPLETE','"Every pup home safe. That\'s the job." \u2014 BOULDER',
        'Pups saved: <b>5/5</b> &middot; Time left: <b>'+secs+'s</b> &middot; +'+rexp+' EXP',
        'RESCUE AGAIN \uD83D\uDEA8', function(){ document.getElementById('victoryov').style.display='none'; startRescue(); });
      return;
    }
    // ---- town story flow ----
    hideStronghold();
    // liberation line with the squad on screen
    playCine([lib], function(){
      liberated[currentTown]=true; saveProgress();
      grantExpAll(40+currentTown*15);
      lbPost('top', totalExp);
      reviveSquad(); clearArena();
      if(t.boss){
        // GRADUATION at the arena
        var bp=player.group.position;
        playCine(DIALOGUE.graduate, function(){
          showVictoryPanel('🎓<br>GRADUATED!','WEST EMBER HIGH — CLASS OF FIRE',
            '“I came in the new kid. I\'m leaving family.” — BLAZE',
            'Towns liberated: <b>6/6</b> &middot; Time in final battle: <b>'+Math.round(battleTime)+'s</b>',
            'PLAY AGAIN 🏠', showHub);
          setObjective('🎓 GRADUATED — THE LAND IS SAFE!');
        });
      } else {
        openGate(currentTown);
        state='EXPLORE';
        document.getElementById('joy').style.display='block';
        document.getElementById('foepill').style.display='none';
        nextObjective();
        setTimeout(function(){ setObjective('✅ '+t.name.toUpperCase()+' LIBERATED!'); }, 10);
        setTimeout(nextObjective, 2600);
      }
    });
  }, 1300);
}
function openGate(townIdx){
  var g=gates[townIdx];
  if(!g||!g.locked) return;
  g.locked=false;
  spawnBurst(new THREE.Vector3(0,3,g.z), 0xffb347, 24, 7);
  // sink the barrier
  var grp=g.grp, sy=0;
  var sink=setInterval(function(){
    sy+=0.25; grp.position.y=-sy;
    if(sy>7){ clearInterval(sink); scene.remove(grp); }
  }, 40);
  beep(660,0.2,'square',0.08);
}
function applyLiberated(){
  for(var i=0;i<6;i++) if(liberated[i]&&gates[i]){ gates[i].locked=false; scene.remove(gates[i].grp); }
}
function clearArena(){
  for(var i=units.length-1;i>=0;i--){
    if(units[i].side==='foe'){ scene.remove(units[i].group); units.splice(i,1); }
    else { // heal + revive squad
      var u=units[i];
      u.dead=false; u.state='idle'; u.hp=u.maxHp; u.hpbar.set(1);
      u.group.scale.setScalar(1);
      u.group.position.y=u.group.userData.baseY;
    }
  }
  // regroup squad around Blaze
  var bp=player.group.position;
  followers.forEach(function(f){
    for(var k=0;k<FOLLOW_OFF.length;k++) if(FOLLOW_OFF[k].id===f.spec.id){
      f.group.position.set(bp.x+FOLLOW_OFF[k].dx, 1.05, bp.z+FOLLOW_OFF[k].dz);
    }
  });
  updateFoeCount();
}
function defeat(){
  if(state!=='BATTLE') return;
  state='DEFEAT';
  showJoy(false);
  beep(200,0.4,'sawtooth',0.08);
  if(battleKind==='encounter'){
    // the wild ones scatter; squad patches up and keeps roaming
    reviveSquad(true); clearArena();
    document.getElementById('defeatsub').textContent='THE WILD ONES SCATTERED…';
    onDefeatNext=function(){
      state='EXPLORE'; showJoy(true);
      document.getElementById('foepill').style.display='none';
      if(gameMode==='story') nextObjective(); else setObjective('🧭 FREE ROAM — keep exploring!');
    };
  } else if(battleKind==='practice'){
    document.getElementById('defeatsub').textContent='PRACTICE MAKES PERFECT';
    onDefeatNext=function(){ document.getElementById('defeatov').style.display='none'; startPractice(); };
  } else if(battleKind==='tournament'){
    document.getElementById('defeatsub').textContent='ELIMINATED IN ROUND '+tourn.round;
    onDefeatNext=showHub;
  } else if(battleKind==='rescue'){
    clearRescueProps();
    document.getElementById('defeatsub').textContent='THE BUILDING BURNED DOWN\u2026';
    onDefeatNext=function(){ document.getElementById('defeatov').style.display='none'; startRescue(); };
  } else if(battleKind==='survival'){
    var bw=survWave, bonus=bw*50;
    addTotalExp(bonus); lbPost('top', totalExp);
    document.getElementById('defeatsub').textContent='SURVIVED '+bw+' WAVE'+(bw===1?'':'S')+' \u2014 +'+bonus+' EXP POSTED!';
    onDefeatNext=function(){ document.getElementById('defeatov').style.display='none'; startSurvival(); };
  } else {
    document.getElementById('defeatsub').textContent='THE VILLAINS HELD '+TOWNS[currentTown].name.toUpperCase();
    onDefeatNext=respawnArena;
  }
  document.getElementById('defeatov').style.display='flex';
}

/* revive the squad; half=true leaves them at 50% hp */
function reviveSquad(half){
  units.forEach(function(u){
    if(u.side!=='hero') return;
    u.dead=false; u.hp=half?Math.round(u.maxHp/2):u.maxHp;
    u.hpbar.set(u.hp/u.maxHp);
    u.group.scale.setScalar(1);
    u.group.position.y=u.group.userData.baseY;
  });
}
function respawnArena(){
  var t=TOWNS[currentTown];
  var bp=player.group.position;
  clearArena();
  player.group.position.set(t.trig.x, 1.05, t.trig.z+4);
  followers.forEach(function(f){
    for(var k=0;k<FOLLOW_OFF.length;k++) if(FOLLOW_OFF[k].id===f.spec.id){
      f.group.position.set(t.trig.x+FOLLOW_OFF[k].dx, 1.05, t.trig.z+4+FOLLOW_OFF[k].dz);
    }
  });
  startTownBattle(currentTown);
}
function resetRun(){
  liberated=[false,false,false,false,false,false]; saveProgress();
  for(var i=0;i<gates.length;i++){ /* gates rebuilt on reload; simplest: reload page */ }
  location.reload();
}

/* =====================================================================
   MODE HUB + EXP/LEVELS + ENCOUNTERS + PRACTICE + TOURNAMENTS + LEADERBOARDS
   The hub is the standard entry every game shares: new modes register in MODES.
   ===================================================================== */
var gameMode='story', battleKind='town';
var onVictoryNext=null, onDefeatNext=null;
var stepAcc=0, nextEncounter=25, lastPX=0, lastPZ=0;
var totalExp=0, tourneyWins=0;
try{ var te=localStorage.getItem('fb3d_totalexp'); if(te) totalExp=parseInt(te,10)||0;
  var tw=localStorage.getItem('fb3d_tourneywins'); if(tw) tourneyWins=parseInt(tw,10)||0; }catch(e){}
function addTotalExp(n){ totalExp+=n; try{localStorage.setItem('fb3d_totalexp',String(totalExp));}catch(e){} }

function showJoy(show){ document.getElementById('joy').style.display=show?'block':'none'; }

var toastTimer=null;
function showToast(txt, ms){
  var el=document.getElementById('toast');
  el.textContent=txt; el.style.display='block'; el.style.opacity=1;
  if(toastTimer) clearTimeout(toastTimer);
  toastTimer=setTimeout(function(){ el.style.opacity=0;
    setTimeout(function(){ el.style.display='none'; },400); }, ms||2200);
}

/* ---------- EXP / levels ---------- */
function saveLevels(){
  try{
    var o={};
    units.forEach(function(u){ if(u.side==='hero')
      o[u.spec.id]={level:u.level,exp:u.exp,expNext:u.expNext,maxHp:u.maxHp,atk:u.atk}; });
    localStorage.setItem('fb3d_levels', JSON.stringify(o));
  }catch(e){}
}
function loadLevels(){
  try{ var raw=localStorage.getItem('fb3d_levels'); if(!raw) return null;
    return JSON.parse(raw); }catch(e){ return null; }
}
function grantExpAll(amount){
  var ups=[];
  units.forEach(function(u){
    if(u.side!=='hero') return;
    u.exp+=amount;
    while(u.exp>=u.expNext){
      u.exp-=u.expNext; u.level++;
      u.expNext=100*u.level;
      u.maxHp=Math.round(u.maxHp*1.12); u.atk=Math.round(u.atk*1.10);
      u.hp=u.maxHp; u.hpbar.set(1);
      ups.push(u.spec.name+'→Lv'+u.level);
    }
  });
  saveLevels(); updateExpBar();
  addTotalExp(amount*5);
  if(ups.length) showToast('⬆️ LEVEL UP! '+ups.slice(0,2).join(', ')+(ups.length>2?' +'+(ups.length-2)+' more':''));
}
function updateExpBar(){
  var bar=document.getElementById('expbar');
  if(!player){ bar.style.display='none'; return; }
  bar.style.display='block';
  document.getElementById('explabel').textContent=player.spec.name+' Lv'+player.level;
  document.getElementById('expfill').style.width=Math.round(player.exp/player.expNext*100)+'%';
}
function avgSquadLevel(){
  var s=0,n=0;
  units.forEach(function(u){ if(u.side==='hero'){ s+=u.level; n++; } });
  return n?Math.max(1,Math.round(s/n)):1;
}

/* ---------- mode hub ---------- */
var MODES=[
  {id:'splash', icon:'\uD83C\uDF0A', name:'SPLASH', desc:'Two teams. Two pools. Dunk your rivals! Last team standing wins.', start:startSplash}
];
// SPLASH ONLY (2026-10-05, Jeff's order): story/free/practice/tournament/rescue/survival
// removed from the hub. Originals saved in workspace/fire-babys-modes-backup/2026-10-05/.
function renderHub(){
  var wrap=document.getElementById('hubbtns');
  wrap.innerHTML='';
  MODES.forEach(function(m){
    var b=document.createElement('button');
    b.className='modebtn';
    b.innerHTML='<div class="ic">'+m.icon+'</div><div><div class="nm">'+m.name+'</div><div class="ds">'+m.desc+'</div></div>';
    b.addEventListener('click', function(){ beep(600,0.1,'square',0.07); m.start(); });
    wrap.appendChild(b);
  });
  var pb=document.createElement('button');
  pb.className='modebtn';
  var pnm='TRAINEE'; try{ pnm=localStorage.getItem('fb3d_title')||'TRAINEE'; }catch(e){}
  pb.innerHTML='<div class="ic">\uD83D\uDC64</div><div><div class="nm">PLAYER</div><div class="ds">'+
    String(pnm).replace(/[<>&]/g,'').slice(0,14)+' \u2014 tap to edit</div></div>';
  pb.addEventListener('click', function(){ beep(600,0.1,'square',0.07);
    document.getElementById('hubov').style.display='none'; openCreation(); });
  wrap.appendChild(pb);
}
/* SPLASH ONLY (2026-10-05): boots the player straight into a SPLASH match.
   Opens the multiplayer menu with SPLASH preselected, auto-creates a lobby,
   fills it with bots, readies up, and starts. */
function startSplash(){
  try{ hideHub(); }catch(e){}
  // Wait for MP system to be initialized before driving the UI
  var _ssTries = 0;
  var _ssIv = setInterval(function(){
    _ssTries++;
    var _mpOk = false;
    try { _mpOk = !!window._mpInitDone && typeof startMultiplayer === 'function'; } catch(e){}
    if(!_mpOk){
      if(_ssTries > 30){ // 15s timeout: try force-init, then proceed anyway
        clearInterval(_ssIv);
        try { if(typeof mpInit === 'function' && !window._mpInitDone) mpInit(); } catch(e){}
        try { if(typeof startMultiplayer === 'function') startMultiplayer(); } catch(e){}
        // continue with the rest of the flow below via a direct call
        _startSplashContinue();
      }
      return;
    }
    clearInterval(_ssIv);
    _startSplashContinue();
  }, 500);
}
function _startSplashContinue(){
    try{ if(typeof startMultiplayer==='function') startMultiplayer(); }catch(e){}
    try{
      var cbtn = document.querySelector('.mpmodebtn[data-mode="catch"]');
      if(cbtn) cbtn.click();
    }catch(e){}
    setTimeout(function(){
      try{ document.getElementById('mpmenuov').style.display='none'; }catch(e){}
      _cdlClick('#mpcreatebtn');
      var iv2 = setInterval(function(){
        var lov = document.getElementById('mplobbyov');
        if(!(lov && lov.style.display === 'flex')) return;
        clearInterval(iv2);
        try {
          var bf = document.getElementById('mpbotfill');
          if(bf && !bf.checked){ bf.checked = true; bf.dispatchEvent(new Event('change')); }
        } catch(e){}
        _cdlClick('#mpreadybtn');
        var iv3 = setInterval(function(){
          var sb = document.getElementById('mpstartbtn');
          if(sb && sb.style.display !== 'none'){ clearInterval(iv3); sb.click(); }
        }, 1000);
      }, 1000);
    }, 900);
}
function showHub(){
  // SPLASH ONLY (2026-10-05, Jeff's order): the hub is gone. Every path that
  // used to return here now drops the player straight into a SPLASH match.
  state='HUB'; gameMode='hub';
  ['victoryov','defeatov','lbov','hubov'].forEach(function(id){ try{ document.getElementById(id).style.display='none'; }catch(e){} });
  try{ refreshNamePill(); }catch(e){}
  startSplash();
}
/* Deep-link: 3d.html?mode=catch (alias ?mode=splash) — from the homepage PLAY SPLASH button.
   The multiplayer code lives inside an IIFE, so its functions are NOT global.
   The only global entry is window.FBMultiplayer.start(); everything else is
   driven by clicking the real MP menu buttons (mode select, create, ready,
   start) exactly like a player would. */
var _catchDL = false;
function _cdlClick(sel){
  try{
    var el = document.querySelector(sel);
    if(el){ el.click(); return true; }
  }catch(e){}
  return false;
}
function catchDeepLink(){
  var q = null;
  try { q = new URLSearchParams(window.location.search).get('mode'); } catch(e){}
  var ql = q ? q.toLowerCase() : '';
  // SPLASH ONLY (Jeff's order): no ?mode param defaults to SPLASH too.
  // Every entry point goes straight to a SPLASH match.
  if(ql !== 'catch' && ql !== 'splash') ql = 'splash';
  _catchDL = true;
  var t0 = Date.now();
  var iv = setInterval(function(){
    var fb = null;
    try { fb = window.FBMultiplayer; } catch(e){}
    if(!fb || typeof fb.start !== 'function'){
      // timeout after 15s: fall back to normal hub flow
      if(Date.now() - t0 > 15000){
        clearInterval(iv);
        _catchDL = false;
        try { if(typeof showHub === 'function') showHub(); } catch(e){}
      }
      return;
    }
    clearInterval(iv);
    try { if(typeof fb.start === 'function') fb.start(); } catch(e){}
    setTimeout(function(){
      var cbtn = document.querySelector('.mpmodebtn[data-mode="catch"]');
      if(cbtn) cbtn.click();
      setTimeout(function(){
        _cdlClick('#mpcreatebtn');
        var t1 = Date.now();
        var iv2 = setInterval(function(){
          var lov = document.getElementById('mplobbyov');
          var lobbyOpen = lov && lov.style.display === 'flex';
          if(!lobbyOpen){
            return;
          }
          clearInterval(iv2);
          try {
            var bf = document.getElementById('mpbotfill');
            if(bf && !bf.checked){ bf.checked = true; bf.dispatchEvent(new Event('change')); }
          } catch(e){}
          _cdlClick('#mpreadybtn');
          var t2 = Date.now();
          var iv3 = setInterval(function(){
            var sb = document.getElementById('mpstartbtn');
            var vis = !!(sb && sb.style.display !== 'none');
            if(vis){
              clearInterval(iv3);
              sb.click();
              return;
            }
          }, 1000);
        }, 1000);
      }, 900);
    }, 900);
  }, 500);
}
function hideHub(){ document.getElementById('hubov').style.display='none'; }
function modeEnter(){
  hideHub();
  refreshNamePill();
  document.getElementById('topbar').style.display='flex';
  showJoy(true);
  document.getElementById('foepill').style.display='none';
  onVictoryNext=null; onDefeatNext=null;
}

function startStoryMode(){
  gameMode='story'; modeEnter();
  if(!seenIntro){
    state='CINE';
    stageLineupAt(0, 34, [{name:'inferno', cls:'D'}]);
    playCine(DIALOGUE.intro, function(){
      seenIntro=true; saveProgress();
      createSquad(0, 40);
      state='EXPLORE';
      setObjective('➤ WALK FORWARD INTO EMBER VILLAGE');
    });
  } else {
    resetGates();
    applyLiberated();
    createSquad(0, 40);
    state='EXPLORE';
    nextObjective();
  }
}
/* re-lock gates for unliberated towns (free mode opens them all) */
function resetGates(){
  gates.forEach(function(g, gi){
    var shouldLock=!liberated[gi];
    g.locked=shouldLock;
    if(shouldLock && !g.grp.parent) scene.add(g.grp);
    if(!shouldLock && g.grp.parent) scene.remove(g.grp);
  });
}
function startFreeMode(){
  gameMode='free'; modeEnter();
  applyLiberated();
  gates.forEach(function(g){ if(g.locked){ g.locked=false; scene.remove(g.grp); } });
  createSquad(0, 40);
  state='EXPLORE';
  setObjective('🧭 FREE ROAM — wild fire-monsters roam everywhere!');
}
function startPractice(){
  gameMode='practice'; modeEnter();
  createSquad(0, 40);
  state='BATTLE'; battleKind='practice'; battleTime=0;
  arenaCenter.set(0,0,30);
  spawnPracticeTargets();
  document.getElementById('foepill').style.display='block';
  setObjective('🎯 MATCH THE HERO TO THE FIRE CLASS!');
  showVictoryPanelDefaults();
}
function spawnPracticeTargets(){
  var clss=['A','B','C','D'];
  var comp=[];
  for(var i=0;i<4;i++) comp.push(['CLASS '+clss[i]+' DUMMY', clss[i], 70, 0]);
  spawnFoes(comp, 0, 30);
  units.forEach(function(u){ if(u.side==='foe'){ u.speed=0; } });
}
function showVictoryPanelDefaults(){ /* labels set per victory */ }

/* ---------- tournaments ---------- */
var tourn={round:0};
var RIVAL_PALETTES=[
  {body:'#7a4ad6', dark:'#2a1a55', core:'#b79aff'},
  {body:'#2ad6c6', dark:'#0a4440', core:'#a9fff5'},
  {body:'#d64a8a', dark:'#551a35', core:'#ffb3d6'}
];
function startTournament(){
  gameMode='tournament'; tourn.round=0; modeEnter();
  createSquad(0, 40);
  tournamentRound();
}
function tournamentRound(){
  tourn.round++;
  battleKind='tournament'; state='BATTLE'; battleTime=0;
  arenaCenter.set(0,0,30);
  var names=['QUARTERFINAL','SEMIFINAL','FINAL'];
  reviveSquad(false); // full heal between rounds
  townBanner('TOURNAMENT', names[tourn.round-1], 1600, function(){
    spawnRivals(tourn.round);
    document.getElementById('foepill').style.display='block';
    setObjective('🏆 WIN ROUND '+tourn.round+'/3!');
  });
}
function spawnRivals(round){
  var n=2+round, pal=RIVAL_PALETTES[(round-1)%3];
  for(var i=0;i<n;i++){
    var boss=(round===3&&i===0);
    var spec={body:pal.body, dark:pal.dark, core:pal.core};
    var g=makeBaby(spec,'villain', boss?1.35:1.0);
    var R=g.userData.R;
    var a=(i/n)*Math.PI*2+0.4, rr=8;
    g.position.set(arenaCenter.x+Math.cos(a)*rr, R*1.55, arenaCenter.z+Math.sin(a)*rr*0.7);
    g.userData.baseY=R*1.55;
    var tag=nameTag(boss?'RIVAL CHAMP':'RIVAL CUB', pal.core); tag.position.y=R*2.6+0.6; g.add(tag);
    var hp=makeHpBar(boss?3.2:2.0); hp.position.y=R*2.1+0.9; g.add(hp); hp.set(1);
    scene.add(g);
    var clss=['A','B','C','D'];
    units.push({ group:g, side:'foe', name:boss?'RIVAL CHAMP':'RIVAL CUB', cls:clss[i%4],
      hp:boss?230:70+round*25, maxHp:boss?230:70+round*25, atk:boss?18:8+round*3,
      speed:4.2, range:2.4, cd:Math.random()*0.8, hpbar:hp, state:'seek', dead:false,
      lunge:0, lungeDir:null, boss:boss, spec:{id:'rival'} });
  }
  updateFoeCount();
}
function tournamentRoundWin(){
  var exp=30*tourn.round;
  grantExpAll(exp); addTotalExp(exp*5);
  if(tourn.round<3){
    showToast('🏆 ROUND '+tourn.round+' WON! +'+exp+' EXP');
    setTimeout(function(){ clearArena(); tournamentRound(); }, 1600);
  } else {
    tourneyWins++;
    try{localStorage.setItem('fb3d_tourneywins',String(tourneyWins));}catch(e){}
    lbPost('champions', tourneyWins);
    showVictoryPanel('🏆', 'TOURNAMENT CHAMPION', '“Undefeated. The bracket is OURS.” — RUKO',
      'Trophies: <b>'+tourneyWins+'</b>', 'HUB 🏠', showHub);
  }
}

/* ---------- rescue mode: save 5 ember-pups in 90 seconds ---------- */
var rescues=[], rescueFires=[], rescueTime=0, rescueSaved=0, rescueLastSec=-1;
function clearRescueProps(){
  for(var i=0;i<rescues.length;i++) scene.remove(rescues[i].group);
  for(var f=0;f<rescueFires.length;f++) scene.remove(rescueFires[f].mesh);
  rescues=[]; rescueFires=[];
}
function startRescue(){
  gameMode='rescue'; modeEnter();
  createSquad(0, 40);
  state='BATTLE'; battleKind='rescue'; battleTime=0;
  arenaCenter.set(0,0,30);
  hideStronghold(); strongHP=0;
  clearRescueProps();
  rescueSaved=0; rescueTime=90; rescueLastSec=-1;
  for(var k=0;k<5;k++){
    var g=makeBaby({body:'#ffd76a', dark:'#a35a00', core:'#fff7c0'}, 'eager', 0.55);
    var a=(k/5)*Math.PI*2+0.3, rr=10+Math.random()*8;
    g.position.set(arenaCenter.x+Math.cos(a)*rr, 0.85, arenaCenter.z+Math.sin(a)*rr*0.7);
    g.userData.baseY=0.85;
    var tag=nameTag('HELP!', '#ffd76a'); tag.position.y=1.7; g.add(tag);
    scene.add(g);
    rescues.push({group:g, tag:tag, saved:false});
  }
  for(var q=0;q<4;q++){
    var fa=(q/4)*Math.PI*2+0.9, fr=13+Math.random()*6;
    var fx=arenaCenter.x+Math.cos(fa)*fr, fz=arenaCenter.z+Math.sin(fa)*fr*0.7;
    var fm=new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.CircleGeometry(2.2, 20),
      new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({color:0xff5a1e, transparent:true, opacity:0.55, depthWrite:false}));
    fm.rotation.x=-Math.PI/2; fm.position.set(fx, 0.06, fz);
    scene.add(fm);
    rescueFires.push({mesh:fm, x:fx, z:fz, r:2.2});
  }
  document.getElementById('foepill').style.display='none';
  setObjective('\uD83D\uDEA8 RESCUE 0/5 PUPS \u2014 90s!');
  townBanner('RESCUE MODE', 'SAVE THE EMBER-PUPS!', 1600);
}
function updateRescue(dt, t){
  battleTime+=dt;
  rescueTime-=dt;
  var sec=Math.ceil(Math.max(0,rescueTime));
  if(sec!==rescueLastSec){ rescueLastSec=sec; setObjective('\uD83D\uDEA8 RESCUE '+rescueSaved+'/5 PUPS \u2014 '+sec+'s!'); }
  if(rescueTime<=0){ defeat(); return; }
  var bp=player.group.position;
  var mv=moveInput(), sp=7.5;
  if(mv.on){
    bp.x=Math.max(arenaCenter.x-26, Math.min(arenaCenter.x+26, bp.x+mv.x*sp*dt));
    bp.z=Math.max(arenaCenter.z-26, Math.min(arenaCenter.z+26, bp.z+mv.z*sp*dt));
    player.group.rotation.y=Math.atan2(mv.x, mv.z);
  }
  updateBaby(player.group, t, dt);
  followers.forEach(function(f){
    var off=null;
    for(var k=0;k<FOLLOW_OFF.length;k++) if(FOLLOW_OFF[k].id===f.spec.id) off=FOLLOW_OFF[k];
    var tx=bp.x+off.dx, tz=bp.z+off.dz, fp=f.group.position;
    var dx=tx-fp.x, dz=tz-fp.z, d=Math.hypot(dx,dz);
    if(d>0.7){ var fs=Math.min(f.speed, d*3.2)*dt; fp.x+=dx/d*fs; fp.z+=dz/d*fs; f.group.rotation.y=Math.atan2(dx,dz); }
    updateBaby(f.group, t, dt);
    f.hpbar.quaternion.copy(camera.quaternion);
  });
  player.hpbar.quaternion.copy(camera.quaternion);
  var i, dx2, dz2;
  for(i=0;i<rescues.length;i++){
    var r=rescues[i];
    if(r.saved) continue;
    updateBaby(r.group, t, dt);
    dx2=bp.x-r.group.position.x; dz2=bp.z-r.group.position.z;
    if(dx2*dx2+dz2*dz2 < 2.2*2.2){
      r.saved=true; rescueSaved++;
      r.group.remove(r.tag);
      spawnBurst(r.group.position.clone().add(new THREE.Vector3(0,1,0)), 0xffd76a, 16, 6);
      beep(880,0.15,'square',0.08);
      showToast('\uD83D\uDEA8 PUP RESCUED! '+rescueSaved+'/5', 1200);
      if(rescueSaved>=5){ victory(); return; }
    }
  }
  for(var q=0;q<rescueFires.length;q++){
    var fp2=rescueFires[q];
    fp2.r=Math.min(5.5, fp2.r+dt*0.045);
    fp2.mesh.scale.setScalar(fp2.r/2.2);
    if(Math.random()<dt*6) spawnBurst(new THREE.Vector3(fp2.x+(Math.random()-0.5)*fp2.r, 0.6, fp2.z+(Math.random()-0.5)*fp2.r), 0xff6a1e, 2, 2);
    for(var h=0;h<units.length;h++){
      var u=units[h];
      if(u.side!=='hero'||u.dead) continue;
      var hx=u.group.position.x-fp2.x, hz=u.group.position.z-fp2.z;
      if(hx*hx+hz*hz < fp2.r*fp2.r){
        u.hp-=10*dt; u.hpbar.set(Math.max(0,u.hp/u.maxHp));
        u.group.userData.flash=0.6;
        if(u.hp<=0 && !u.dead){
          u.dead=true; u.state='dying'; u.deathT=0;
          fbExtinguishPoof(u.group);
          spawnBurst(u.group.position.clone().add(new THREE.Vector3(0,1,0)), 0xff8c2e, 16, 6);
        }
      }
    }
  }
  if(heroesAlive()===0){ defeat(); return; }
  var dist=21*camDistMul, hh=13.5*camDistMul;
  camera.position.set(arenaCenter.x, hh, arenaCenter.z+dist);
  camera.lookAt(arenaCenter.x, 1, arenaCenter.z-2);
}

/* ---------- survival mode: endless escalating waves ---------- */
var survWave=0, survBetween=false;
function startSurvival(){
  gameMode='survival'; modeEnter();
  createSquad(0, 40);
  state='BATTLE'; battleKind='survival'; battleTime=0;
  arenaCenter.set(0,0,30);
  hideStronghold(); strongHP=0;
  survWave=0;
  document.getElementById('foepill').style.display='block';
  setObjective('\uD83D\uDC80 SURVIVE THE WAVES!');
  townBanner('SURVIVAL MODE', 'HOW LONG CAN YOU LAST?', 1600, function(){ spawnSurvWave(); });
}
function spawnSurvWave(){
  if(state!=='BATTLE'||battleKind!=='survival') return;
  survBetween=false;
  survWave++;
  var VN=[['Twiggy','A'],['Slick','B'],['Buzz','C'],['Slag','D']], comp=[];
  var n=2+Math.min(6, survWave);
  for(var i=0;i<n;i++){
    var v=VN[i%4];
    comp.push([v[0], v[1], Math.round((60+survWave*22)*(1+i*0.12)), Math.round(7+survWave*2.2)]);
  }
  spawnFoes(comp, arenaCenter.x, arenaCenter.z);
  setObjective('\uD83D\uDC80 WAVE '+survWave+' \u2014 '+n+' FOES!');
  updateFoeCount();
}

  document.getElementById('vsub').textContent=sub;
  document.getElementById('vquote').textContent=quote;
  document.getElementById('vstatline').innerHTML=statline;
  document.getElementById('againbtn').textContent=btnLabel;
  onVictoryNext=nextFn;
  onDefeatNext=null;
  document.getElementById('victoryov').style.display='flex';
  document.getElementById('joy').style.display='none';
}

function playerTitle(){
  try{
    var t=localStorage.getItem('fb3d_title');
    if(t) return t;
    t='Blaze Trainee #'+(1000+Math.floor(Math.random()*9000));
    localStorage.setItem('fb3d_title', t); return t;
  }catch(e){ return 'Blaze Trainee #0000'; }
}

/* ---------------- player creation (name + flame color) ---------------- */
var PLAYER_COLORS=['#ffc93c','#ff8c2e','#e8352c','#3ca9ff','#2ad6c6','#b79aff'];
var selColor=null;
function blazeSpec(){ for(var i=0;i<HEROES.length;i++) if(HEROES[i].id==='blaze') return HEROES[i]; return null; }
function needsCreation(){
  try{
    if(localStorage.getItem('fb3d_created')) return false;
    var t=localStorage.getItem('fb3d_title');
    if(!t) return true;
    return /^Blaze Trainee #\d+$/.test(t); // auto title, never customized
  }catch(e){ return true; }
}
function cleanName(n){ return String(n||'').replace(/[<>&"']/g,'').trim().slice(0,14); }
function applyPlayerColor(){
  var c=null; try{ c=localStorage.getItem('fb3d_color'); }catch(e){}
  if(!c) return;
  var bs=blazeSpec(); if(!bs) return;
  bs.body=c; bs.dark=shade(c,0.55);
  if(typeof player!=='undefined' && player && player.group){
    var u=player.group.userData;
    u.body.material.map=bodyTexture(bs,'eager'); u.body.material.needsUpdate=true;
    var am=limbMat(c), dm=limbMat(bs.dark);
    if(u.armL){ u.armL.material=am; u.armR.material=am; u.legL.material=dm; u.legR.material=dm; }
  }
}
function openCreation(){
  var cur=''; try{ cur=localStorage.getItem('fb3d_title')||''; }catch(e){}
  if(/^Blaze Trainee #\d+$/.test(cur)) cur='';
  document.getElementById('pname').value=cur;
  selColor=null; try{ selColor=localStorage.getItem('fb3d_color'); }catch(e){}
  var w=document.getElementById('pcswatches'); w.innerHTML='';
  PLAYER_COLORS.forEach(function(c){
    var d=document.createElement('div');
    d.className='pcsw'+(c===selColor?' sel':'');
    d.style.background=c;
    (function(col, el){
      el.addEventListener('click', function(){
        selColor=col; beep(700,0.08,'square',0.06);
        for(var i=0;i<w.children.length;i++) w.children[i].classList.remove('sel');
        el.classList.add('sel');
      });
    })(c, d);
    w.appendChild(d);
  });
  if(!selColor){ selColor=PLAYER_COLORS[0]; w.children[0].classList.add('sel'); }
  document.getElementById('createov').style.display='flex';
}
function saveCreation(){
  var nm=cleanName(document.getElementById('pname').value);
  try{
    if(!nm) nm='Blaze Trainee #'+(1000+Math.floor(Math.random()*9000));
    localStorage.setItem('fb3d_title', nm);
    localStorage.setItem('fb3d_color', selColor||PLAYER_COLORS[0]);
    localStorage.setItem('fb3d_created','1');
  }catch(e){}
  applyPlayerColor();
  refreshNamePill();
  document.getElementById('createov').style.display='none';
  beep(880,0.15,'square',0.08);
  showHub();
}
function refreshNamePill(){
  var el=document.getElementById('pnamehud');
  if(!el) return;
  var t='TRAINEE';
  try{ t=localStorage.getItem('fb3d_title')||playerTitle(); }catch(e){}
  el.textContent=String(t).slice(0,14).toUpperCase();
}
function lbHash(s){
  var h=5381;
  for(var i=0;i<s.length;i++){ h=(((h<<5)+h) ^ s.charCodeAt(i))>>>0; }
  return ('0000000'+h.toString(16)).slice(-8);
}
function lbNote(live){
  document.getElementById('lbnote').textContent=live?'🌐 LIVE LEADERBOARD':'📴 LOCAL SCORES — no name, no data leaves your device';
}
function renderLB(type){
  document.getElementById('tab_top').classList.toggle('on', type==='top');
  document.getElementById('tab_champ').classList.toggle('on', type==='champions');
  var me={title:playerTitle(), score:type==='top'?totalExp:tourneyWins, me:true};
  function draw(rows, live){
    rows=rows.slice().sort(function(a,b){return b.score-a.score;}).slice(0,20);
    var el=document.getElementById('lblist'); el.innerHTML='';
    rows.forEach(function(r,i){
      var d=document.createElement('div');
      d.className='lbrow'+(r.me?' me':'');
      var medal=i===0?'🥇':i===1?'🥈':i===2?'🥉':(i+1)+'.';
      d.innerHTML='<span class="rk">'+medal+'</span><span>'+String(r.title).replace(/[<>&]/g,'')+'</span><span class="sc">'+r.score+'</span>';
      el.appendChild(d);
    });
    lbNote(live);
  }
  var bots=(type==='top'?LB_BOTS_TOP:LB_BOTS_CHAMP).map(function(b){return {title:b.title,score:b.score};});
  bots.push(me);
  if(LEADERBOARD_API_URL && navigator.onLine){
    lbNote(false);
    document.getElementById('lbnote').textContent='⏳ CONNECTING…';
    fetch(LEADERBOARD_API_URL+'/board?type='+type, {method:'GET'})
      .then(function(r){ return r.json(); })
      .then(function(d){
        var rows=(d&&d.rows)||[];
        var hasMe=rows.some(function(r){ return r.title===me.title; });
        draw(hasMe?rows:rows.concat([me]), true);
      })
      .catch(function(){ draw(bots, false); });
  } else draw(bots, false);
}
function lbPost(type, score){
  if(!LEADERBOARD_API_URL || !navigator.onLine) return;
  try{
    var ts=Date.now(), title=playerTitle();
    var body={title:title, score:score, type:type, ts:ts,
      hash:lbHash(score+'|'+title+'|'+type+'|'+ts+'|'+LB_SALT)};
    fetch(LEADERBOARD_API_URL+'/score', {method:'POST',
      headers:{'Content-Type':'application/json'}, body:JSON.stringify(body)})
      .catch(function(){});
  }catch(e){}
}
function showLeaderboards(){
  document.getElementById('hubov').style.display='none';
  renderLB('top');
  document.getElementById('lbov').style.display='flex';
}

/* ---------------- tiny audio ---------------- */
var AC=null;
var fbMuted=false; try{ fbMuted=localStorage.getItem('fb_muted')==='1'; }catch(e){}
function beep(freq, dur, type, vol){
  try{
    if(fbMuted) return;
    if(!AC) AC=new (window.AudioContext||window.webkitAudioContext)();
    var o=AC.createOscillator(), g=AC.createGain();
    o.type=type||'square'; o.frequency.value=freq;
    g.gain.setValueAtTime(vol||0.08, AC.currentTime);
    g.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.0001, AC.currentTime+dur);
    o.connect(g); g.connect(AC.destination);
    o.start(); o.stop(AC.currentTime+dur);
  }catch(e){}
}

/* ---------------- init + main loop ---------------- */
var titleCast=[];
function init(){
  applyPlayerColor();
  var canvas=document.getElementById('game');
  renderer=new THREE.WebGLRenderer({ canvas:canvas, antialias:FBQ.antialias, powerPreference:'high-performance' });
  renderer.setPixelRatio(FBQ.pixelRatio);
  renderer.setSize(innerWidth, innerHeight);
  if('outputColorSpace' in renderer && THREE.SRGBColorSpace) renderer.outputColorSpace=THREE.SRGBColorSpace;
  // graphics glow-up: real-time soft shadows + filmic tone mapping
  renderer.shadowMap.enabled=FBQ.shadows;
  renderer.shadowMap.type=FBQ.shadowSoft?THREE.PCFSoftShadowMap:THREE.PCFShadowMap;
  if(THREE.ACESFilmicToneMapping!==undefined){
    renderer.toneMapping=THREE.ACESFilmicToneMapping;
    renderer.toneMappingExposure=1.25;
  }

  scene=new THREE.Scene();
  scene.fog=new THREE.Fog(0x7a4a2a, 38, 135);
  camera=new THREE.PerspectiveCamera(52, innerWidth/innerHeight, 0.1, 400);
  camera.position.set(0,10.5,53); camera.lookAt(0,1.5,34);

  scene.add(new THREE.HemisphereLight(0xffd9a8, 0x6a4028, 1.12));
  var dir=new THREE.DirectionalLight(0xffe0b0, 1.25); dir.position.set(-14,26,52);
  dir.castShadow=true;
  dir.shadow.mapSize.set(FBQ.shadowSize,FBQ.shadowSize);
  dir.shadow.camera.near=4; dir.shadow.camera.far=80;
  dir.shadow.camera.left=-30; dir.shadow.camera.right=30;
  dir.shadow.camera.top=30; dir.shadow.camera.bottom=-30;
  dir.shadow.bias=-0.0004; dir.shadow.normalBias=0.5;
  scene.add(dir); scene.add(dir.target); window.__sun=dir;
  var fill=new THREE.DirectionalLight(0x7aa8ff, 0.45); fill.position.set(12,16,-14); scene.add(fill);
  // premium pass: warm rim/back light so characters separate from the scenery
  var rim=new THREE.DirectionalLight(0xff8a40, 0.55); rim.position.set(18,10,-34); scene.add(rim);

  FLAME_TEX=flameTexture(); GLOW_TEX=glowTexture();
  buildOpenWorld();
  buildSceneryFX();

  // title backdrop: the crew at the start pad
  var xs=[-6,-3,0,3,6];
  for(var i=0;i<HEROES.length;i++){
    var g=makeBaby(HEROES[i], HEROES[i].face, 1);
    g.position.set(xs[i],1.55,40); g.userData.baseY=1.55;
    g.rotation.y=Math.PI;
    var tag=nameTag(HEROES[i].name,'#ffd76a'); tag.position.y=2.9; g.add(tag);
    scene.add(g); titleCast.push(g);
  }
  // premium pass: warm pool of stage light under the title crew
  var stageGlow=new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.PlaneGeometry(30,13),
    new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({ map:GLOW_TEX, transparent:true, blending:THREE.AdditiveBlending, depthWrite:false, opacity:0.5 }));
  stageGlow.rotation.x=-Math.PI/2; stageGlow.position.set(0,0.07,40); scene.add(stageGlow);

  bindInput();
  clock=new THREE.Clock();
  animate();
}

var fpsAcc=0, fpsN=0, fpsT=0, titleA=0;
var __fbLastPX = null, __fbLastPZ = null, __fbTimeAcc = 0;
function animate(){
  requestAnimationFrame(animate);
  var dt=Math.min(clock.getDelta(), 0.05), t=clock.elapsedTime;

  // Task tracking: distance + time
  try {
    if (typeof fbTaskEvent === 'function' && typeof player !== 'undefined' && player && player.group && mpInGame) {
      var px = player.group.position.x, pz = player.group.position.z;
      if (__fbLastPX !== null) {
        var dx = px - __fbLastPX, dz = pz - __fbLastPZ;
        var dist = Math.sqrt(dx*dx + dz*dz);
        if (dist > 0.01) fbTaskEvent('distance', dist);
      }
      __fbLastPX = px; __fbLastPZ = pz;
      __fbTimeAcc += dt;
      if (__fbTimeAcc >= 60) { fbTaskEvent('time_min', 1); if (typeof __fbSeasonXP === 'function') __fbSeasonXP(10, 'time'); __fbTimeAcc = 0; }
    }
  } catch(e){}

  for(var i=0;i<torches.length;i++){
    var tc=torches[i], s=0.85+0.3*Math.sin(t*10+tc.phase);
    tc.fl.scale.set(1.5*s,2.2*(0.9+0.25*Math.sin(t*13+tc.phase)),1);
    tc.gl.material.opacity=0.55+0.2*Math.sin(t*7+tc.phase);
  }
  if(window.__voltOrbs) for(var v2=0;v2<window.__voltOrbs.length;v2++){
    var vo=window.__voltOrbs[v2];
    if(vo.arc){
      vo.m.material.opacity=0.3+0.6*Math.abs(Math.sin(t*7+vo.ph));
      if(Math.random()<dt*6){
        var pts=vo.m.geometry.attributes.position;
        for(var p3=1;p3<6;p3++){ pts.setX(p3, vo.bx+(Math.random()-0.5)*1.4); pts.setZ(p3, vo.bz+(Math.random()-0.5)*1.4); }
        pts.needsUpdate=true;
      }
    } else vo.m.scale.setScalar(1+0.15*Math.sin(t*4+vo.ph));
  }
  if(window.__banners) for(var b2=0;b2<window.__banners.length;b2++){
    window.__banners[b2].m.rotation.y=Math.sin(t*1.2+window.__banners[b2].ph)*0.18;
  }
  if(window.__smog) for(var s4=0;s4<window.__smog.length;s4++){
    var sm2=window.__smog[s4];
    sm2.s.position.x+=sm2.vx*dt;
    if(sm2.s.position.x>22) sm2.s.position.x=-22;
  }
  if(window.__lava) window.__lava.material.color.setHSL(0.045+0.012*Math.sin(t*2.2), 1, 0.5+0.06*Math.sin(t*3.1));
  if(window.__river) window.__river.material.color.setHSL(0.06+0.01*Math.sin(t*2.6), 1, 0.5);

  if(cine){
    updateCine(t, dt);
  } else if(window.__fbPausedShared === true && (function(){ var pm = document.getElementById('pausemenu'); return pm && pm.style.display === 'flex'; })()){
    /* PAUSED (gamer-check fix 2026-10-12): freeze every gameplay state.
       Before this, the pause flag only opened the menu — the single-player
       world kept running behind it. Ambience above still animates. */
  } else if(state==='TITLE'){
    titleA+=dt*0.18;
    camera.position.set(Math.sin(titleA)*16, 8, 40+Math.cos(titleA)*16);
    camera.lookAt(0,1.5,40);
    for(var k=0;k<titleCast.length;k++) updateBaby(titleCast[k], t, dt);
  } else if(state==='EXPLORE'){
    updateExplore(dt, t);
  } else if(state==='BATTLE'){
    if(battleKind==='rescue') updateRescue(dt, t); else updateBattle(dt, t);
  } else if(state==='VICTORY'||state==='DEFEAT'){
    for(var u2=0;u2<units.length;u2++) if(!units[u2].dead) updateBaby(units[u2].group, t, dt);
  }

  updateParticles(dt);
  updateDmg(dt);
  // sun shadow rig follows the player so shadows stay crisp everywhere
  if(window.__sun){
    var sp=(typeof player!=='undefined'&&player&&player.group)?player.group.position:null;
    var sx=sp?sp.x:0, sz=sp?sp.z:40;
    window.__sun.position.set(sx-14,26,sz+12);
    window.__sun.target.position.set(sx,0,sz);
    window.__sun.target.updateMatrixWorld();
  }
  if(typeof FBF!=='undefined') FBF.update(dt);
  updateEmbers(dt);
  renderer.render(scene, camera);

  fpsAcc+=dt; fpsN++; fpsT+=dt;
  if(fpsT>0.5){
    var _fps = Math.round(fpsN/fpsAcc);
    document.getElementById('fps').textContent=_fps;
    // auto quality: if FPS stays low, drop to low quality for smoothness
    try{
      if(typeof FBQ!=='undefined' && FBQ.level!=='low'){
        window._fbLowFpsN = (_fps < 25) ? (window._fbLowFpsN||0)+1 : 0;
        if(window._fbLowFpsN >= 6){
          window._fbLowFpsN = 0;
          FBQ.level='low'; FBQ.pixelRatio=1; FBQ.shadows=false;
          try{ renderer.setPixelRatio(1); }catch(e){}
          try{ renderer.shadowMap.enabled=false; }catch(e){}
          try{ localStorage.setItem('fb_quality','low'); }catch(e){}
        }
      }
    }catch(e){}
    fpsAcc=0;fpsN=0;fpsT=0;
  }
}
</script>

<script>
/* =====================================================================
   MULTIPLAYER INTEGRATION — Fire Babys 3D
   Uses FireBabysNet client library + Cloudflare Workers backend.
   Single-player modes are untouched; multiplayer is an additional hub option.
   ===================================================================== */
(function(){
"use strict";

var MP_SERVER = "wss://fire-babys-multiplayer.jeffpruden288.workers.dev";
var net = null;              // FireBabysNet instance
var mpMode = "catch";        // SPLASH ONLY (2026-10-05): all other modes removed
var mpReady = false;         // local ready state
var mpInGame = false;        // true when multiplayer game is running
var remotePlayers = {};      // playerId -> { group, name, team, targetX, targetZ, targetDir }
var mpScores = { team0: 0, team1: 0 };  // versus scoring: fires extinguished per team
var mpMyTeam = 0;
var mpGameMode = "coop";     // actual mode of running game

/* ---------- combat state (versus + co-op fun) ---------- */
var mpHP = 5, mpMaxHP = 5;   // local player health
var mpBlocking = false;      // local block stance
var mpBlockKey = false;      // K key held
var mpAtkCd = 0;             // attack cooldown timer (s)
var mpAtkAnimT = 0;          // local attack scale-punch timer (s)
var mpKO = false;            // local knocked-out flag
var mpKOTimer = 0;           // respawn countdown (s)
var mpKOs = { team0: 0, team1: 0 };  // KO counts per team
var mpShield = null;         // local shield bubble mesh

/* ---------- bronze knight armor (2D stage rewards) ----------
   Pieces earned in the 2D game (stage 5/10/15/20). Server-authoritative
   via the auth worker; falls back to local 2D save until server is live.
   Full set = +10% damage dealt, -10% damage taken, gold glow. */
var __FB_ARMOR_URL = 'https://fire-babys-auth.jeffpruden288.workers.dev';
var __FB_ARMOR = { greaves: false, gauntlets: false, helmet: false, chestplate: false };
var __FB_ARMOR_BOOST = 0;    // 0..0.10
var __FB_ARMOR_FULL = false;
var __FB_STATS = { kills: 0, deaths: 0 };  // session deltas, reported to server

function __fbArmorBoost(a){
  var n = 0;
  if(a.greaves) n++; if(a.gauntlets) n++; if(a.helmet) n++; if(a.chestplate) n++;
  return n * 0.025;
}
function __fbLocalArmor(){
  try {
    var raw = localStorage.getItem('fb_bronze_armor_v1');
    if(raw){ var p = JSON.parse(raw); if(p && p.pieces) return p.pieces; }
  } catch(e){}
  return { greaves: false, gauntlets: false, helmet: false, chestplate: false };
}
function __fbApplyArmor(a){
  __FB_ARMOR = {
    greaves: !!a.greaves, gauntlets: !!a.gauntlets,
    helmet: !!a.helmet, chestplate: !!a.chestplate,
  };
  __FB_ARMOR_BOOST = __fbArmorBoost(__FB_ARMOR);
  __FB_ARMOR_FULL = __FB_ARMOR.chestplate && __FB_ARMOR.helmet && __FB_ARMOR.gauntlets && __FB_ARMOR.greaves;
  try {
    if(player && player.group) addBronzeArmor(player.group, 1, __FB_ARMOR);
  } catch(e){}
  // Broadcast so opponents see the armor (and fear it).
  try {
    if(typeof net !== 'undefined' && net && net.connected && typeof mpInGame !== 'undefined' && mpInGame){
      net.sendAction({ kind: 'armor', data: {
        g: __FB_ARMOR.greaves ? 1 : 0, ga: __FB_ARMOR.gauntlets ? 1 : 0,
        h: __FB_ARMOR.helmet ? 1 : 0, c: __FB_ARMOR.chestplate ? 1 : 0 } });
    }
  } catch(e){}
  // full bronze set unlocks the Soot-Crusher Power Fists
  try { if (typeof __fbCheckWeaponUnlocks === 'function') __fbCheckWeaponUnlocks(); } catch(e){}
}
function __fbFetchArmor(){
  var t = null;
  try { if(window.FireBabysAuth && window.FireBabysAuth.getToken) t = window.FireBabysAuth.getToken(); } catch(e){}
  if(!t){ __fbApplyArmor(__fbLocalArmor()); return; }
  fetch(__FB_ARMOR_URL + '/profile/get', {
    method: 'POST', headers: { 'content-type': 'application/json' },
    body: JSON.stringify({ token: t }),
  }).then(function(r){ return r.json(); }).then(function(j){
    if(j && j.ok && j.profile && j.profile.armor) __fbApplyArmor(j.profile.armor);
    else __fbApplyArmor(__fbLocalArmor());
  }).catch(function(){ __fbApplyArmor(__fbLocalArmor()); });
}
function __fbReportStats(){
  var t = null;
  try { if(window.FireBabysAuth && window.FireBabysAuth.getToken) t = window.FireBabysAuth.getToken(); } catch(e){}
  if(!t) return;
  var d = { kills: __FB_STATS.kills, deaths: __FB_STATS.deaths, wins: 0, losses: 0 };
  if(!d.kills && !d.deaths) return;
  __FB_STATS.kills = 0; __FB_STATS.deaths = 0;
  fetch(__FB_ARMOR_URL + '/profile/stats', {
    method: 'POST', headers: { 'content-type': 'application/json' },
    body: JSON.stringify({ token: t, delta: d }),
  }).catch(function(){});
}

/* ----- armor meshes: flat gray pieces, gold glow on full set ----- */
function addBronzeArmor(g, R, armor){
  if(!g || !armor || typeof THREE === 'undefined') return;
  try {
    if(g.userData.bronzeArmor){
      g.userData.bronzeArmor.forEach(function(m){ g.remove(m); });
    }
    g.userData.bronzeArmor = null;
    g.userData.bronzeGlow = null;
    var parts = [];
    var gray = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: 0x8a8a8a, roughness: 0.55, metalness: 0.45 });
    var full = armor.chestplate && armor.helmet && armor.gauntlets && armor.greaves;
    var chestMat = full
      ? new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: 0xc09a4a, roughness: 0.35, metalness: 0.7, emissive: 0xffd76a, emissiveIntensity: 0.35 })
      : gray;
    if(armor.greaves){
      [-1, 1].forEach(function(s){
        var gr = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.CylinderGeometry(R*0.26, R*0.30, R*0.55, 10), gray);
        gr.position.set(s*R*0.38, -R*0.95, 0); gr.castShadow = true;
        g.add(gr); parts.push(gr);
      });
    }
    if(armor.gauntlets){
      [-1, 1].forEach(function(s){
        var ga = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.CylinderGeometry(R*0.20, R*0.23, R*0.45, 10), gray);
        ga.position.set(s*R*1.02, -R*0.15, 0); ga.rotation.z = s * -0.12; ga.castShadow = true;
        g.add(ga); parts.push(ga);
      });
    }
    if(armor.helmet){
      var helm = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.SphereGeometry(R*0.92, 18, 12, 0, Math.PI*2, 0, Math.PI*0.55), gray);
      helm.position.y = R*0.55; helm.castShadow = true;
      g.add(helm); parts.push(helm);
      var brim = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.TorusGeometry(R*0.88, R*0.07, 8, 20), gray);
      brim.rotation.x = Math.PI/2; brim.position.y = R*0.62;
      g.add(brim); parts.push(brim);
    }
    if(armor.chestplate){
      var chest = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.SphereGeometry(R*1.04, 18, 12, 0, Math.PI*2, 0, Math.PI*0.62), chestMat);
      chest.position.y = R*0.02; chest.castShadow = true;
      g.add(chest); parts.push(chest);
      var dia = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.OctahedronGeometry(R*0.22),
        new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: 0xbfefff, roughness: 0.05, metalness: 0.1,
          emissive: 0x88ccff, emissiveIntensity: full ? 0.9 : 0.35 }));
      dia.position.set(0, R*0.12, R*0.98); dia.rotation.y = Math.PI/4;
      g.add(dia); parts.push(dia);
    }
    if(full && typeof GLOW_TEX !== 'undefined' && GLOW_TEX){
      var glow = new THREE.Sprite(new THREE.SpriteMaterial({ map: GLOW_TEX, color: 0xffd76a,
        blending: THREE.AdditiveBlending, depthWrite: false, transparent: true, opacity: 0.35 }));
      glow.scale.set(R*3.6, R*3.6, 1);
      g.add(glow); parts.push(glow);
      g.userData.bronzeGlow = glow;
    }
    g.userData.bronzeArmor = parts;
  } catch(e){}
}

/* ----- leaderboard overlay ----- */
function __fbShowLeaderboard(){
  try {
    var old = document.getElementById('fb-lb-ov');
    if(old){ old.parentNode.removeChild(old); return; }
    var ov = document.createElement('div');
    ov.id = 'fb-lb-ov';
    ov.style.cssText = 'position:fixed;inset:0;z-index:99990;display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:center;background:rgba(8,4,2,0.85);padding:20px;box-sizing:border-box;';
    ov.innerHTML = '<div style="width:100%;max-width:440px;max-height:80vh;overflow-y:auto;background:#1c1006;border:3px solid #ffd76a;border-radius:20px;padding:24px;">' +
      '<div style="color:#ffd76a;font-size:22px;font-weight:900;letter-spacing:2px;text-align:center;">🏆 LEADERBOARD</div>' +
      '<div id="fb-lb-rows" style="color:#ffe9d6;font-size:15px;margin:16px 0;text-align:center;">Loading...</div>' +
      '<button id="fb-lb-close" style="display:block;margin:0 auto;padding:12px 30px;font-size:16px;font-weight:900;border:none;border-radius:12px;cursor:pointer;color:#2a1206;background:linear-gradient(180deg,#ffd76a,#ff8c2e);">CLOSE</button></div>';
    ov.querySelector('#fb-lb-close').addEventListener('click', function(){ ov.parentNode.removeChild(ov); });
    ov.addEventListener('click', function(e){ if(e.target === ov) ov.parentNode.removeChild(ov); });
    document.body.appendChild(ov);
    fetch(__FB_ARMOR_URL + '/leaderboard?limit=25').then(function(r){ return r.json(); }).then(function(j){
      var el = document.getElementById('fb-lb-rows');
      if(!el) return;
      if(!j || !j.ok || !j.leaders || !j.leaders.length){ el.textContent = 'No legends yet. Be the first!'; return; }
      var html = '';
      j.leaders.forEach(function(p, i){
        var medal = i === 0 ? '🥇' : i === 1 ? '🥈' : i === 2 ? '🥉' : (i+1) + '.';
        var armor = p.fullSet ? ' 💎' : p.pieces ? ' 🛡️x' + p.pieces : '';
        html += '<div style="display:flex;justify-content:space-between;padding:8px 4px;border-bottom:1px solid rgba(255,140,46,0.25);">' +
          '<span>' + medal + ' ' + String(p.name || 'Fire Tamer').slice(0,18) + armor + '</span>' +
          '<span style="color:#ffd76a;">' + p.wins + 'W · ' + p.kills + 'K</span></div>';
      });
      el.innerHTML = html;
    }).catch(function(){
      var el = document.getElementById('fb-lb-rows');
      if(el) el.textContent = 'Could not reach the leaderboard. Try again soon!';
    });
  } catch(e){}
}
