/* ============ Fire Babys 3D: 250 World Tasks + Silver Armor ============
   Complete all 250 tasks -> full Silver Armor set (+20% over everything).
   Silver overrides Bronze (+10%). */

var FB_TASK_STORE_KEY = 'fb_silver_tasks_v1';
var FB_SILVER_KEY = 'fb_silver_armor_v1';

/* ----- task progress store ----- */
var __fbTaskProgress = {};   // taskId -> current count
var __fbTasksDone = {};      // taskId -> true when complete
var __fbTaskInitDone = false;

function __fbLoadTasks() {
  if (__fbTaskInitDone) return;
  __fbTaskInitDone = true;
  try {
    var raw = localStorage.getItem(FB_TASK_STORE_KEY);
    if (raw) {
      var d = JSON.parse(raw);
      __fbTaskProgress = d.progress || {};
      __fbTasksDone = d.done || {};
    }
  } catch (e) {}
  // check silver armor
  try {
    var sraw = localStorage.getItem(FB_SILVER_KEY);
    if (sraw) {
      var s = JSON.parse(sraw);
      if (s && s.unlocked) __FB_SILVER_UNLOCKED = true;
    }
  } catch (e) {}
}

function __fbSaveTasks() {
  try {
    localStorage.setItem(FB_TASK_STORE_KEY, JSON.stringify({
      progress: __fbTaskProgress,
      done: __fbTasksDone,
    }));
  } catch (e) {}
}

/* ----- record a task event ----- */
function fbTaskEvent(event, value) {
  try {
    __fbLoadTasks();
    value = value || 1;
    if (typeof FB_TASKS_250 === 'undefined') return;
    var changed = false;
    for (var i = 0; i < FB_TASKS_250.length; i++) {
      var t = FB_TASKS_250[i];
      if (t.event !== event) continue;
      if (__fbTasksDone[t.id]) continue;
      var cur = (__fbTaskProgress[t.id] || 0) + value;
      __fbTaskProgress[t.id] = cur;
      changed = true;
      if (cur >= t.target) {
        __fbTasksDone[t.id] = true;
        __fbOnTaskComplete(t);
      }
    }
    if (changed) __fbSaveTasks();
    __fbCheckSilver();
  } catch (e) {}
}

function __fbOnTaskComplete(t) {
  try {
    if (typeof toast === 'function') toast('✅ TASK DONE: ' + t.name);
    if (typeof beep === 'function') beep(660, 0.12, 'sine', 0.06);
  } catch (e) {}
  // Season 1 XP for every completed task
  try { if (typeof __fbSeasonXP === 'function') __fbSeasonXP(50, 'task'); } catch(e){}
  // update UI if open
  try { __fbRefreshTaskUI(); } catch (e) {}
}

function __fbTasksCompletedCount() {
  var n = 0;
  for (var k in __fbTasksDone) if (__fbTasksDone[k]) n++;
  return n;
}

/* ----- silver armor ----- */
var __FB_SILVER_UNLOCKED = false;
var __FB_SILVER_BOOST = 0.20;

function __fbCheckSilver() {
  if (__FB_SILVER_UNLOCKED) return;
  var done = __fbTasksCompletedCount();
  if (done >= 250) {
    __FB_SILVER_UNLOCKED = true;
    try {
      localStorage.setItem(FB_SILVER_KEY, JSON.stringify({ unlocked: true, at: Date.now() }));
    } catch (e) {}
    // apply silver visuals
    try {
      if (player && player.group) addSilverArmor(player.group, 1);
    } catch (e) {}
    // celebrate
    try {
      if (typeof toast === 'function') toast('🥈 SILVER ARMOR UNLOCKED! +20% OVER EVERYTHING!');
      if (typeof beep === 'function') { beep(523, 0.15, 'sine', 0.08); setTimeout(function(){ beep(659, 0.15, 'sine', 0.08); }, 150); setTimeout(function(){ beep(784, 0.3, 'sine', 0.08); }, 300); }
    } catch (e) {}
    // broadcast to opponents
    try {
      if (typeof net !== 'undefined' && net && net.connected && typeof mpInGame !== 'undefined' && mpInGame) {
        net.sendAction({ kind: 'silver_armor', data: { on: 1 } });
      }
    } catch (e) {}
    // sync to server
    try { __fbSyncSilver(); } catch (e) {}
    // silver unlocks the Magma Core Anvil
    try { if (typeof __fbCheckWeaponUnlocks === 'function') __fbCheckWeaponUnlocks(); } catch (e) {}
  }
}

/* ----- silver armor meshes: bright metallic silver ----- */
function addSilverArmor(g, R) {
  if (!g || typeof THREE === 'undefined') return;
  try {
    // remove bronze first (silver overrides)
    if (g.userData.bronzeArmor) {
      g.userData.bronzeArmor.forEach(function(m){ g.remove(m); });
      g.userData.bronzeArmor = null;
    }
    if (g.userData.silverArmor) {
      g.userData.silverArmor.forEach(function(m){ g.remove(m); });
    }
    var parts = [];
    var silver = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({
      color: 0xd8d8e0, roughness: 0.25, metalness: 0.9,
      emissive: 0x8899ff, emissiveIntensity: 0.12,
    });
    // greaves
    [-1, 1].forEach(function(s){
      var gr = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.CylinderGeometry(R*0.27, R*0.31, R*0.58, 10), silver);
      gr.position.set(s*R*0.38, -R*0.95, 0); gr.castShadow = true;
      g.add(gr); parts.push(gr);
    });
    // gauntlets
    [-1, 1].forEach(function(s){
      var ga = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.CylinderGeometry(R*0.21, R*0.24, R*0.48, 10), silver);
      ga.position.set(s*R*1.02, -R*0.15, 0); ga.rotation.z = s * -0.12; ga.castShadow = true;
      g.add(ga); parts.push(ga);
    });
    // helmet
    var helm = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.SphereGeometry(R*0.94, 18, 12, 0, Math.PI*2, 0, Math.PI*0.55), silver);
    helm.position.y = R*0.55; helm.castShadow = true;
    g.add(helm); parts.push(helm);
    var brim = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.TorusGeometry(R*0.90, R*0.07, 8, 20), silver);
    brim.rotation.x = Math.PI/2; brim.position.y = R*0.62;
    g.add(brim); parts.push(brim);
    // chestplate with silver star
    var chest = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.SphereGeometry(R*1.06, 18, 12, 0, Math.PI*2, 0, Math.PI*0.62), silver);
    chest.position.y = R*0.02; chest.castShadow = true;
    g.add(chest); parts.push(chest);
    var star = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.OctahedronGeometry(R*0.24),
      new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: 0xffffff, roughness: 0.05, metalness: 0.2,
        emissive: 0xcceeff, emissiveIntensity: 1.0 }));
    star.position.set(0, R*0.12, R*1.0); star.rotation.y = Math.PI/4;
    g.add(star); parts.push(star);
    // silver glow
    if (typeof GLOW_TEX !== 'undefined' && GLOW_TEX) {
      var glow = new THREE.Sprite(new THREE.SpriteMaterial({ map: GLOW_TEX, color: 0xcceeff,
        blending: THREE.AdditiveBlending, depthWrite: false, transparent: true, opacity: 0.4 }));
      glow.scale.set(R*3.8, R*3.8, 1);
      g.add(glow); parts.push(glow);
    }
    g.userData.silverArmor = parts;
  } catch (e) {}
}

/* ----- effective boost: silver (20%) overrides bronze (10%); season boost stacks on top ----- */
function __fbEffectiveBoost() {
  var b = 0;
  if (typeof __FB_SILVER_UNLOCKED !== 'undefined' && __FB_SILVER_UNLOCKED) b = __FB_SILVER_BOOST;
  else b = (typeof __FB_ARMOR_BOOST !== 'undefined') ? __FB_ARMOR_BOOST : 0;
  try { if (typeof __FB_SEASON_BOOST === 'number' && __FB_SEASON_BOOST > 0) b += __FB_SEASON_BOOST; } catch(e){}
  return b;
}

/* ----- server sync ----- */
function __fbSyncSilver() {
  var t = null;
  try { if (window.FireBabysAuth && window.FireBabysAuth.getToken) t = window.FireBabysAuth.getToken(); } catch (e) {}
  if (!t) return;
  fetch('https://fire-babys-auth.jeffpruden288.workers.dev/profile/silver', {
    method: 'POST', headers: { 'content-type': 'application/json' },
    body: JSON.stringify({ token: t, unlocked: __FB_SILVER_UNLOCKED, tasksDone: __fbTasksCompletedCount() }),
  }).catch(function(){});
}

/* ----- task UI panel ----- */
var __fbTaskUIOpen = false;
function __fbShowTasks() {
  try {
    __fbLoadTasks();
    var old = document.getElementById('fb-task-ov');
    if (old) { old.parentNode.removeChild(old); __fbTaskUIOpen = false; return; }
    __fbTaskUIOpen = true;
    var done = __fbTasksCompletedCount();
    var ov = document.createElement('div');
    ov.id = 'fb-task-ov';
    ov.style.cssText = 'position:fixed;inset:0;z-index:99990;display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:center;background:rgba(8,4,2,0.88);padding:16px;box-sizing:border-box;';
    var cats = ['warrior','guardian','explorer','champion','legend'];
    var icons = { warrior: '⚔️', guardian: '🔥', explorer: '🗺️', champion: '👑', legend: '💎' };
    var html = '<div style="width:100%;max-width:520px;max-height:85vh;display:flex;flex-direction:column;background:#1c1006;border:3px solid #cceeff;border-radius:20px;padding:20px;">' +
      '<div style="color:#cceeff;font-size:20px;font-weight:900;letter-spacing:2px;text-align:center;">🥈 WORLD TASKS — ' + done + '/250</div>' +
      '<div style="color:#ffe9d6;font-size:13px;text-align:center;margin:8px 0;">Complete all 250 to earn the <b style="color:#cceeff;">SILVER ARMOR</b> (+20% over everything!)</div>' +
      '<div style="height:8px;background:#3a2410;border-radius:4px;margin-bottom:12px;"><div style="height:100%;width:' + (done/250*100) + '%;background:linear-gradient(90deg,#8899ff,#cceeff);border-radius:4px;"></div></div>' +
      '<div id="fb-task-cats" style="display:flex;gap:6px;margin-bottom:12px;flex-wrap:wrap;justify-content:center;"></div>' +
      '<div id="fb-task-list" style="flex:1;overflow-y:auto;min-height:200px;"></div>' +
      '<button id="fb-task-close" style="margin-top:12px;padding:12px;font-size:16px;font-weight:900;border:none;border-radius:12px;cursor:pointer;color:#0a1a2a;background:linear-gradient(180deg,#cceeff,#8899ff);">CLOSE</button></div>';
    ov.innerHTML = html;
    ov.querySelector('#fb-task-close').addEventListener('click', function(){ ov.parentNode.removeChild(ov); __fbTaskUIOpen = false; });
    ov.addEventListener('click', function(e){ if (e.target === ov){ ov.parentNode.removeChild(ov); __fbTaskUIOpen = false; } });
    document.body.appendChild(ov);
    // category tabs
    var catEl = document.getElementById('fb-task-cats');
    cats.forEach(function(c, i){
      var b = document.createElement('button');
      var cDone = FB_TASKS_250.filter(function(t){ return t.category === c && __fbTasksDone[t.id]; }).length;
      b.textContent = icons[c] + ' ' + c + ' ' + cDone + '/50';
      b.style.cssText = 'padding:8px 12px;font-size:13px;font-weight:700;border:none;border-radius:8px;cursor:pointer;color:#ffe9d6;background:' + (i===0 ? '#4a6a9a' : '#3a2410') + ';';
      b.addEventListener('click', function(){ __fbRenderTaskCat(c); });
      catEl.appendChild(b);
    });
    __fbRenderTaskCat('warrior');
  } catch (e) {}
}

function __fbRenderTaskCat(cat) {
  try {
    var list = document.getElementById('fb-task-list');
    if (!list) return;
    var html = '';
    FB_TASKS_250.filter(function(t){ return t.category === cat; }).forEach(function(t){
      var isDone = !!__fbTasksDone[t.id];
      var prog = Math.min(__fbTaskProgress[t.id] || 0, t.target);
      var pct = Math.round(prog / t.target * 100);
      html += '<div style="padding:10px;margin-bottom:8px;border-radius:10px;background:' + (isDone ? 'rgba(136,153,255,0.15)' : 'rgba(255,140,46,0.08)') + ';border:1px solid ' + (isDone ? '#8899ff' : 'rgba(255,140,46,0.3)') + ';">' +
        '<div style="color:' + (isDone ? '#cceeff' : '#ffe9d6') + ';font-size:14px;font-weight:700;">' + (isDone ? '✅ ' : '') + t.name + '</div>' +
        '<div style="color:#c9a882;font-size:12px;margin:4px 0;">' + t.desc + '</div>' +
        '<div style="height:6px;background:#2a1a0c;border-radius:3px;"><div style="height:100%;width:' + pct + '%;background:' + (isDone ? '#8899ff' : '#ff8c2e') + ';border-radius:3px;"></div></div>' +
        '<div style="color:#c9a882;font-size:11px;margin-top:4px;">' + prog + ' / ' + t.target + '</div></div>';
    });
    list.innerHTML = html;
  } catch (e) {}
}

function __fbRefreshTaskUI() {
  if (__fbTaskUIOpen) {
    try {
      var ov = document.getElementById('fb-task-ov');
      if (ov) { ov.parentNode.removeChild(ov); __fbTaskUIOpen = false; }
      __fbShowTasks();
    } catch (e) {}
  }
}

/* ---------- jump state ---------- */
var mpJumpY = 0, mpJumpV = 0, mpJumps = 0, mpFlipT = 0;
var JUMP_GRAV = 25, JUMP_V1 = 14, JUMP_V2 = 12, ATK_RANGE = 3.5;

/* ---------- AI bots (host-simulated, real server players) ---------- */
var mpBotsEnabled = true;   // "fill empty slots with bots" toggle (host)
var mpBots = [];            // host-side bot sim objects
var mpBotAcc = 0;           // bot tick accumulator (seconds)
var mpBotStarting = false;  // bot-fill sequence in progress (lobby)
var BOT_NAMES = ['🤖 Ember','🤖 Cinder','🤖 Spark','🤖 Ash','🤖 Pyro','🤖 Kindle',
  '🤖 Flint','🤖 Scorch','🤖 Tinder','🤖 Coal','🤖 Match','🤖 Bonfire',
  '🤖 Toasty','🤖 Char','🤖 Smokey','🤖 Firefly','🤖 Lava','🤖 Magma',
  '🤖 Torch','🤖 Ignis','🤖 Fuego','🤖 Vulcan','🤖 Sear','🤖 Singe',
  '🤖 Warmy','🤖 Hotshot','🤖 Sizzle','🤖 Blaze','🤖 Inferno','🤖 Flare'];
function mpIsBotId(id){
  for(var i=0;i<mpBots.length;i++) if(mpBots[i].id === id) return true;
  return false;
}
function mpIsBotName(n){ return typeof n === 'string' && n.indexOf('🤖') === 0; } function mpDisplayName(n){ return typeof n === 'string' && n.indexOf('🤖') === 0 ? n.slice(2) : n; }

/* ---------- team colors ---------- */
var TEAM_COLORS = [
  { body:'#3ca9ff', dark:'#0a3a6a', core:'#c0e8ff', name:'BLUE' },  // team 0
  { body:'#ff5a3c', dark:'#6a0a0a', core:'#ffc0b0', name:'RED'  }   // team 1
];

/* ---------- hub integration ---------- */
function mpAddHubButton(){
  // Called after renderHub(); adds MULTIPLAYER as an extra button
  var wrap = document.getElementById('hubbtns');
  if(!wrap || document.getElementById('mp_hubbtn')) return;
  var b = document.createElement('button');
  b.className = 'modebtn'; b.id = 'mp_hubbtn';
  b.innerHTML = '<div class="ic">🌐</div><div><div class="nm">MULTIPLAYER</div><div class="ds">Co-op & versus — up to 30 players!</div></div>';
  b.addEventListener('click', function(){ if(typeof beep==='function') beep(600,0.1,'square',0.07); startMultiplayer(); });
  // insert at top
  wrap.insertBefore(b, wrap.firstChild);
}

/* ---------- multiplayer menu ---------- */
function startMultiplayer(){
  if(typeof hideHub === 'function') hideHub();
  document.getElementById('mpmenuov').style.display = 'flex';
  document.getElementById('mpmenuerr').style.display = 'none';
  // mode select buttons
  var btns = document.querySelectorAll('.mpmodebtn');
  for(var i=0;i<btns.length;i++){
    (function(btn){
      btn.classList.toggle('on', btn.getAttribute('data-mode') === mpMode);
      btn.onclick = function(){
        mpMode = btn.getAttribute('data-mode');
        for(var j=0;j<btns.length;j++) btns[j].classList.remove('on');
        btn.classList.add('on');
        if(typeof beep==='function') beep(500,0.08,'square',0.06);
      };
    })(btns[i]);
  }
  // wire buttons (once)
  wireOnce('mpcreatebtn', mpCreateLobby);
  wireOnce('mpjoinbtn', mpJoinLobby);
  wireOnce('mpbackbtn', function(){
    document.getElementById('mpmenuov').style.display = 'none';
    if(typeof showHub==='function') showHub();
  });
}
function wireOnce(id, fn){
  var el = document.getElementById(id);
  if(el && !el._mpwired){ el._mpwired = true; el.addEventListener('click', fn); }
}
function mpShowErr(id, msg){
  var e = document.getElementById(id);
  if(e){ e.textContent = msg; e.style.display = 'block'; }
}
function mpPlayerName(){
  var n = '';
  try {
    n = (document.getElementById('mpname').value || '').trim().slice(0,14);
    if(!n) n = (localStorage.getItem('fb3d_title') || '').trim().slice(0,14);
  } catch(e){}
  return n || 'Player';
}

/* ---------- lobby create / join ---------- */
function mpCreateLobby(){
  var btn = document.getElementById('mpcreatebtn');
  btn.disabled = true; btn.textContent = 'CREATING...';
  mpShowErr('mpmenuerr', '');
  net = new FireBabysNet({ server: MP_SERVER });
  mpWireNet();
  net.createLobby({ mode: mpMode }).then(function(code){
    return mpConnectTo(code);
  }).catch(function(e){
    btn.disabled = false; btn.textContent = 'CREATE LOBBY 🔥';
    mpShowErr('mpmenuerr', 'Could not create lobby: ' + (e.message || 'network error'));
  });
}
function mpJoinLobby(){
  var code = (document.getElementById('mpcode').value || '').trim().toUpperCase();
  if(code.length !== 6){ mpShowErr('mpmenuerr', 'Enter a 6-letter lobby code'); return; }
  var btn = document.getElementById('mpjoinbtn');
  btn.disabled = true; btn.textContent = 'JOINING...';
  mpShowErr('mpmenuerr', '');
  net = new FireBabysNet({ server: MP_SERVER });
  mpWireNet();
  mpConnectTo(code).catch(function(e){
    btn.disabled = false; btn.textContent = 'JOIN LOBBY';
    mpShowErr('mpmenuerr', 'Could not join: ' + (e.message || 'lobby not found'));
  });
}
function mpConnectTo(code){
  return net.connect(code, { name: mpPlayerName() }).then(function(){
    document.getElementById('mpmenuov').style.display = 'none';
    document.getElementById('mplobbyov').style.display = 'flex';
    mpReady = false;
    updateReadyBtn();
    wireOnce('mpreadybtn', mpToggleReady);
    wireOnce('mpstartbtn', mpHostStart);
    wireOnce('mpleavebtn', mpLeaveLobby);
    // bot-fill toggle
    var bf = document.getElementById('mpbotfill');
    if(bf && !bf._mpw){
      bf._mpw = true;
      bf.addEventListener('change', function(){
        mpBotsEnabled = bf.checked;
        if(net && net.state){ try { mpRenderLobby(net.state); } catch(e){} }
      });
    }
    // reset buttons
    var cb = document.getElementById('mpcreatebtn');
    if(cb){ cb.disabled = false; cb.textContent = 'CREATE LOBBY 🔥'; }
    var jb = document.getElementById('mpjoinbtn');
    if(jb){ jb.disabled = false; jb.textContent = 'JOIN LOBBY'; }
  });
}

/* ---------- net event wiring ---------- */
function mpWireNet(){
  net.on('lobby', mpRenderLobby);
  net.on('start', mpOnGameStart);
  net.on('end', mpOnGameEnd);
  net.on('pos', mpOnPos);
  net.on('action', mpOnAction);
  net.on('presence', mpOnPresence);
  net.on('host', mpOnHostChange);
  net.on('error', function(msg){ mpShowErr('mplobbyerr', msg); });
  net.on('close', mpOnDisconnect);
  net.on('reconnecting', function(info){
    mpShowErr('mplobbyerr', 'Reconnecting... (attempt ' + info.attempt + ')');
  });
}
function mpRenderLobby(state){
  if(!state) return;
  document.getElementById('mplobbycode').textContent = 'CODE: ' + state.code;
  var modeLabel = state.mode === 'coop' ? '🤝 CO-OP' : (state.mode === 'catch' ? '🏃 SPLASH' : '⚔️ ' + state.mode.toUpperCase());
  document.getElementById('mplobbymode').textContent = modeLabel + ' · ' + state.players.length + '/' + state.maxPlayers;
  document.getElementById('mplobbyerr').style.display = 'none';

  var list = document.getElementById('mpplayerlist');
  list.innerHTML = '';
  var isVersus = state.mode !== 'coop';
  state.players.forEach(function(p){
    var div = document.createElement('div');
    div.className = 'mpplayer' + (p.ready ? ' ready' : '');
    var teamTag = '';
    if(isVersus){
      var tc = p.team === 0 ? 'team0' : 'team1';
      var tn = p.team === 0 ? 'BLUE' : 'RED';
      teamTag = '<span class="teamtag ' + tc + '">' + tn + '</span>';
    }
    var hostStar = (p.id === state.hostId) ? ' <span class="hostname">👑</span>' : '';
    var youTag = (net && p.id === net.playerId) ? ' (you)' : '';
    var botTag = ''; /* bots show clean gamer names now - no badge */
    div.innerHTML = '<div class="rdot"></div><div>' + escapeHtml(mpDisplayName(p.name)) + youTag + hostStar + '</div>' + teamTag +
      '<div style="margin-left:auto;color:' + (p.ready ? '#4aff6a' : '#888') + '">' + (p.ready ? 'READY' : '...') + '</div>';
    list.appendChild(div);
    // track my team
    if(net && p.id === net.playerId) mpMyTeam = p.team || 0;
  });

  // host start button: visible only to host
  var startBtn = document.getElementById('mpstartbtn');
  // bot-fill toggle: host only, versus modes only
  var botWrap = document.getElementById('mpbotwrap');
  if(botWrap) botWrap.style.display = (net && net.isHost && isVersus) ? 'flex' : 'none';
  if(net && net.isHost){
    startBtn.style.display = '';
    var need = isVersus ? ((typeof FireBabysNet !== 'undefined' && FireBabysNet.MODES[ state.mode ]) || 0) : 0;
    var humans = state.players.filter(function(p){ return !mpIsBotName(p.name); }).length;
    var allReady = state.players.length > 0 && state.players.every(function(p){ return p.ready; });
    // Host doesn't need to be "ready" to start — clicking START is their ready.
    // Only require non-host players to be ready.
    var othersReady = state.players.length > 0 && state.players.every(function(p){
      try { if(net && p.id === net.playerId) return true; } catch(e){}
      return p.ready;
    });
    var canStart = false, label = '';
    if(mpBotStarting){
      canStart = false; label = '🎮 ADDING PLAYERS...';
    } else if(!isVersus){
      canStart = othersReady; label = othersReady ? '🚀 START GAME' : '⏳ WAITING FOR READY...';
    } else if(state.mode === 'catch'){
      var ccn = state.players.length;
      var ccOk = ccn >= 4 && ccn <= 30 && ccn % 2 === 0;
      if(ccOk){
        canStart = othersReady; label = othersReady ? '🚀 START GAME' : '⏳ WAITING FOR READY...';
      } else if(mpBotsEnabled && othersReady){
        var ccTarget = Math.max(4, humans + (humans % 2 === 0 ? 0 : 1));
        if(ccTarget <= 30 && ccTarget > humans){ canStart = true; label = '🎮 START WITH ' + (ccTarget - humans) + ' PLAYERS'; }
        else { canStart = false; label = '⏳ NEED 4-30 PLAYERS (EVEN NUMBER)'; }
      } else {
        canStart = false; label = '⏳ NEED 4-30 PLAYERS (EVEN NUMBER)';
      }
    } else if(state.players.length === need){
      canStart = othersReady; label = othersReady ? '🚀 START GAME' : '⏳ WAITING FOR READY...';
    } else if(mpBotsEnabled && humans < need){
      canStart = othersReady;
      label = othersReady ? '🎮 START WITH ' + (need - humans) + ' PLAYERS' : '⏳ WAITING FOR READY...';
    } else {
      canStart = false; label = '⏳ NEED ' + need + ' PLAYERS (have ' + state.players.length + ')';
    }
    startBtn.disabled = !canStart;
    startBtn.textContent = label;
    // auto-start once bot fill completes (all present + ready)
    var ccNeedMet = state.mode === 'catch'
      ? (state.players.length >= 4 && state.players.length <= 30 && state.players.length % 2 === 0)
      : state.players.length === need;
    if(mpBotStarting && ccNeedMet && allReady){
      mpBotStarting = false;
      try { net.start(); } catch(e){}
    }
  } else {
    startBtn.style.display = 'none';
  }
}
function escapeHtml(s){
  return String(s).replace(/&/g,'&amp;').replace(/</g,'&lt;').replace(/>/g,'&gt;');
}
function mpToggleReady(){
  mpReady = !mpReady;
  if(net) net.setReady(mpReady);
  updateReadyBtn();
}
function updateReadyBtn(){
  var b = document.getElementById('mpreadybtn');
  b.textContent = mpReady ? '❌ NOT READY' : '✅ READY';
  b.style.background = mpReady
    ? 'linear-gradient(180deg,#8a5a2e,#5e3a1e)'
    : 'linear-gradient(180deg,#2e8a4a,#1e5e2e)';
}
function mpLeaveLobby(){
  // Hide pause menu immediately (belt & suspenders: fbTogglePause may have missed it)
  try{ var _pm = document.getElementById('pausemenu'); if(_pm) _pm.style.display = 'none'; }catch(e){}
  try{ if(typeof fbPaused !== 'undefined') fbPaused = false; }catch(e){}
  // Show immediate feedback - quitting takes time (bot disconnects)
  try{
    var _old = document.getElementById('mp-leaving-ov');
    if(_old) _old.parentNode.removeChild(_old);
    var _lo = document.createElement('div');
    _lo.id = 'mp-leaving-ov';
    _lo.style.cssText = 'position:fixed;inset:0;z-index:999999;display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:center;background:rgba(0,0,0,0.7);color:#ffd76a;font:800 20px system-ui;';
    _lo.textContent = 'Leaving match...';
    document.body.appendChild(_lo);
  }catch(e){}
  // Do the slow cleanup asynchronously so the UI updates first
  setTimeout(function(){
    try{
      mpClearBots();
      mpCleanupNet();
      try{ document.getElementById('mplobbyov').style.display = 'none'; }catch(e){}
      if(mpInGame) mpExitGame();
      try{ var _pm2 = document.getElementById('pausemenu'); if(_pm2) _pm2.style.display = 'none'; }catch(e){}
      var _lo2 = document.getElementById('mp-leaving-ov');
      if(_lo2) _lo2.parentNode.removeChild(_lo2);
      if(typeof showHub === 'function') showHub();
    }catch(e){
      try{ var _lo3 = document.getElementById('mp-leaving-ov'); if(_lo3) _lo3.parentNode.removeChild(_lo3); }catch(ee){}
    }
  }, 100);
}
function mpCleanupNet(){
  if(net){ try { net.disconnect(); } catch(e){} net = null; }
  mpReady = false; mpInGame = false;
}
function mpOnDisconnect(){
  if(mpInGame){
    // try to show a toast; the net lib auto-reconnects
    if(typeof toast === 'function') toast('Connection lost — reconnecting...');
  }
}

/* =====================================================================
   COMBAT + JUMP — attack / block / double-jump for multiplayer.
   Client-side hit detection: each client checks if THEY were hit.
   ===================================================================== */
function mpMakeShieldMesh(){
  var s = new THREE.Mesh(
    new THREE.SphereGeometry(1.7, 18, 14),
    new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({ color: 0x4db2ff, transparent: true, opacity: 0.28,
      side: THREE.DoubleSide, depthWrite: false })
  );
  s.visible = false;
  return s;
}
function mpEnsureShield(){
  if(!mpShield) mpShield = mpMakeShieldMesh();
  try {
    if(player && player.group){
      if(mpShield.parent !== player.group){
        if(mpShield.parent) mpShield.parent.remove(mpShield);
        player.group.add(mpShield);
      }
      mpShield.visible = mpBlocking;
    }
  } catch(e){}
}
function mpShowCombat(on){
  var el = document.getElementById('mpcombat');
  if(el) el.classList.toggle('on', !!on);
  if(!on && mpBlocking) mpSetBlock(false);
}

/* =====================================================================
   SINGLE-PLAYER COMBAT — attack / block / double-jump in every mode.
   Reuses the multiplayer combat state + UI; branches where MP needs net.
   ===================================================================== */
var spActive = false;        // true when SP combat UI should show
var spBlockToastT = 0;       // throttle for BLOCKED! toast

function combatActive(){ return mpInGame || spActive; }

// single-player: damage nearest foe in range with the player's attack
function spHitFoe(){
  try {
    if(!player || !player.group || player.dead) return;
    if(typeof units === 'undefined' || typeof dealDamage !== 'function') return;
    var bp = player.group.position;
    var best = null, bd = ATK_RANGE;
    for(var i=0;i<units.length;i++){
      var u = units[i];
      if(u.side === 'foe' && !u.dead && u.group){
        var dx = u.group.position.x - bp.x, dz = u.group.position.z - bp.z;
        var d = Math.hypot(dx, dz);
        if(d < bd){ bd = d; best = u; }
      }
    }
    if(best){
      player.group.rotation.y = Math.atan2(
        best.group.position.x - bp.x, best.group.position.z - bp.z);
      var wmult = 1;
      try { wmult = (typeof __fbWeaponMult === 'function') ? __fbWeaponMult() : 1; } catch(e){}
      var dmg = player.atk * wmult * (0.85 + Math.random()*0.3);
      dealDamage(player, best, dmg);
    }
  } catch(e){}
}

// per-frame SP combat: cooldowns, attack anim, jump physics (runs AFTER updateBaby)
function spPostFrame(dt){
  if(!spActive) return;
  if(!player || !player.group || player.dead) return;
  if(mpAtkCd > 0) mpAtkCd -= dt;
  if(mpAtkAnimT > 0){
    mpAtkAnimT -= dt;
    var ak = Math.sin(Math.max(0,(0.28-mpAtkAnimT))/0.28*Math.PI);
    try { player.group.scale.set(1+0.25*ak, 1-0.15*ak, 1+0.25*ak); } catch(e){}
    if(typeof fbSwingWeapon==='function') fbSwingWeapon(player.group, ak);
    if(mpAtkAnimT <= 0){ try { player.group.scale.set(1,1,1); } catch(e){} if(typeof fbSwingWeapon==='function') fbSwingWeapon(player.group, 0); }
  }
  if(mpJumpY > 0 || mpJumpV !== 0){
    mpJumpV -= JUMP_GRAV*dt;
    mpJumpY += mpJumpV*dt;
    if(mpJumpY <= 0){ mpJumpY = 0; mpJumpV = 0; mpJumps = 0; }
  }
  if(mpFlipT > 0){
    mpFlipT -= dt;
    try { player.group.rotation.x = (0.45-mpFlipT)/0.45*Math.PI*2; } catch(e){}
    if(mpFlipT <= 0){ try { player.group.rotation.x = 0; } catch(e){} }
  }
  try { player.group.position.y += mpJumpY; } catch(e){}
}

// block negates incoming damage in single-player (wrap dealDamage once)
var _origDealDamage = null;
function spPatchDealDamage(){
  if(_origDealDamage || typeof dealDamage !== 'function') return;
  _origDealDamage = dealDamage;
  dealDamage = function(src, tgt, amount){
    try {
      if(tgt === player && !tgt.dead && mpBlocking && !mpInGame){
        try { tgt.group.userData.flash = 0.4; } catch(e){}
        spBlockToastT -= 0.016;
        if(spBlockToastT <= 0){
          spBlockToastT = 0.9;
          if(typeof toast === 'function') toast('🛡️ BLOCKED!');
          if(typeof beep === 'function') beep(880, 0.08, 'square', 0.05);
        }
        if(src && src.group && player && player.group){
          var bp = player.group.position, ap = src.group.position;
          var dx = ap.x - bp.x, dz = ap.z - bp.z, d = Math.hypot(dx,dz)||1;
          ap.x += dx/d*0.8; ap.z += dz/d*0.8;
        }
        return;
      }
    } catch(e){}
    _origDealDamage(src, tgt, amount);
  };
}

// patch battle loops so jump/attack-anim apply there too
var _origUpdateBattle = null, _origUpdateRescue = null;
function spPatchBattleLoops(){
  if(typeof updateBattle === 'function' && !_origUpdateBattle){
    _origUpdateBattle = updateBattle;
    updateBattle = function(dt, t){ _origUpdateBattle(dt, t); spPostFrame(dt); };
  }
  if(typeof updateRescue === 'function' && !_origUpdateRescue){
    _origUpdateRescue = updateRescue;
    updateRescue = function(dt, t){ _origUpdateRescue(dt, t); spPostFrame(dt); };
  }
}

// visibility: show combat buttons whenever in active gameplay (any mode)
function spRefreshUI(){
  var show = false;
  try {
    if(mpInGame) show = true;
    else if((state === 'EXPLORE' || state === 'BATTLE') &&
            gameMode !== 'multiplayer' && gameMode !== 'hub' &&
            typeof player !== 'undefined' && player && player.group && !player.dead &&
            typeof cine !== 'undefined' && !cine){
      show = true;
    }
  } catch(e){}
  if(show && !spActive && !mpInGame){
    mpAtkCd = 0; mpAtkAnimT = 0; mpBlocking = false;
    mpJumpY = 0; mpJumpV = 0; mpJumps = 0; mpFlipT = 0;
    try { mpEnsureShield(); } catch(e){}
  }
  spActive = show && !mpInGame;
  var el = document.getElementById('mpcombat');
  if(el) el.classList.toggle('on', !!show);
  if(!show && mpBlocking && !mpInGame) mpSetBlock(false);
}
/* ----- attack (multiplayer + single-player) ----- */
function mpDoAttack(){
  if(mpGameMode === 'catch') return; // SPLASH: grab-and-carry only, no attacks
  if(!combatActive() || mpBlocking || mpAtkCd > 0) return;
  if(mpInGame && mpKO) return;
  if(!mpInGame && (!player || !player.group || player.dead)) return;
  mpAtkCd = 0.8;
  mpAtkAnimT = 0.28;
  try { if (typeof fbTaskEvent === 'function') { fbTaskEvent('attack', 1); fbTaskEvent('damage', 1); } } catch(e){}
  if(mpInGame){
    try {
      var bp = player.group.position;
      net.sendAction({ kind: 'attack', x: Math.round(bp.x*100), y: Math.round(bp.z*100),
        data: { dir: Math.round((player.group.rotation.y||0)*100), boost: (typeof __fbEffectiveBoost === 'function' ? __fbEffectiveBoost() : __FB_ARMOR_BOOST), wmult: (typeof __fbWeaponMult === 'function' ? __fbWeaponMult() : 1) } });
      // host: server doesn't echo own actions, so check bot hits locally
      if(net.isHost && mpBots.length) mpBotHits({ id: net.playerId }, bp.x, bp.z);
    } catch(e){}
  } else {
    spHitFoe();
  }
  if(typeof beep === 'function') beep(220, 0.12, 'sawtooth', 0.08);
}
/* ----- block (multiplayer + single-player) ----- */
function mpSetBlock(on){
  try { if (on && typeof fbTaskEvent === 'function') fbTaskEvent('block', 1); } catch(e){}
  if(!combatActive() || (mpInGame && mpKO)) on = false;
  if(mpBlocking === on) return;
  mpBlocking = on;
  mpEnsureShield();
  var bb = document.getElementById('mpblkbtn');
  if(bb) bb.classList.toggle('active', on);
  if(mpInGame){
    try { net.sendAction({ kind: 'block', data: { on: on ? 1 : 0 } }); } catch(e){}
  }
  if(on && typeof beep === 'function') beep(520, 0.07, 'square', 0.05);
}
/* ----- jump / double jump (multiplayer + single-player) ----- */
function mpDoJump(){
  try { if (typeof fbTaskEvent === 'function') fbTaskEvent('jump', 1); } catch(e){}
  if(!combatActive() || (mpInGame && mpKO)) return;
  if(!mpInGame && (!player || !player.group || player.dead)) return;
  if(mpJumps === 0){
    mpJumpV = JUMP_V1; mpJumps = 1;
    if(mpInGame){ try { net.sendAction({ kind: 'jump', data: { n: 1 } }); } catch(e){} }
    if(typeof beep === 'function') beep(440, 0.1, 'square', 0.06);
  } else if(mpJumps === 1){
    mpJumpV = JUMP_V2; mpJumps = 2; mpFlipT = 0.45;
    if(mpInGame){ try { net.sendAction({ kind: 'jump', data: { n: 2 } }); } catch(e){} }
    if(typeof beep === 'function') beep(660, 0.12, 'square', 0.06);
  }
}
/* ----- local player takes a hit ----- */
function mpTakeHit(attackerId, ax, az, friendly, msg){
  if(!player || !player.group) return;
  var bp = player.group.position;
  // knockback away from attacker
  var dx = bp.x - ax, dz = bp.z - az, d = Math.hypot(dx, dz) || 1;
  var nx = Math.max(-26, Math.min(26, bp.x + dx/d*1.6));
  var nz = Math.max(-108, Math.min(44, bp.z + dz/d*1.6));
  try {
    if(typeof resolveColliders === 'function'){ var rc = resolveColliders(player, nx, nz); nx = rc.x; nz = rc.z; }
  } catch(e){}
  bp.x = nx; bp.z = nz;
  try { player.group.userData.flash = 1; } catch(e){}
  if(typeof beep === 'function') beep(150, 0.15, 'sawtooth', 0.09);
  if(friendly){ if(typeof toast === 'function') toast('💨 Friendly bump!'); return; }
  // Bronze armor: attacker deals +boost%, defender takes -boost%. Weapon multiplies damage.
  var atkBoost = 0, wmult = 1;
  try {
    if (msg && msg.data) {
      if (typeof msg.data.boost === 'number') atkBoost = Math.max(0, Math.min(0.5, msg.data.boost));
      if (typeof msg.data.wmult === 'number') wmult = Math.max(1, Math.min(5, msg.data.wmult));
    }
  } catch(e){}
  var defBoost = (typeof __fbEffectiveBoost === 'function' ? __fbEffectiveBoost() : __FB_ARMOR_BOOST);
   var dmg = 1 * wmult * (1 + atkBoost) * (1 - defBoost);
  mpHP = Math.max(0, mpHP - dmg);
  try { player.hpbar.set(mpHP/mpMaxHP, true); } catch(e){}
  try { net.sendAction({ kind: 'hit', data: { by: attackerId, hp: mpHP } }); } catch(e){}
  if(mpHP <= 0) mpDoKO(attackerId);
}
/* ----- local KO + respawn ----- */
function mpDoKO(attackerId){
  if(mpKO) return;
  mpKO = true; mpKOTimer = 3.0;
  // Stats: I died.
  try { __FB_STATS.deaths++; __fbReportStats(); } catch(e){}
  mpSetBlock(false);
  try { player.group.rotation.z = Math.PI/2 * 0.9; } catch(e){}
  fbExtinguishPoof(player.group);
  try { net.sendAction({ kind: 'ko', data: { by: attackerId || '' } }); } catch(e){}
  var arp = (attackerId && remotePlayers[attackerId]) ? remotePlayers[attackerId] : null;
  var atkTeam = arp ? arp.team : mpMyTeam;
  if(mpGameMode !== 'coop'){
    if(atkTeam === 0) mpKOs.team0++; else mpKOs.team1++;
    updateMpScoreboard();
  }
  if(typeof toast === 'function') toast('💀 KNOCKED OUT! Respawning...');
  if(typeof beep === 'function') beep(90, 0.4, 'sawtooth', 0.1);
}
function mpRespawn(){
  mpKO = false; mpHP = mpMaxHP;
  mpJumpY = 0; mpJumpV = 0; mpJumps = 0; mpFlipT = 0;
  try { player.hpbar.set(1); } catch(e){}
  try {
    player.group.rotation.z = 0; player.group.rotation.x = 0;
    player.group.scale.set(1,1,1);
  } catch(e){}
  fbReignite(player.group);
  fbDressPlayer();
  var sx = 0, sz = 40;
  if(mpGameMode !== 'coop') sx = (mpMyTeam === 0 ? -6 : 6);
  if(mpGameMode === 'catch'){ sx = 0; sz = (mpMyTeam === 0 ? -5 : 29); }
  try { player.group.position.x = sx; player.group.position.z = sz; } catch(e){}
  try { net.sendAction({ kind: 'respawn', data: { hp: mpHP } }); } catch(e){}
  if(typeof toast === 'function') toast('🔥 Back in the fight!');
  if(typeof beep === 'function') beep(520, 0.12, 'square', 0.07);
}
/* ----- wire combat buttons + keys ----- */
function mpCombatInit(){
  var ab = document.getElementById('mpatkbtn');
  var bb = document.getElementById('mpblkbtn');
  var jb = document.getElementById('mpjumpbtn');
  if(ab && !ab._mpw){
    ab._mpw = true;
    ab.addEventListener('pointerdown', function(e){ e.preventDefault(); mpDoAttack(); });
  }
  if(bb && !bb._mpw){
    bb._mpw = true;
    bb.addEventListener('pointerdown', function(e){ e.preventDefault(); mpSetBlock(true); });
    var off = function(){ mpSetBlock(false); };
    bb.addEventListener('pointerup', off);
    bb.addEventListener('pointercancel', off);
    bb.addEventListener('pointerleave', off);
  }
  if(jb && !jb._mpw){
    jb._mpw = true;
    jb.addEventListener('pointerdown', function(e){ e.preventDefault(); mpDoJump(); });
  }
  if(!window._mpCombatKeys){
    window._mpCombatKeys = true;
    window.addEventListener('keydown', function(e){
      if(!combatActive()) return;
      var k = (e.key||'').toLowerCase();
      if(k === 'j'){ mpDoAttack(); }
      else if(k === 'k'){ if(!mpBlockKey){ mpBlockKey = true; mpSetBlock(true); } }
      else if(k === ' '){ mpDoJump(); e.preventDefault(); }
    });
    window.addEventListener('keyup', function(e){
      var k = (e.key||'').toLowerCase();
      if(k === 'k'){ mpBlockKey = false; if(combatActive()) mpSetBlock(false); }
    });
  }
  var lb = document.getElementById('mplbbtn');
  if(lb && !lb._mpw){
    lb._mpw = true;
    lb.addEventListener('pointerdown', function(e){ e.preventDefault(); __fbShowLeaderboard(); });
  }
  var tk = document.getElementById('mptaskbtn');
  if(tk && !tk._mpw){
    tk._mpw = true;
    tk.addEventListener('pointerdown', function(e){ e.preventDefault(); if (typeof __fbShowTasks === 'function') __fbShowTasks(); });
  }
  var vb = document.getElementById('mpvaultbtn');
  if(vb && !vb._mpw){
    vb._mpw = true;
    vb.addEventListener('pointerdown', function(e){ e.preventDefault(); if (typeof __fbShowVault === 'function') __fbShowVault(); });
  }
  var snb = document.getElementById('mpseasonbtn');
  if(snb && !snb._mpw){
    snb._mpw = true;
    snb.addEventListener('pointerdown', function(e){ e.preventDefault(); if (typeof __fbShowSeason === 'function') __fbShowSeason(); });
  }
  var amb = document.getElementById('mparmorbtn');
  if(amb && !amb._mpw){
    amb._mpw = true;
    amb.addEventListener('pointerdown', function(e){ e.preventDefault(); if (typeof __fbShowArmory === 'function') __fbShowArmory(); });
  }
}

/* ----- pause menu ----- */
var fbPaused = false;
function fbTogglePause(show){
  fbPaused = (typeof show === 'boolean') ? show : !fbPaused;
  try { window.__fbPausedShared = fbPaused; } catch(e){} /* bridge for animate() — fbPaused is closure-scoped */
  var pm = document.getElementById('pausemenu');
  if(pm) pm.style.display = fbPaused ? 'flex' : 'none';
  var pp = document.getElementById('pausepill');
  if(pp) pp.textContent = fbPaused ? '▶️' : '⏸️';
  try {
    if(typeof mpClock !== 'undefined' && mpClock){
      if(fbPaused){ mpClock._pausedAt = performance.now(); }
      else if(mpClock._pausedAt){ /* resume: clock delta continues naturally */ delete mpClock._pausedAt; }
    }
  } catch(e){}
}
function fbPauseInit(){
  var pp = document.getElementById('pausepill');
  if(pp && !pp._fbw){
    pp._fbw = true;
    pp.addEventListener('pointerdown', function(e){ e.preventDefault(); e.stopPropagation(); fbTogglePause(); });
  }
  var sp = document.getElementById('soundpill');
  if(sp && !sp._fbw){
    sp._fbw = true;
    try { sp.textContent = fbMuted ? '🔇' : '🔊'; } catch(e){}
    sp.addEventListener('pointerdown', function(e){ e.preventDefault(); e.stopPropagation();
      fbMuted = !fbMuted;
      try{ localStorage.setItem('fb_muted', fbMuted ? '1' : '0'); }catch(x){}
      sp.textContent = fbMuted ? '🔇' : '🔊';
      if(!fbMuted){ try{ beep(880, 0.08, 'sine', 0.07); }catch(x){} }
    });
  }
  if(!window._fbVisPause){
    window._fbVisPause = true;
    document.addEventListener('visibilitychange', function(){
      try { if(document.hidden && !fbPaused && ((typeof mpInGame !== 'undefined' && mpInGame) || (typeof state !== 'undefined' && (state === 'EXPLORE' || state === 'BATTLE')))) fbTogglePause(true); } catch(e){}
    });
  }
  var rb = document.getElementById('resumebtn');
  if(rb && !rb._fbw){
    rb._fbw = true;
    rb.addEventListener('click', function(e){ e.preventDefault(); fbTogglePause(false); });
  }
  var qb = document.getElementById('quitbtn');
  if(qb && !qb._fbw){
    qb._fbw = true;
    qb.addEventListener('click', function(e){
      e.preventDefault();
      fbTogglePause(false);
      try { if(typeof mpLeaveLobby === 'function') mpLeaveLobby(); } catch(e){}
      try { if((typeof mpInGame === 'undefined' || !mpInGame) && typeof showHub === 'function' && typeof state !== 'undefined' && (state === 'EXPLORE' || state === 'BATTLE')) showHub(); } catch(e){}
    });
  }
  if(!window._fbPauseKey){
    window._fbPauseKey = true;
    window.addEventListener('keydown', function(e){
      var k = (e.key||'').toLowerCase();
      if(k === 'escape' || k === 'p'){
        try { if((typeof mpInGame !== 'undefined' && mpInGame) || (typeof state !== 'undefined' && (state === 'EXPLORE' || state === 'BATTLE'))) fbTogglePause(); } catch(e){}
      }
    });
  }
}
try { if(document.readyState === 'loading') document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', fbPauseInit); else fbPauseInit(); } catch(e){}

/* =====================================================================
   AI BOTS — host-simulated players that fill empty team slots.
   Bots are REAL server players: the host opens one WebSocket per bot,
   so team draft, exact-count start, and all pos/action relay work
   unchanged on every client. The host runs the bot AI each frame.
   ===================================================================== */
function mpHostStart(){
  if(!net || !net.isHost || mpBotStarting) return;
  var st = net.state; if(!st) return;
  var isVersus = st.mode !== 'coop';
  var need = isVersus ? ((typeof FireBabysNet !== 'undefined' && FireBabysNet.MODES[st.mode]) || 0) : 0;
  var humans = st.players.filter(function(p){ return !mpIsBotName(p.name); }).length;
  if(st.mode === 'catch'){
    var ccn2 = st.players.length;
    var ccOk2 = ccn2 >= 4 && ccn2 <= 30 && ccn2 % 2 === 0;
    if(!ccOk2 && mpBotsEnabled){
      var ccT2 = Math.max(4, humans + (humans % 2 === 0 ? 0 : 1));
      if(ccT2 <= 30 && ccT2 > humans){ mpStartWithBots(ccT2 - humans); return; }
    }
    try { net.start(); } catch(e){}
    return;
  }
  if(isVersus && mpBotsEnabled && humans < need && st.players.length < need){
    mpStartWithBots(need - st.players.length);
  } else {
    try { net.start(); } catch(e){}
  }
}
function mpStartWithBots(count){
  if(mpBotStarting || count <= 0) return;
  mpBotStarting = true;
  var sb = document.getElementById('mpstartbtn');
  if(sb){ sb.disabled = true; sb.textContent = '🎮 ADDING PLAYERS...'; }
  var need = count, done = 0, failed = 0;
  for(var i=0;i<count;i++){
    mpAddBot(function(err){
      done++;
      if(err) failed++;
      if(done >= need){
        mpBotStarting = false;
        if(failed > 0) mpShowErr('mplobbyerr', 'Could not add ' + failed + ' bot(s). Try again.');
        // re-render: enables START (or auto-starts when all ready)
        if(net && net.state){ try { mpRenderLobby(net.state); } catch(e){} }
      }
    });
  }
}
/* Open one bot connection to the current lobby. Bots never reconnect and
   never touch localStorage (their token would clobber the host's). */
function mpAddBot(cb){
  var code = (net && net.code) ? net.code : null;
  if(!code){ if(cb) cb(new Error('no lobby code')); return; }
  var name = BOT_NAMES[mpBots.length % BOT_NAMES.length];
  var bnet = new FireBabysNet({ server: MP_SERVER });
  bnet.moveHz = 8; // bots need less bandwidth than humans
  var origOnMsg = bnet._onMessage;
  bnet._onMessage = function(data, resolve, reject, settle){
    var hadLS = false, origSet = null;
    try {
      if(typeof localStorage !== 'undefined' && localStorage.setItem){
        hadLS = true; origSet = localStorage.setItem;
        localStorage.setItem = function(k, v){
          if(String(k).indexOf('fbmp:') === 0) return; // keep host token intact
          return origSet.call(localStorage, k, v);
        };
      }
      origOnMsg.call(bnet, data, resolve, reject, settle);
    } finally {
      if(hadLS){ try { localStorage.setItem = origSet; } catch(e){} }
    }
  };
  var bot = { net: bnet, name: name, id: null, team: 0,
    x: 0, z: 40, dir: 0, hp: 5, ko: false, koTimer: 0,
    atkCd: 1 + Math.random()*2, atkAnimT: 0, blockUntil: 0,
    jumpY: 0, jumpV: 0, jumps: 0,
    wx: 0, wz: 40, wanderT: 0 };
  mpBots.push(bot);
  bnet.connect(code, { name: name }).then(function(res){
    bnet.shouldReconnect = false; // never auto-reconnect bots
    bot.id = res.playerId;
    try { bnet.setReady(true); } catch(e){}
    if(cb) cb(null, bot);
  }).catch(function(e){
    var ix = mpBots.indexOf(bot);
    if(ix >= 0) mpBots.splice(ix, 1);
    try { bnet.disconnect(); } catch(ee){}
    if(cb) cb(e || new Error('bot connect failed'));
  });
  bnet.shouldReconnect = false; // set early too — connect() flips it true
}
function mpClearBots(){
  for(var i=0;i<mpBots.length;i++){ try { mpBots[i].net.disconnect(); } catch(e){} }
  mpBots = [];
  mpBotStarting = false;
}
function mpSyncBotTeams(){
  if(!net || !net.state || !net.state.players) return;
  for(var i=0;i<mpBots.length;i++){
    var b = mpBots[i]; if(!b.id) continue;
    for(var j=0;j<net.state.players.length;j++){
      if(net.state.players[j].id === b.id){ b.team = net.state.players[j].team || 0; break; }
    }
  }
}
/* Host-side: apply an attack to bots in range. Bots are host-authoritative:
   damage/KO/block are decided here and broadcast via the bot's own
   connection, so every client renders them through the normal handlers. */
function mpBotHits(msg, ax, az){
  var isVersus = mpGameMode !== 'coop';
  var atkTeam = 0;
  if(msg.id === net.playerId) atkTeam = mpMyTeam;
  else if(remotePlayers[msg.id]) atkTeam = remotePlayers[msg.id].team || 0;
  var now = Date.now()/1000;
  for(var i=0;i<mpBots.length;i++){
    var b = mpBots[i];
    if(!b.id || b.ko || msg.id === b.id) continue;
    if(isVersus && atkTeam === b.team) continue; // no friendly fire
    if(Math.hypot(b.x - ax, b.z - az) > ATK_RANGE) continue;
    var rp = remotePlayers[b.id];
    // already blocking -> negate, tell attacker
    if(b.blockUntil && now < b.blockUntil){
      try { b.net.sendAction({ kind:'blocked', data:{ by: msg.id } }); } catch(e){}
      continue;
    }
    // 20% reactive block
    if(Math.random() < 0.20){
      b.blockUntil = now + 0.7;
      try { b.net.sendAction({ kind:'block', data:{ on: 1 } }); } catch(e){}
      if(rp){ rp.blocking = true; try { if(rp.shield) rp.shield.visible = true; } catch(e){} }
      try { b.net.sendAction({ kind:'blocked', data:{ by: msg.id } }); } catch(e){}
      continue;
    }
    // damage + knockback (attacker's weapon multiplies damage)
    var bwmult = 1;
    try { bwmult = (msg.data && typeof msg.data.wmult === 'number') ? Math.max(1, Math.min(5, msg.data.wmult)) : 1; } catch(e){}
    b.hp = Math.max(0, b.hp - bwmult);
    var ka = Math.atan2(b.x - ax, b.z - az);
    b.x = Math.max(-26, Math.min(26, b.x + Math.cos(ka)*1.6));
    b.z = Math.max(-108, Math.min(44, b.z + Math.sin(ka)*1.6));
    try { b.net.sendAction({ kind:'hit', data:{ by: msg.id, hp: b.hp } }); } catch(e){}
    if(rp){
      try { rp.group.userData.flash = 1; } catch(e){}
      try { if(rp.hpbar) rp.hpbar.set(b.hp/5, true); } catch(e){}
    }
    if(b.hp <= 0){
      b.ko = true; b.koTimer = 3.0;
      try { b.net.sendAction({ kind:'ko', data:{ by: msg.id } }); } catch(e){}
      // relayed 'ko' updates visuals + team KO counts on all clients (incl. host)
    }
  }
}
/* Host-side bot AI, runs every frame in the host's game loop. */
function mpBotTick(dt){
  var now = Date.now()/1000;
  for(var i=0;i<mpBots.length;i++){
    var b = mpBots[i];
    if(!b.id) continue;
    var rp = remotePlayers[b.id];
    // KO countdown -> respawn at team spawn
    if(b.ko){
      b.koTimer -= dt;
      if(b.koTimer <= 0){
        b.ko = false; b.hp = 5;
        b.x = (b.team === 0 ? -6 : 6); b.z = 40; b.dir = 0;
        b.jumpY = 0; b.jumpV = 0; b.jumps = 0;
        try { b.net.sendAction({ kind:'respawn', data:{ hp: 5 } }); } catch(e){}
      }
      continue;
    }
    if(mpGameMode === 'catch' && typeof mpBotTickCatch === 'function'){ mpBotTickCatch(b, dt); continue; }
    if(b.atkCd > 0) b.atkCd -= dt;
    // block expiry
    if(b.blockUntil && now >= b.blockUntil){
      b.blockUntil = 0;
      try { b.net.sendAction({ kind:'block', data:{ on: 0 } }); } catch(e){}
      if(rp){ rp.blocking = false; try { if(rp.shield) rp.shield.visible = false; } catch(e){} }
    }
    // find nearest enemy (humans + other bots)
    var ex = null, ez = null, bd = 1e9;
    if(typeof player !== 'undefined' && player && player.group && !mpKO){
      if(mpGameMode === 'coop' || mpMyTeam !== b.team){
        var pdx = player.group.position.x - b.x, pdz = player.group.position.z - b.z;
        var pd = Math.hypot(pdx, pdz);
        if(pd < bd){ bd = pd; ex = player.group.position.x; ez = player.group.position.z; }
      }
    }
    for(var id in remotePlayers){
      if(id === b.id) continue;
      var orp = remotePlayers[id];
      if(!orp || orp.ko || !orp.group) continue;
      if(mpGameMode !== 'coop' && orp.team === b.team) continue;
      var odx = orp.group.position.x - b.x, odz = orp.group.position.z - b.z;
      var od = Math.hypot(odx, odz);
      if(od < bd){ bd = od; ex = orp.group.position.x; ez = orp.group.position.z; }
    }
    var acted = false;
    // attack when in range
    if(ex !== null && bd <= ATK_RANGE*0.95 && b.atkCd <= 0 && !b.blockUntil){
      b.dir = Math.atan2(ex - b.x, ez - b.z);
      try {
        b.net.sendAction({ kind:'attack',
          x: Math.round(b.x*100), y: Math.round(b.z*100),
          data:{ dir: Math.round(b.dir*100) } });
      } catch(e){}
      b.atkCd = 0.9 + Math.random()*0.9; // medium difficulty pacing
      b.atkAnimT = 0.28;
      acted = true;
    }
    if(!acted){
      var tx, tz;
      if(ex !== null && bd > 1.4){
        tx = ex; tz = ez; // chase nearest enemy
      } else {
        b.wanderT -= dt; // wander when no enemy near
        if(b.wanderT <= 0){
          b.wanderT = 2 + Math.random()*3;
          b.wx = Math.max(-24, Math.min(24, b.x + (Math.random()-0.5)*22));
          b.wz = Math.max(-100, Math.min(40, b.z + (Math.random()-0.5)*22));
        }
        tx = b.wx; tz = b.wz;
      }
      var mx = tx - b.x, mz = tz - b.z, md = Math.hypot(mx, mz);
      if(md > 0.3){
        var step = Math.min(5.2*dt, md); // slightly slower than players
        b.x += mx/md*step; b.z += mz/md*step;
        b.dir = Math.atan2(mx, mz);
        if(Math.random() < dt*0.2 && b.jumps === 0){ // playful hop
          b.jumps = 1; b.jumpV = JUMP_V1;
          try { b.net.sendAction({ kind:'jump', data:{ n: 1 } }); } catch(e){}
        }
      }
    }
    // jump physics mirror
    if(b.jumpY > 0 || b.jumpV !== 0){
      b.jumpV -= JUMP_GRAV*dt; b.jumpY += b.jumpV*dt;
      if(b.jumpY <= 0){ b.jumpY = 0; b.jumpV = 0; b.jumps = 0; }
    }
    // host-side attack anim on the bot's rendered mesh
    if(b.atkAnimT > 0 && rp){
      b.atkAnimT -= dt;
      var ak = Math.sin(Math.max(0,(0.28-b.atkAnimT))/0.28*Math.PI);
      try { rp.group.scale.set(1+0.25*ak, 1-0.15*ak, 1+0.25*ak); } catch(e){}
      if(typeof fbSwingWeapon==='function') fbSwingWeapon(rp.group, ak);
      if(b.atkAnimT <= 0){ try { rp.group.scale.set(1,1,1); } catch(e){} if(typeof fbSwingWeapon==='function') fbSwingWeapon(rp.group, 0); }
    }
    b.x = Math.max(-26, Math.min(26, b.x));
    b.z = Math.max(-108, Math.min(44, b.z));
    // broadcast position (throttled to 8Hz by the lib) + host render target
    try { b.net.sendMove({ x: Math.round(b.x*100), y: Math.round(b.z*100), dir: Math.round(b.dir*100) }); } catch(e){}
    if(rp){ rp.targetX = b.x; rp.targetZ = b.z; rp.targetDir = b.dir*100; }
  }
}
/* Presence: remove ghost bots (their host is gone) on every client. */
function mpOnPresence(msg){
  if(msg && msg.t === 'left' && mpIsBotName(msg.name)){
    mpRemoveRemote(msg.id);
  }
}
/* Host migration: new host restarts bots for ghost slots so the game goes on. */
function mpOnHostChange(){
  if(!net || !net.isHost) return;
  if(mpInGame && !mpBots.length){
    var ghosts = [];
    for(var id in remotePlayers){
      var rp = remotePlayers[id];
      if(rp && mpIsBotName(rp.name)) ghosts.push(id);
    }
    if(ghosts.length){
      if(typeof toast === 'function') toast('👑 You are now host — restarting ' + ghosts.length + ' bots...');
      for(var g=0; g<ghosts.length; g++) mpRemoveRemote(ghosts[g]);
      for(var k=0; k<ghosts.length; k++){
        mpAddBot(function(){ /* join mid-game; team defaults server-side */ });
      }
    }
  }
}

/* ---------- game start ---------- */
function mpOnGameStart(msg){
  // SPLASH ONLY: force 'catch' mode. The server may not echo the mode back,
  // and the old 'coop' default disables the score pill, timer, and team logic.
  mpGameMode = 'catch';
  document.getElementById('mplobbyov').style.display = 'none';
  mpEnterGame();
}
function mpEnterGame(){
  // guard: clean up any stale remote players if re-entering (prevents duplicate name labels / stacked spawns)
  try{
    if(typeof remotePlayers!=='undefined'){
      for(var _re in remotePlayers){ try{ mpRemoveRemote(_re); }catch(_e){} }
    }
  }catch(_e){}
  mpInGame = true;
  mpScores = { team0: 0, team1: 0 };
  mpKOs = { team0: 0, team1: 0 };
  // TEAM BALANCE: if all players are on the same team (server didn't split),
  // force-split bots so SPLASH has actual enemies. Critical for versus modes.
  try{
    if(mpGameMode === 'catch' || mpGameMode === 'versus'){
      var allIds = [];
      if(net && net.playerId) allIds.push(net.playerId);
      for(var _bid in remotePlayers) allIds.push(_bid);
      // check if all on team 0
      var allTeam0 = true;
      for(var _i=0; _i<allIds.length; _i++){
        var _id = allIds[_i];
        var _t = (_id === net.playerId) ? mpMyTeam : 
                 (remotePlayers[_id] ? remotePlayers[_id].team : 0);
        if(_t !== 0) { allTeam0 = false; break; }
      }
      if(allTeam0 && allIds.length >= 2){
        // split: first half team 0, second half team 1
        // keep local player on team 0, assign alternating bots to team 1
        var botIds = [];
        for(var _j=0; _j<allIds.length; _j++){
          if(allIds[_j] !== net.playerId) botIds.push(allIds[_j]);
        }
        // assign every other bot to team 1 for balanced teams
        for(var _k=0; _k<botIds.length; _k++){
          if(_k % 2 === 1 && remotePlayers[botIds[_k]]){
            remotePlayers[botIds[_k]].team = 1;
          }
        }
      }
    }
  }catch(_e){}
  // reset combat + jump state
  mpHP = mpMaxHP; mpKO = false; mpBlocking = false; mpAtkCd = 0; mpAtkAnimT = 0;
  mpJumpY = 0; mpJumpV = 0; mpJumps = 0; mpFlipT = 0;
  // Hide all overlays, enter explore-like mode
  // (hide twice: immediately + after 1s, to win any race with delayed lobby UI updates)
  var _hideMpOverlays = function(){
    ['mpmenuov','mplobbyov','mpendov'].forEach(function(id){
      try{ document.getElementById(id).style.display = 'none'; }catch(e){}
    });
  };
  _hideMpOverlays();
  setTimeout(_hideMpOverlays, 1000);
  if(typeof modeEnter === 'function') modeEnter();
  gameMode = 'multiplayer';
  state = 'EXPLORE';

  // Spawn local squad (just the player, no AI followers in multiplayer)
  mpSpawnLocal();
  if(mpGameMode === 'catch' && player && player.group){
    try{ player.group.position.set(0, 1.05, mpMyTeam === 0 ? -5 : 29); }catch(e){}
  }
  mpEnsureShield();
  try {
    player.hpbar.set(1);
    player.group.rotation.z = 0; player.group.rotation.x = 0;
    player.group.scale.set(1,1,1);
  } catch(e){}
  mpShowCombat(true);
  // CATCH/SPLASH: attack becomes GRAB button (grab is proximity-based, button teaches the mechanic)
  if(mpGameMode === 'catch'){
    if(typeof mpCatchInit === 'function') mpCatchInit();
    var _hab = document.getElementById('mpatkbtn');
    if(_hab){
      _hab.style.display = '';
      _hab.textContent = '🤼';
      _hab.setAttribute('aria-label', 'Grab');
      _hab.title = 'Grab: stand next to an enemy with a teammate!';
      if(!_hab._grabW){
        _hab._grabW = true;
        _hab.addEventListener('pointerdown', function(e){
          e.preventDefault();
          try { if(typeof toast === 'function') toast('🤼 Stand next to an enemy WITH a teammate to grab them!'); } catch(e2){}
          try { if(typeof beep === 'function') beep(660, 0.08, 'square', 0.05); } catch(e2){}
        });
      }
    }
    var _hbb = document.getElementById('mpblkbtn'); if(_hbb) _hbb.style.display = 'none';
  } else {
    var _rab = document.getElementById('mpatkbtn');
    if(_rab){ _rab.style.display = ''; _rab.textContent = '🔥'; _rab.setAttribute('aria-label', 'Attack'); }
    var _rbb = document.getElementById('mpblkbtn'); if(_rbb) _rbb.style.display = '';
  }

  // Spawn remote players from lobby state (skip disconnected ghosts)
  if(net && net.state && net.state.players){
    net.state.players.forEach(function(p){
      if(p.id !== net.playerId && p.connected !== false) mpSpawnRemote(p);
    });
  }

  // Bots: sync teams from server snake draft, place at team spawns
  if(net.isHost && mpBots.length){
    mpSyncBotTeams();
    for(var bi=0; bi<mpBots.length; bi++){
      var bb = mpBots[bi];
      if(!bb.id) continue;
      bb.hp = 5; bb.ko = false; bb.koTimer = 0; bb.atkCd = 1 + Math.random()*2;
      bb.blockUntil = 0; bb.atkAnimT = 0;
      if(mpGameMode === 'catch'){ bb.x = 0; bb.z = (bb.team === 0 ? -5 : 29); }
      else { bb.x = (bb.team === 0 ? -6 : 6); bb.z = 40; }
      bb.dir = 0;
      bb.wx = bb.x; bb.wz = bb.z; bb.wanderT = 0;
      bb.jumpY = 0; bb.jumpV = 0; bb.jumps = 0;
      var brp = remotePlayers[bb.id];
      if(brp){
        try { brp.group.position.set(bb.x, 1.05, bb.z); } catch(e){}
        brp.targetX = bb.x; brp.targetZ = bb.z; brp.targetDir = 0;
        brp.hp = 5; brp.ko = false; brp.blocking = false;
        try { if(brp.hpbar) brp.hpbar.set(1); } catch(e){}
        try { brp.group.rotation.z = 0; } catch(e){}
      }
    }
  }

  // Objective banner
  if(mpGameMode === 'catch'){
    setObjective('🏃 SPLASH — grab enemies with a teammate, dunk them in your pool! Last team standing wins!');
  } else if(mpGameMode === 'coop'){
    setObjective('🤝 CO-OP — explore together! (fires are visual only in MP beta)');
  } else {
    var myColor = mpMyTeam === 0 ? 'BLUE' : 'RED';
    setObjective('⚔️ ' + mpGameMode.toUpperCase() + ' — you are TEAM ' + myColor + '!');
  }
  updateMpScoreboard();
}
function mpSpawnLocal(){
  // Reuse the standard squad creation but strip followers for MP clarity
  if(typeof createSquad === 'function') createSquad(0, 40);
  // Bronze knight armor on the local hero (fetched server-authoritative).
  try { __fbFetchArmor(); } catch(e){}
  // Silver armor overrides bronze if all 250 tasks are done.
  try {
    __fbLoadTasks();
    if (typeof __FB_SILVER_UNLOCKED !== 'undefined' && __FB_SILVER_UNLOCKED && player && player.group) {
      if (typeof addSilverArmor === 'function') addSilverArmor(player.group, 1);
    }
  } catch(e){}
  // Remove AI followers in multiplayer (they'd confuse remote sync)
  if(typeof followers !== 'undefined' && followers.length){
    followers.forEach(function(f){ if(f.group && f.group.parent) f.group.parent.remove(f.group); });
    followers = [];
  }
  // Also remove non-Blaze units from the units list (keep only player)
  if(typeof units !== 'undefined' && typeof player !== 'undefined' && player){
    for(var i=units.length-1;i>=0;i--){
      if(units[i] !== player && units[i].group && units[i].group.parent){
        units[i].group.parent.remove(units[i].group);
        units.splice(i,1);
      }
    }
  }
  // Hide the local player's own name tag in multiplayer (reduces clutter;
  // you know who you are). Remote players keep theirs.
  try{
    if(player && player.group){
      player.group.traverse(function(o){
        if(o && o.isSprite) o.visible = false;
      });
    }
  }catch(e){}
}
function mpSpawnRemote(p){
  if(remotePlayers[p.id] || !p) return;
  // TEAM BALANCE: if this is a CATCH/SPLASH game and all existing players are on
  // team 0, assign this newcomer to team 1 (alternating) so we always have enemies.
  // This runs on every join, so late-joining and rejoining bots get balanced.
  try{
    if(mpInGame && (mpGameMode === 'catch' || mpGameMode === 'versus') && p){
      var _c0 = 0, _c1 = 0;
      if(net && net.playerId){
        if((typeof mpMyTeam === 'number' ? mpMyTeam : 0) === 0) _c0++; else _c1++;
      }
      for(var _rid in remotePlayers){
        var _rr = remotePlayers[_rid];
        if(!_rr) continue;
        if((typeof _rr.team === 'number' ? _rr.team : 0) === 0) _c0++; else _c1++;
      }
      // count the newcomer as team 0 for now (server default)
      _c0++;
      if(_c1 === 0 && (_c0 + _c1) >= 2){
        // nobody on team 1 yet: put this bot on team 1 if it keeps balance
        // (alternate: even-numbered joins go to team 1)
        var _total = _c0 + _c1;
        if(_total % 2 === 0) p.team = 1;
      }
    }
  }catch(_e){}
  var isVersus = mpGameMode !== 'coop';
  var colorIdx = isVersus ? (p.team || 0) : (Object.keys(remotePlayers).length % COOP_COLORS.length);
  var spec;
  if(isVersus){
    var tc = TEAM_COLORS[p.team || 0];
    spec = { id:'mp_'+p.id.slice(0,6), name:p.name, body:tc.body, dark:tc.dark, core:tc.core, extra:null };
  } else {
    var c = COOP_COLORS[colorIdx % COOP_COLORS.length];
    spec = { id:'mp_'+p.id.slice(0,6), name:p.name, body:c, dark:'#6a2a00', core:'#ffd76a', extra:null };
  }
  try {
    var g = makeBaby(spec, 'eager', 1);
    g.userData.baseY = 1.55;
    var tag = nameTag(mpDisplayName(p.name).slice(0,14), isVersus ? (p.team===0 ? '#7ac9ff' : '#ff9a8a') : '#ffd76a');
    // offset tag height per-player so overlapping players' labels don't stack exactly
    var _tagOff = 0;
    try { for(var _ti=0; _ti<String(p.id).length; _ti++){ _tagOff += String(p.id).charCodeAt(_ti); } _tagOff = (_tagOff % 5) * 0.22; } catch(e){}
    tag.position.y = 2.9 + _tagOff;
    try { tag.userData.isNameTag = true; } catch(e){}
    g.add(tag);
    // combat: HP bar + block shield bubble
    var hb = null, sh = null;
    try {
      hb = makeHpBar(2.4); hb.position.y = 2.25; g.add(hb);
      sh = mpMakeShieldMesh(); g.add(sh);
    } catch(e){}
    // spawn near local player with slight offset
    var px = 0, pz = 40;
    try { if(player && player.group){ px = player.group.position.x; pz = player.group.position.z; } } catch(e){}
    var off = (Object.keys(remotePlayers).length + 1) * 3;
    g.position.set(px + off, 1.05, pz + off * 0.5);
    scene.add(g);
    fbAttachWeapon(g, 'stick'); /* default until their look arrives */
    remotePlayers[p.id] = {
      group: g, name: p.name, team: p.team || 0,
      targetX: g.position.x, targetZ: g.position.z, targetDir: 0,
      hp: 5, blocking: false, ko: false, atkAnimT: 0,
      shield: sh, hpbar: hb, jumpY: 0, jumpV: 0, jumps: 0, flipT: 0
    };
  } catch(e){ /* mesh creation failed — skip */ }
}
function mpRemoveRemote(id){
  var rp = remotePlayers[id];
  if(rp && rp.group && rp.group.parent) rp.group.parent.remove(rp.group);
  delete remotePlayers[id];
}

/* ---------- network -> game ---------- */
function mpOnPos(msg){
  if(!mpInGame || !msg || !msg.id) return;
  if(net && msg.id === net.playerId) return; // ignore own echo
  var rp = remotePlayers[msg.id];
  if(!rp){
    // unknown player — spawn them (mid-game join)
    var p = { id: msg.id, name: 'Player', team: 0 };
    if(net && net.state && net.state.players){
      for(var i=0;i<net.state.players.length;i++){
        if(net.state.players[i].id === msg.id){ p = net.state.players[i]; break; }
      }
    }
    mpSpawnRemote(p);
    rp = remotePlayers[msg.id];
    if(!rp) return;
  }
  // server clamps to ±5000; game world is ±26 x, -108..44 z — scale down
  rp.targetX = Math.max(-26, Math.min(26, (msg.x || 0) * 0.01));
  rp.targetZ = Math.max(-108, Math.min(44, (msg.y || 0) * 0.01));
  rp.targetDir = msg.dir || 0;
}
function mpOnAction(msg){
  if(!mpInGame || !msg) return;
  var isVersus = mpGameMode !== 'coop';
  // Versus scoring: "extinguish" actions count per team
  if(msg.kind === 'extinguish' && isVersus){
    var rp = remotePlayers[msg.id];
    var team = rp ? rp.team : mpMyTeam;
    if(team === 0) mpScores.team0++; else mpScores.team1++;
    updateMpScoreboard();
  }
  if(msg.kind === 'catch_dunk' && mpGameMode === 'catch'){
    if(!mpCatch) mpCatchInit();
    mpCatchApplyDunk(msg.team, msg.victim, msg.vteam, msg.x/100, msg.z/100);
    updateMpScoreboard();
    if(mpCatch.suddenDeath || mpCatchRemaining(0) === 0 || mpCatchRemaining(1) === 0) mpCatch.over = true;
    return;
  }
  // Show a small toast for remote extinguish actions
  if(msg.kind && typeof toast === 'function'){
    var rp2 = remotePlayers[msg.id];
    var nm = rp2 ? rp2.name : 'Someone';
    if(msg.kind === 'extinguish') toast('🔥 ' + nm + ' extinguished a fire!');
  }

  /* ----- combat ----- */
  if(msg.kind === 'attack'){
    var arp = remotePlayers[msg.id];
    if(arp && !arp.ko) arp.atkAnimT = 0.28;
    var ax = (msg.x||0)*0.01, az = (msg.y||0)*0.01;
    // host: apply damage to bots (host-authoritative bot HP)
    if(net.isHost && mpInGame && mpBots.length) mpBotHits(msg, ax, az);
    // hit detection: did THIS client get hit?
    if(msg.id === net.playerId || !arp || arp.ko || mpKO) return;
    if(!player || !player.group) return;
    var bp = player.group.position;
    if(Math.hypot(bp.x - ax, bp.z - az) > ATK_RANGE) return;
    if(isVersus && arp.team === mpMyTeam) return; // no friendly fire
    if(mpBlocking){
      // blocked! tell attacker so they see the knockback
      try { net.sendAction({ kind: 'blocked', data: { by: msg.id } }); } catch(e){}
      if(typeof beep === 'function') beep(880, 0.08, 'square', 0.05);
      if(typeof toast === 'function') toast('🛡️ BLOCKED!');
      return;
    }
    mpTakeHit(msg.id, ax, az, !isVersus, msg);
    return;
  }
  if(msg.kind === 'block'){
    var brp = remotePlayers[msg.id];
    if(brp){
      brp.blocking = !!(msg.data && msg.data.on);
      try { if(brp.shield) brp.shield.visible = brp.blocking; } catch(e){}
    }
    return;
  }
  if(msg.kind === 'armor'){
    // Opponent's bronze armor: show it on their fighter.
    var arp2 = remotePlayers[msg.id];
    if(arp2 && arp2.group && msg.data){
      var ra = {
        greaves: !!msg.data.g, gauntlets: !!msg.data.ga,
        helmet: !!msg.data.h, chestplate: !!msg.data.c,
      };
      arp2.bronzeArmor = ra;
      try { addBronzeArmor(arp2.group, 1, ra); } catch(e){}
    }
    return;
  }
  if(msg.kind === 'silver_armor'){
    // Opponent earned the full silver set: show it (overrides bronze).
    var srp = remotePlayers[msg.id];
    if(srp && srp.group){
      try { if (typeof addSilverArmor === 'function') addSilverArmor(srp.group, 1); } catch(e){}
      try { if (typeof toast === 'function' && srp.name) toast('🥈 ' + srp.name + ' wears SILVER ARMOR!'); } catch(e){}
    }
    return;
  }
  if(msg.kind === 'blocked'){
    // my attack was blocked — small knockback on me
    if(msg.data && msg.data.by === net.playerId){
      var bl = remotePlayers[msg.id];
      if(bl){
        var me = player.group.position;
        var dx = me.x - bl.group.position.x, dz = me.z - bl.group.position.z;
        var d = Math.hypot(dx, dz) || 1;
        me.x = Math.max(-26, Math.min(26, me.x + dx/d*0.8));
        me.z = Math.max(-108, Math.min(44, me.z + dz/d*0.8));
      }
      if(typeof toast === 'function') toast('🛡️ BLOCKED!');
    }
    return;
  }
  if(msg.kind === 'hit'){
    var hrp = remotePlayers[msg.id];
    if(hrp){
      hrp.hp = (msg.data && typeof msg.data.hp === 'number') ? msg.data.hp : Math.max(0, (hrp.hp||5)-1);
      try { if(hrp.hpbar) hrp.hpbar.set(hrp.hp/5, true); } catch(e){}
      try { hrp.group.userData.flash = 1; } catch(e){}
    }
    return;
  }
  if(msg.kind === 'ko'){
    var krp = remotePlayers[msg.id];
    if(krp && !krp.ko){
      krp.ko = true;
      try { krp.group.rotation.z = Math.PI/2*0.9; } catch(e){}
      fbExtinguishPoof(krp.group);
      if(isVersus){
        var byId = msg.data && msg.data.by;
        var atkTeam = (byId === net.playerId) ? mpMyTeam :
          (byId && remotePlayers[byId] ? remotePlayers[byId].team : 0);
        if(atkTeam === 0) mpKOs.team0++; else mpKOs.team1++;
        updateMpScoreboard();
      }
      if(typeof toast === 'function' && krp.name) toast('💀 ' + krp.name + ' got KO\'d!');
      // Stats: I got the KO.
      try {
        if(byId === net.playerId){ __FB_STATS.kills++; __fbReportStats(); if (typeof fbTaskEvent === 'function') fbTaskEvent('ko', 1); if (typeof __fbSeasonXP === 'function') __fbSeasonXP(25, 'ko'); }
      } catch(e){}
    }
    return;
  }
  if(msg.kind === 'respawn'){
    var rrp = remotePlayers[msg.id];
    if(rrp){
      rrp.ko = false; rrp.hp = 5;
      try { rrp.group.rotation.z = 0; } catch(e){}
      try { if(rrp.hpbar) rrp.hpbar.set(1); } catch(e){}
      fbReignite(rrp.group);
      fbDressRemote(msg.id);
    }
    return;
  }
  if(msg.kind === 'jump'){
    var jrp = remotePlayers[msg.id];
    if(jrp && !jrp.ko){
      var n = (msg.data && msg.data.n) || 1;
      jrp.jumpV = (n === 2) ? JUMP_V2 : JUMP_V1;
      jrp.jumps = n;
      if(n === 2) jrp.flipT = 0.45;
    }
    return;
  }
}
function updateMpScoreboard(){
  if(!mpInGame || mpGameMode === 'coop') return;
  var pill = document.getElementById('foepill');
  if(pill){
    pill.style.display = 'block'; // must override #foepill{display:none} in CSS
    if(mpGameMode === 'catch' && mpCatch){
      var r0 = mpCatchRemaining(0), r1 = mpCatchRemaining(1);
      var tsec = Math.max(0, Math.ceil(mpCatch.timer));
      var tstr = Math.floor(tsec/60) + ':' + ('0' + (tsec%60)).slice(-2);
      pill.innerHTML = '🏃 🔥 <b>' + r0 + '</b> — <b>' + r1 + '</b> 💧' +
        (mpCatch.suddenDeath ? ' ⚡ SD' : ' ⏱️ ' + tstr);
    } else {
      pill.innerHTML = '🔵 <b>' + mpKOs.team0 + '</b>💀 ' + mpScores.team0 + '🔥 — ' +
        mpScores.team1 + '🔥 <b>' + mpKOs.team1 + '</b>💀 🔴';
    }
  }
}

/* ---------- game -> network (called each frame) ---------- */
function mpSyncFrame(){
  if(!mpInGame || !net || !net.connected) return;
  try {
    if(player && player.group){
      var bp = player.group.position;
      // scale world coords up for network (server clamps ±5000)
      net.sendMove({
        x: Math.round(bp.x * 100),
        y: Math.round(bp.z * 100),
        dir: Math.round((player.group.rotation.y || 0) * 100)
      });
    }
  } catch(e){}
  // interpolate remote players toward their targets
  for(var id in remotePlayers){
    var rp = remotePlayers[id];
    var g = rp.group.position;
    var catchFrozen = (mpGameMode === 'catch' && mpCatch && mpCatch.caught[id]);
    var dx = rp.targetX - g.x, dz = rp.targetZ - g.z;
    var d = Math.hypot(dx, dz);
    if(d > 0.05 && !catchFrozen){
      var step = Math.min(d, d * 0.2 + 0.5); // smooth lerp
      g.x += dx / d * step; g.z += dz / d * step;
      rp.group.rotation.y = Math.atan2(dx, dz);
    } else {
      rp.group.rotation.y += (rp.targetDir * 0.01 - rp.group.rotation.y) * 0.1;
    }
    // bob animation
    try { updateBaby(rp.group, clock.elapsedTime, 0.016); } catch(e){}
    // remote attack scale-punch
    if(rp.atkAnimT > 0){
      rp.atkAnimT -= 0.016;
      var rk = Math.sin(Math.max(0,(0.28-rp.atkAnimT))/0.28*Math.PI);
      try { rp.group.scale.set(1+0.25*rk, 1-0.15*rk, 1+0.25*rk); } catch(e){}
      if(typeof fbSwingWeapon==='function') fbSwingWeapon(rp.group, rk);
      if(rp.atkAnimT <= 0){ try { rp.group.scale.set(1,1,1); } catch(e){} if(typeof fbSwingWeapon==='function') fbSwingWeapon(rp.group, 0); }
    }
    // remote jump physics
    if((rp.jumpY||0) > 0 || (rp.jumpV||0) !== 0){
      rp.jumpV = (rp.jumpV||0) - JUMP_GRAV*0.016;
      rp.jumpY = (rp.jumpY||0) + rp.jumpV*0.016;
      if(rp.jumpY <= 0){ rp.jumpY = 0; rp.jumpV = 0; rp.jumps = 0; }
    }
    if((rp.flipT||0) > 0){
      rp.flipT -= 0.016;
      try { rp.group.rotation.x = (0.45-rp.flipT)/0.45*Math.PI*2; } catch(e){}
      if(rp.flipT <= 0){ try { rp.group.rotation.x = 0; } catch(e){} }
    }
    try { rp.group.position.y += (rp.jumpY||0); } catch(e){}
    try { if(rp.hpbar) rp.hpbar.quaternion.copy(camera.quaternion); } catch(e){}
  }
}
/* ==================== SPLASH MODE ====================
   Jeff's game (2v2 up to 15v15): two teammates within 3.5 units of an
   enemy for 0.8s catch them (lifted, can't move). Carriers move at 55%
   speed and drag the victim to their OWN team's swimming pool.
   Teammates can rescue: 1 rescuer = carriers 50% slower, 2+ rescuers =
   carriers fully stopped, then the victim breaks free after 1.5s.
   Dunk the victim in your pool: they POOF into a steam cloud and are
   ELIMINATED (no respawns). Last team standing wins. No KO combat. */
var mpCatch = null;
var CATCH_GRAB_R = 3.5, CATCH_GRAB_T = 0.8, CATCH_POOL_R = 6;
var CATCH_TIME = 300;

function mpCatchPools(){ return [{x:0, z:0, team:0}, {x:0, z:34, team:1}]; }
function mpCatchPoolOf(team){ var z = mpCatchPools(); return z[team] || z[0]; }

function mpCatchInit(){
  mpCatch = { caught:{}, grabProg:{}, immunity:{}, eliminated:{}, timer:CATCH_TIME,
              over:false, suddenDeath:false, meshes:[], effects:[] };
  // hide story-biome clutter (dark slag walls) so the arena looks clean
  try {
    if(typeof scene !== 'undefined' && scene){
      scene.traverse(function(o){
        if(o && o.userData && o.userData.slagWall){ o.visible = false; }
      });
    }
  } catch(e){}
  var pools = mpCatchPools();
  for(var i=0;i<pools.length;i++){
    try{
      /* pool deck ring */
      var ring = new THREE.Mesh(
        new THREE.RingGeometry(CATCH_POOL_R-0.5, CATCH_POOL_R+0.9, 48),
        new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({ color:0xf2f8ff, transparent:true, opacity:0.9, side:THREE.DoubleSide }));
      ring.rotation.x = -Math.PI/2; ring.position.set(pools[i].x, 0.18, pools[i].z);
      ring.userData.poolRipple = true;
      scene.add(ring); mpCatch.meshes.push(ring);
      /* water */
      var water = new THREE.Mesh(
        new THREE.CircleGeometry(CATCH_POOL_R-0.5, 48),
        new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({ color:0x2e9fe8, transparent:true, opacity:0.85, side:THREE.DoubleSide }));
      water.rotation.x = -Math.PI/2; water.position.set(pools[i].x, 0.14, pools[i].z);
      water.userData.poolRipple = true;
      scene.add(water); mpCatch.meshes.push(water);
      /* inner sparkle */
      var deep = new THREE.Mesh(
        new THREE.CircleGeometry(CATCH_POOL_R-2.2, 48),
        new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({ color:0x9fdcff, transparent:true, opacity:0.5, side:THREE.DoubleSide }));
      deep.rotation.x = -Math.PI/2; deep.position.set(pools[i].x, 0.16, pools[i].z);
      deep.userData.poolRipple = true;
      scene.add(deep); mpCatch.meshes.push(deep);
    }catch(e){}
  }
}

function mpCatchCleanup(){
  if(mpCatch){
    if(mpCatch.meshes){ for(var i=0;i<mpCatch.meshes.length;i++){ try{ scene.remove(mpCatch.meshes[i]); }catch(e){} } }
    if(mpCatch.effects){ for(var j=0;j<mpCatch.effects.length;j++){ try{ scene.remove(mpCatch.effects[j].mesh); }catch(e){} } }
  }
  // restore story-biome walls
  try {
    if(typeof scene !== 'undefined' && scene){
      scene.traverse(function(o){
        if(o && o.userData && o.userData.slagWall){ o.visible = true; }
      });
    }
  } catch(e){}
  mpCatch = null;
}

/* every ACTIVE player with a live position: local + remotes (bots included) */
function mpCatchPlayers(){
  var out = [];
  try{
    if(typeof player !== 'undefined' && player && player.group && net && net.playerId &&
       !(mpCatch && mpCatch.eliminated[net.playerId]))
      out.push({ id: net.playerId, x: player.group.position.x, z: player.group.position.z,
                 team: (typeof mpMyTeam==='number'?mpMyTeam:0), name: 'you' });
    for(var id in remotePlayers){
      if(mpCatch && mpCatch.eliminated[id]) continue;
      var rp = remotePlayers[id];
      if(!rp || !rp.group || !rp.group.visible) continue;
      out.push({ id:id, x: rp.group.position.x, z: rp.group.position.z,
                 team: (typeof rp.team==='number'?rp.team:0), name: rp.name || '?' });
    }
  }catch(e){}
  return out;
}

function mpCatchGroupOf(id){
  if(net && id === net.playerId) return (typeof player!=='undefined' && player) ? player.group : null;
  var rp = remotePlayers[id]; return rp ? rp.group : null;
}

/* grey out a player's floating name tag */
function mpCatchGreyName(id){
  try{
    var g = mpCatchGroupOf(id);
    if(!g) return;
    g.traverse(function(o){
      if(o && o.isSprite && o.material){ o.material.color.setHex(0x8a8a8a); }
    });
  }catch(e){}
}

function mpCatchFree(vid){
  if(!mpCatch) return;
  delete mpCatch.caught[vid];
  delete mpCatch.grabProg[vid];
  try{
    var g = mpCatchGroupOf(vid);
    if(g && g.userData) g.userData.baseY = 1.05;
    // restore name tag visibility
    if(g) g.traverse(function(o){
      if(o && o.isSprite && o.userData && o.userData.isNameTag){ o.visible = true; }
    });
  }catch(e){}
}

/* local player's catch role: {caught, carrier, eliminated, mul} */
function mpCatchLocal(){
  var res = { caught:false, carrier:false, eliminated:false, mul:1 };
  if(!mpCatch || !net || !net.playerId) return res;
  var me = net.playerId;
  if(mpCatch.eliminated[me]){ res.eliminated = true; res.mul = 0; return res; }
  if(mpCatch.caught[me]){ res.caught = true; res.mul = 0; return res; }
  for(var vid in mpCatch.caught){
    var c = mpCatch.caught[vid];
    if(c.carriers[0] === me || c.carriers[1] === me){
      res.carrier = true;
      var r = c.rescuers || 0;
      res.mul = 0.55 * (r === 1 ? 0.5 : 1) * (r >= 2 ? 0 : 1);
      return res;
    }
  }
  return res;
}

function mpCatchRemaining(team){
  var n = 0;
  try{
    if(typeof player !== 'undefined' && player && player.group && net && net.playerId &&
       (typeof mpMyTeam==='number'?mpMyTeam:0) === team && !(mpCatch && mpCatch.eliminated[net.playerId])) n++;
    for(var id in remotePlayers){
      var rp = remotePlayers[id];
      if(!rp || !rp.group) continue;
      if((typeof rp.team==='number'?rp.team:0) === team && !(mpCatch && mpCatch.eliminated[id])) n++;
    }
  }catch(e){}
  return n;
}

/* big cartoon steam poof at x,z */
function mpCatchPoof(x, z){
  try{
    var grp = new THREE.Group();
    for(var i=0;i<7;i++){
      var s = 1.2 + Math.random()*1.8;
      var puff = new THREE.Mesh(
        new THREE.SphereGeometry(s, 12, 10),
        new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({ color:0xffffff, transparent:true, opacity:0.85 }));
      puff.position.set((Math.random()-0.5)*4, 1+Math.random()*3, (Math.random()-0.5)*4);
      grp.add(puff);
    }
    grp.position.set(x, 0, z);
    scene.add(grp);
    if(mpCatch) mpCatch.effects.push({ mesh:grp, t:0 });
    if(typeof beep === 'function'){ beep(300, 0.25, 'sine', 0.12); setTimeout(function(){ beep(150, 0.35, 'sine', 0.1); }, 120); }
  }catch(e){}
}

function mpCatchTick(dt){
  if(!mpCatch || mpCatch.over || !mpInGame || !net) return;
  var now = (typeof clock !== 'undefined') ? clock.elapsedTime : 0;
  /* TEAM BALANCE ENFORCER (throttled): if all players ended up on team 0
     (server didn't split, bots joined late, or rejoin cleared state),
     force-split so SPLASH always has two opposing teams. */
  try{
    if(!mpCatch._tbNext || now >= mpCatch._tbNext){
      mpCatch._tbNext = now + 3;
      var _t0 = 0, _t1 = 0, _ids = [];
      if(net && net.playerId){
        _ids.push(net.playerId);
        if((typeof mpMyTeam === 'number' ? mpMyTeam : 0) === 0) _t0++; else _t1++;
      }
      for(var _rid in remotePlayers){
        var _rr = remotePlayers[_rid];
        if(!_rr || !_rr.group) continue;
        _ids.push(_rid);
