        if((typeof _rr.team === 'number' ? _rr.team : 0) === 0) _t0++; else _t1++;
      }
      if(_ids.length >= 2 && (_t0 === 0 || _t1 === 0)){
        // all on one team: split bots, keep local player where they are
        var _bots = [];
        for(var _bi = 0; _bi < _ids.length; _bi++){
          if(_ids[_bi] !== net.playerId) _bots.push(_ids[_bi]);
        }
        var _targetT1 = Math.ceil(_bots.length / 2);
        var _assigned = 0;
        for(var _bj = 0; _bj < _bots.length && _assigned < _targetT1; _bj++){
          // assign every other bot to team 1 for a balanced split
          if(_bj % 2 === 1 && remotePlayers[_bots[_bj]]){
            remotePlayers[_bots[_bj]].team = 1;
            _assigned++;
          }
        }
        // safety: if still none on team 1 (e.g. 1 bot), force the first bot over
        if(_assigned === 0 && _bots.length > 0 && remotePlayers[_bots[0]]){
          remotePlayers[_bots[0]].team = 1;
        }
      }
    }
  }catch(_e){}
  /* pool ripple */
  try{
    for(var mi=0; mi<mpCatch.meshes.length; mi++){
      var mm = mpCatch.meshes[mi];
      if(mm && mm.userData && mm.userData.poolRipple)
        mm.material.opacity = 0.55 + 0.3 * Math.abs(Math.sin(now * 2.4 + mm.position.x));
    }
  }catch(e){}
  /* steam poof effects */
  try{
    for(var ei=mpCatch.effects.length-1; ei>=0; ei--){
      var ef = mpCatch.effects[ei];
      ef.t += dt;
      var k = ef.t / 1.4;
      ef.mesh.scale.setScalar(1 + k*2.2);
      ef.mesh.position.y = k * 3;
      ef.mesh.traverse(function(o){ if(o && o.material) o.material.opacity = Math.max(0, 0.85 * (1-k)); });
      if(k >= 1){ scene.remove(ef.mesh); mpCatch.effects.splice(ei,1); }
    }
  }catch(e){}
  /* match timer */
  if(!mpCatch.suddenDeath){
    mpCatch.timer -= dt;
    if(mpCatch.timer <= 0){
      var r0 = mpCatchRemaining(0), r1 = mpCatchRemaining(1);
      if(r0 !== r1){
        mpCatch.over = true;
        if(net.isHost){ try{ net.end({results:{note:'🏃 SPLASH — time!'}}); }catch(e2){} }
        return;
      }
      mpCatch.suddenDeath = true;
      if(typeof toast === 'function') toast('⚡ SUDDEN DEATH — next elimination wins!');
    }
  }
  var plist = mpCatchPlayers();
  var byId = {}; for(var i=0;i<plist.length;i++) byId[plist[i].id] = plist[i];
  /* grab detection */
  for(var vi=0; vi<plist.length; vi++){
    var V = plist[vi];
    if(mpCatch.caught[V.id]) continue;
    if(mpCatch.immunity[V.id] && now < mpCatch.immunity[V.id]) continue;
    var atk = [];
    for(var ai=0; ai<plist.length; ai++){
      var A = plist[ai];
      if(A.id === V.id || A.team === V.team) continue;
      if(Math.hypot(A.x - V.x, A.z - V.z) <= CATCH_GRAB_R) atk.push(A);
    }
    if(atk.length >= 2){
      var gp = mpCatch.grabProg[V.id] || (mpCatch.grabProg[V.id] = {t:0});
      gp.t += dt;
      if(gp.t >= CATCH_GRAB_T){
        atk.sort(function(a,b){
          return Math.hypot(a.x-V.x, a.z-V.z) - Math.hypot(b.x-V.x, b.z-V.z);
        });
        mpCatch.caught[V.id] = { carriers:[atk[0].id, atk[1].id], team: atk[0].team, stopT:0, rescuers:0 };
        delete mpCatch.grabProg[V.id];
        try{
          var vg0 = mpCatchGroupOf(V.id);
          if(vg0 && vg0.userData) vg0.userData.baseY = 2.3;
          // hide victim's name tag while carried to avoid label stacking with carrier
          if(vg0) vg0.traverse(function(o){
            if(o && o.isSprite && o.userData && o.userData.isNameTag){ o.visible = false; }
          });
        }catch(e2){}
        if(typeof toast === 'function') toast('🙌 ' + mpDisplayName(V.name) + ' got CAUGHT!');
        if(typeof beep === 'function') beep(440, 0.12, 'square', 0.07);
      }
    } else delete mpCatch.grabProg[V.id];
  }
  /* carried victims: validate carriers, rescue tug-of-war, pool dunk */
  for(var vid in mpCatch.caught){
    var c = mpCatch.caught[vid];
    var Vp = byId[vid];
    var c0 = byId[c.carriers[0]], c1 = byId[c.carriers[1]];
    var c0ok = c0 && !mpCatch.caught[c.carriers[0]] && !mpCatch.eliminated[c.carriers[0]] &&
               Math.hypot(c0.x-Vp.x, c0.z-Vp.z) <= 9;
    var c1ok = c1 && !mpCatch.caught[c.carriers[1]] && !mpCatch.eliminated[c.carriers[1]] &&
               Math.hypot(c1.x-Vp.x, c1.z-Vp.z) <= 9;
    if(!Vp || !c0ok || !c1ok){ mpCatchFree(vid); continue; }
    var rescuers = 0;
    for(var ri=0; ri<plist.length; ri++){
      var R = plist[ri];
      if(R.id === vid || R.team !== Vp.team) continue;
      if(Math.hypot(R.x-Vp.x, R.z-Vp.z) <= CATCH_GRAB_R) rescuers++;
    }
    c.rescuers = rescuers;
    if(rescuers >= 2){
      c.stopT += dt;
      if(c.stopT >= 1.5){
        mpCatchFree(vid);
        mpCatch.immunity[vid] = now + 2;
        if(typeof toast === 'function') toast('💪 ' + mpDisplayName(Vp.name) + ' was RESCUED!');
        continue;
      }
    } else c.stopT = 0;
    var mx = (c0.x + c1.x) / 2, mz = (c0.z + c1.z) / 2;
    try{
      var vg = mpCatchGroupOf(vid);
      if(vg){ vg.position.x = mx; vg.position.z = mz; }
    }catch(e2){}
    if(net.isHost){
      var pool = mpCatchPoolOf(c.team);
      if(Math.hypot(mx - pool.x, mz - pool.z) <= CATCH_POOL_R) mpCatchDoDunk(c.team, vid, Vp.team, mx, mz);
    }
  }
  updateMpScoreboard();
}

function mpCatchDoDunk(team, vid, vteam, x, z){
  if(!mpCatch || mpCatch.over || mpCatch.eliminated[vid]) return;
  try{ net.sendAction({ kind:'catch_dunk', team:team, victim:vid, vteam:vteam, x:Math.round(x*100), z:Math.round(z*100) }); }catch(e){}
  mpCatchApplyDunk(team, vid, vteam, x, z);
  var r0 = mpCatchRemaining(0), r1 = mpCatchRemaining(1);
  if(r0 === 0 || r1 === 0 || mpCatch.suddenDeath){
    mpCatch.over = true;
    var winner = r0 === 0 ? 1 : 0;
    try{ net.end({results:{note:'🏃 SPLASH — ' + (winner===0 ? '🔵 BLUE' : '🔴 RED') + ' team wins!'}}); }catch(e2){}
  }
}

function mpCatchApplyDunk(team, vid, vteam, x, z){
  if(!mpCatch || mpCatch.eliminated[vid]) return;
  mpCatch.eliminated[vid] = true;
  mpCatchFree(vid);
  mpCatchPoof(x, z);
  try{
    var g = mpCatchGroupOf(vid);
    if(g) g.visible = false;
  }catch(e){}
  mpCatchGreyName(vid);
  /* host parks eliminated bots */
  try{
    if(net && net.isHost){
      for(var i=0;i<mpBots.length;i++){
        if(mpBots[i].id === vid){ mpBots[i].eliminated = true; break; }
      }
    }
  }catch(e2){}
  var nm = '?';
  try{
    if(net && vid === net.playerId) nm = 'you';
    else { var rp = remotePlayers[vid]; nm = mpDisplayName(rp ? rp.name : '?'); }
  }catch(e3){}
  if(typeof toast === 'function') toast('💨 POOF! ' + nm + ' is OUT!');
  updateMpScoreboard();
}

/* bot AI for CATCH: chase, gang-grab, carry to pool, rescue teammates */
function mpBotTickCatch(b, dt){
  if(!mpCatch || b.eliminated) return;
  if(mpCatch.caught[b.id]) return; /* I'm caught: stay put */
  if(mpCatch.eliminated[b.id]) return;
  var plist = mpCatchPlayers();
  var byId = {}; for(var i=0;i<plist.length;i++) byId[plist[i].id] = plist[i];
  var carrying = null;
  for(var vid in mpCatch.caught){
    var cc = mpCatch.caught[vid];
    if(cc.carriers[0] === b.id || cc.carriers[1] === b.id){ carrying = cc; break; }
  }
  var tx = null, tz = null, smul = 1;
  if(carrying){
    var pool = mpCatchPoolOf(carrying.team);
    tx = pool.x; tz = pool.z;
    var r = carrying.rescuers || 0;
    smul = 0.55 * (r === 1 ? 0.5 : 1) * (r >= 2 ? 0 : 1);
  } else {
    var bv = null, bd = 1e9;
    for(var vid2 in mpCatch.caught){
      var Vp = byId[vid2];
      if(!Vp || Vp.team !== b.team) continue;
      var d = Math.hypot(Vp.x - b.x, Vp.z - b.z);
      if(d < bd){ bd = d; bv = Vp; }
    }
    if(bv){ tx = bv.x; tz = bv.z; }
    else {
      var ex = null, ez = null, ed = 1e9;
      for(var pi=0; pi<plist.length; pi++){
        var P = plist[pi];
        if(P.id === b.id || P.team === b.team) continue;
        if(mpCatch.caught[P.id]) continue;
        var pd = Math.hypot(P.x - b.x, P.z - b.z);
        if(pd < ed){ ed = pd; ex = P.x; ez = P.z; }
      }
      if(ex !== null){ tx = ex; tz = ez; }
      else {
        b.wanderT -= dt;
        if(b.wanderT <= 0){
          b.wanderT = 2 + Math.random()*3;
          b.wx = Math.max(-24, Math.min(24, b.x + (Math.random()-0.5)*22));
          b.wz = Math.max(-100, Math.min(40, b.z + (Math.random()-0.5)*22));
        }
        tx = b.wx; tz = b.wz;
      }
    }
  }
  if(tx !== null){
    var mx = tx - b.x, mz = tz - b.z, md = Math.hypot(mx, mz);
    // stop at 1.5 units to avoid overlapping/stacking on top of targets
    if(md > 1.5){
      var step = Math.min(5.2 * smul * dt, md - 1.5);
      b.x += mx / md * step; b.z += mz / md * step;
      b.dir = Math.atan2(mx, mz);
      b.x = Math.max(-26, Math.min(26, b.x));
      b.z = Math.max(-108, Math.min(44, b.z));
    }
  }
}
/* ================== END SPLASH MODE ================== */

/* Public: send an extinguish action (call from game code when player extinguishes) */
function mpSendExtinguish(x, z){
  if(!mpInGame || !net) return;
  try {
    net.sendAction({ kind: 'extinguish', x: Math.round(x*100), y: Math.round(z*100) });
    if(typeof fbTaskEvent === 'function'){ fbTaskEvent('extinguish', 1); if(mpGameMode !== 'coop') fbTaskEvent('extinguish_versus', 1); }
    if(mpGameMode !== 'coop'){
      if(mpMyTeam === 0) mpScores.team0++; else mpScores.team1++;
      updateMpScoreboard();
    }
  } catch(e){}
}

/* ---------- game end / exit ---------- */
function mpOnGameEnd(msg){
  mpExitGame();
  // show results
  var stats = '';
  if(mpGameMode === 'catch'){
    stats = '🏃 SPLASH — last team standing wins!';
  } else if(mpGameMode !== 'coop'){
    var w = mpScores.team0 > mpScores.team1 ? '🔵 BLUE TEAM WINS!' :
            mpScores.team1 > mpScores.team0 ? '🔴 RED TEAM WINS!' : "🤝 IT'S A TIE!";
    stats = w + '<br>Blue: ' + mpScores.team0 + ' — Red: ' + mpScores.team1;
  } else {
    stats = '🤝 Co-op session complete!<br>Thanks for playing together!';
  }
  if(msg && msg.results && msg.results.note) stats += '<br>' + escapeHtml(msg.results.note);
  document.getElementById('mpendstats').innerHTML = stats;
  document.getElementById('mpendov').style.display = 'flex';
  wireOnce('mpendlobbybtn', function(){
    document.getElementById('mpendov').style.display = 'none';
    document.getElementById('mplobbyov').style.display = 'flex';
    if(net) net.setReady(false);
    mpReady = false; updateReadyBtn();
  });
  wireOnce('mpendhubbtn', function(){
    document.getElementById('mpendov').style.display = 'none';
    mpCleanupNet();
    if(typeof showHub === 'function') showHub();
  });
}
function mpExitGame(){
  mpShowCombat(false);
  if(typeof mpCatchCleanup === 'function') mpCatchCleanup();
  var _cab = document.getElementById('mpatkbtn'); if(_cab) _cab.style.display = '';
  var _cbb = document.getElementById('mpblkbtn'); if(_cbb) _cbb.style.display = '';
  mpInGame = false;
  mpClearBots();
  mpBlocking = false; mpKO = false;
  // remove remote players
  for(var id in remotePlayers) mpRemoveRemote(id);
  remotePlayers = {};
  // restore foepill
  var pill = document.getElementById('foepill');
  if(pill) pill.style.display = 'none';
  // respawn normal squad for single-player
  if(typeof createSquad === 'function' && player){
    try { createSquad(0, 40); } catch(e){}
  }
}

/* ---------- hook into game loop ---------- */
// Patch updateExplore to call mpSyncFrame when in multiplayer mode
var _origUpdateExplore = null;
function mpPatchGameLoop(){
  if(_origUpdateExplore || typeof updateExplore !== 'function') return;
  _origUpdateExplore = updateExplore;
  updateExplore = function(dt, t){
    // In multiplayer: skip story triggers, random encounters, followers
    if(gameMode === 'multiplayer'){
      mpUpdateExplore(dt, t);
      mpSyncFrame();
      return;
    }
    _origUpdateExplore(dt, t);
    spPostFrame(dt); // single-player combat: jump/attack-anim/cooldowns
  };
}
function mpUpdateExplore(dt, t){
  // Simplified explore: movement + camera, no story/encounters/followers
  // --- combat timers ---
  if(mpKO){
    mpKOTimer -= dt;
    if(mpKOTimer <= 0) mpRespawn();
  }
  // --- host bot AI ---
  if(net && net.isHost && mpBots.length){ mpBotAcc=(mpBotAcc||0)+dt; var _bh=1/((typeof FBQ!=='undefined'&&FBQ.botHz)||20); if(mpBotAcc>=_bh){ mpBotTick(mpBotAcc); mpBotAcc=0; } }
  if(mpAtkCd > 0) mpAtkCd -= dt;
  if(mpAtkAnimT > 0){
    mpAtkAnimT -= dt;
    var ak = Math.sin(Math.max(0,(0.28-mpAtkAnimT))/0.28*Math.PI);
    try { player.group.scale.set(1+0.25*ak, 1-0.15*ak, 1+0.25*ak); } catch(e){}
    if(typeof fbSwingWeapon==='function') fbSwingWeapon(player.group, ak);
    if(mpAtkAnimT <= 0){ try { player.group.scale.set(1,1,1); } catch(e){} if(typeof fbSwingWeapon==='function') fbSwingWeapon(player.group, 0); }
  }
  // --- jump physics ---
  if(mpJumpY > 0 || mpJumpV !== 0){
    mpJumpV -= JUMP_GRAV*dt;
    mpJumpY += mpJumpV*dt;
    if(mpJumpY <= 0){ mpJumpY = 0; mpJumpV = 0; mpJumps = 0; }
  }
  if(mpFlipT > 0){
    mpFlipT -= dt;
    try { player.group.rotation.x = (0.45-mpFlipT)/0.45*Math.PI*2; } catch(e){}
    if(mpFlipT <= 0){ try { player.group.rotation.x = 0; } catch(e){} }
  }

  if(mpGameMode === 'catch' && typeof mpCatchTick === 'function'){ try{ mpCatchTick(dt); }catch(e){} }
  var mv = (typeof moveInput === 'function') ? moveInput() : {on:false,x:0,z:0};
  var bp = player.group.position;
  var catchMul = 1, catchHeld = false;
  if(mpGameMode === 'catch' && mpCatch && typeof mpCatchLocal === 'function'){
    try{ var lr = mpCatchLocal(); catchMul = lr.mul; catchHeld = lr.caught || lr.eliminated; }catch(e){}
  }
  if(mv.on && !mpKO && !catchHeld){
    var sp = (mpBlocking ? 3.75 : 7.5) * catchMul * dt; // blocking slows 50%; SPLASH carry/rescue slows
    var nx = bp.x + mv.x*sp, nz = bp.z + mv.z*sp;
    nx = Math.max(-26, Math.min(26, nx)); nz = Math.max(-108, Math.min(44, nz));
    if(typeof resolveColliders === 'function'){
      var rc = resolveColliders(player, nx, nz);
      bp.x = rc.x; bp.z = rc.z;
    } else { bp.x = nx; bp.z = nz; }
    player.group.rotation.y = Math.atan2(mv.x, mv.z);
  }
  try { updateBaby(player.group, t, dt); } catch(e){}
  try { player.group.position.y += mpJumpY; } catch(e){} // jump offset on top of bob
  try { player.hpbar.quaternion.copy(camera.quaternion); } catch(e){}
  // follow camera
  var cx = bp.x*0.55, cz = bp.z + 13.5*camDistMul, cy = 10.5*camDistMul;
  camera.position.lerp(new THREE.Vector3(cx,cy,cz), 1-Math.pow(0.01,dt));
  camera.lookAt(bp.x*0.6, 1.5, bp.z-5);
  // stage pill
  try {
    document.getElementById('stagepill').textContent = '🌐 ' +
      (mpGameMode === 'coop' ? 'CO-OP' : (mpGameMode === 'catch' ? 'SPLASH' : mpGameMode.toUpperCase()));
  } catch(e){}
  // SPLASH arena lighting: bright warm fog so the match stays readable
  // (the open-world biome fog at the old field was nearly black-red)
  if(mpGameMode === 'catch' && mpInGame && typeof scene !== 'undefined' && scene.fog){
    try{ scene.fog.color.lerp(new THREE.Color(0xffd9a8), 1-Math.pow(0.2,dt)); }catch(e){}
  }
}

/* ---------- init ---------- */
function mpInit(){
  window._mpInitDone = true;
  // Set FBMultiplayer FIRST so deep-link flow never waits forever,
  // even if a patch step below throws.
  try{
    window.FBMultiplayer = {
      start: startMultiplayer, leave: mpLeaveLobby,
      sendExtinguish: mpSendExtinguish, net: function(){ return net; }
    };
  }catch(e){}
  try{ mpPatchGameLoop(); }catch(e){ try{ console.warn('mpPatchGameLoop failed:', e); }catch(ee){} }
  try{ mpCombatInit(); }catch(e){ try{ console.warn('mpCombatInit failed:', e); }catch(ee){} }
  try{ spPatchDealDamage(); }catch(e){}   // block negates damage in single-player
  try{ spPatchBattleLoops(); }catch(e){}  // jump/anim in battle + rescue loops
  // Patch renderHub to add our button after each render
  try{
    if(typeof renderHub === 'function' && !renderHub._mpPatched){
      var _origRenderHub = renderHub;
      renderHub = function(){ _origRenderHub(); mpAddHubButton(); };
      renderHub._mpPatched = true;
    }
  }catch(e){}
  // Combat buttons visibility: every 250ms, show in ANY active gameplay mode
  try{
    if(!window._spCombatUITimer){
      window._spCombatUITimer = true;
      setInterval(function(){ try { spRefreshUI(); } catch(e){} }, 250);
    }
  }catch(e){}
}

// Auto-init when game scripts are ready (init() has run)
var _mpInitTries = 0;
var _mpInitTimer = setInterval(function(){
  _mpInitTries++;
  if(typeof renderHub === 'function' && typeof updateExplore === 'function'){
    clearInterval(_mpInitTimer);
    try { mpInit(); } catch(e){ console.warn('MP init failed:', e); }
  } else if(_mpInitTries > 100){
    clearInterval(_mpInitTimer);
  }
}, 100);

/* ----- Season 1 state (localStorage, fbSeason1_* namespace) ----- */
var __fbSeason1 = { xp: 0, claimed: {}, badges: [] };
var __FB_SEASON_BOOST = 0;
var __fbSeasonInitDone = false;

function __fbLoadSeason(){
  if (__fbSeasonInitDone) return;
  __fbSeasonInitDone = true;
  try {
    var xp = parseInt(localStorage.getItem('fbSeason1_xp'), 10);
    if (!isNaN(xp) && xp >= 0) __fbSeason1.xp = xp;
    var cl = localStorage.getItem('fbSeason1_claimed');
    if (cl) { var c = JSON.parse(cl); if (c) __fbSeason1.claimed = c; }
    var bg = localStorage.getItem('fbSeason1_badges');
    if (bg) { var b = JSON.parse(bg); if (b && b.length !== undefined) __fbSeason1.badges = b; }
    var bo = parseFloat(localStorage.getItem('fbSeason1_boost'));
    if (!isNaN(bo) && bo > 0) __FB_SEASON_BOOST = bo;
  } catch(e){}
  /* merge server profile in background (defensive: endpoint may not exist yet) */
  try { __fbFetchSeason(); } catch(e){}
}

function __fbSaveSeason(){
  try {
    localStorage.setItem('fbSeason1_xp', String(__fbSeason1.xp));
    localStorage.setItem('fbSeason1_claimed', JSON.stringify(__fbSeason1.claimed));
    localStorage.setItem('fbSeason1_badges', JSON.stringify(__fbSeason1.badges));
    localStorage.setItem('fbSeason1_boost', String(__FB_SEASON_BOOST));
  } catch(e){}
}

/* current tier: 1..20 (tier N reward unlocks at N*100 XP) */
function __fbSeasonTier(){
  __fbLoadSeason();
  var t = Math.floor(__fbSeason1.xp / FB_SEASON1.xpPerTier) + 1;
  if (t < 1) t = 1;
  if (t > FB_SEASON1.tiers) t = FB_SEASON1.tiers;
  return t;
}

/* award season XP; auto-claims newly unlocked tier rewards */
function __fbSeasonXP(n, why){
  try {
    __fbLoadSeason();
    n = n || 0;
    if (n <= 0) return;
    var before = Math.floor(__fbSeason1.xp / FB_SEASON1.xpPerTier);
    __fbSeason1.xp += n;
    var after = Math.floor(__fbSeason1.xp / FB_SEASON1.xpPerTier);
    if (after > before) {
      for (var t = before + 1; t <= after && t <= FB_SEASON1.tiers; t++) {
        __fbClaimSeasonTier(t);
      }
      try { __fbSyncSeason(); } catch(e){}
      try { __fbRefreshSeasonUI(); } catch(e){}
      try { __fbRefreshVaultUI(); } catch(e){}
    }
    __fbSaveSeason();
  } catch(e){}
}

/* grant one tier reward (idempotent) */
function __fbSeasonGrant(r){
  try {
    var sty = (typeof window.FireBabysStyle !== 'undefined') ? window.FireBabysStyle : null;
    if (r.t === 'coins') {
      if (sty && typeof sty.addCoins === 'function') sty.addCoins(r.n);
    } else if (r.t === 'gems') {
      if (sty && typeof sty.addGems === 'function') sty.addGems(r.n);
    } else if (r.t === 'emote') {
      if (sty && typeof sty.grantEmote === 'function') sty.grantEmote(r.id);
    } else if (r.t === 'badge') {
      if (__fbSeason1.badges.indexOf(r.id) < 0) __fbSeason1.badges.push(r.id);
    } else if (r.t === 'boost') {
      __FB_SEASON_BOOST = Math.round((__FB_SEASON_BOOST + r.n) * 10000) / 10000;
    } else if (r.t === 'crown') {
      if (sty && typeof sty.addGems === 'function') sty.addGems(r.gems);
      __FB_SEASON_BOOST = Math.round((__FB_SEASON_BOOST + r.boost) * 10000) / 10000;
      if (__fbSeason1.badges.indexOf('ignition_crown') < 0) __fbSeason1.badges.push('ignition_crown');
    }
    /* a weapon unlock can ride along with any reward type */
    if (r.weapon && typeof __fbUnlockWeapon === 'function') { try { __fbUnlockWeapon(r.weapon); } catch(e){} }
  } catch(e){}
}

function __fbClaimSeasonTier(tier){
  try {
    if (__fbSeason1.claimed[tier]) return;
    var r = FB_SEASON1_REWARDS[tier - 1];
    if (!r) return;
    __fbSeason1.claimed[tier] = true;
    __fbSeasonGrant(r);
    __fbSaveSeason();
    try { if (typeof toast === 'function') toast('🎫 TIER ' + tier + ': ' + r.e + ' ' + r.name + '!'); } catch(e){}
    try { if (typeof beep === 'function') { beep(784, 0.12, 'sine', 0.07); setTimeout(function(){ beep(1046, 0.22, 'sine', 0.07); }, 130); } } catch(e){}
  } catch(e){}
}

/* ----- server sync (defensive: endpoints may not exist yet) ----- */
function __fbSyncSeason(){
  var t = null;
  try { if (window.FireBabysAuth && window.FireBabysAuth.getToken) t = window.FireBabysAuth.getToken(); } catch(e){}
  if (!t) return;
  try {
    fetch('https://fire-babys-auth.jeffpruden288.workers.dev/profile/season', {
      method: 'POST', headers: { 'content-type': 'application/json' },
      body: JSON.stringify({ token: t, season: FB_SEASON1.id, xp: __fbSeason1.xp,
        tier: __fbSeasonTier(), badges: __fbSeason1.badges, boost: __FB_SEASON_BOOST })
    }).catch(function(){});
  } catch(e){}
}

function __fbFetchSeason(){
  var t = null;
  try { if (window.FireBabysAuth && window.FireBabysAuth.getToken) t = window.FireBabysAuth.getToken(); } catch(e){}
  if (!t) return;
  try {
    fetch('https://fire-babys-auth.jeffpruden288.workers.dev/profile/get', {
      method: 'POST', headers: { 'content-type': 'application/json' },
      body: JSON.stringify({ token: t })
    }).then(function(r){ return r.json(); }).then(function(j){
      try {
        if (j && j.ok && j.profile && j.profile.season && j.profile.season.id === FB_SEASON1.id) {
          var s = j.profile.season;
          if (typeof s.xp === 'number' && s.xp > __fbSeason1.xp) {
            __fbSeason1.xp = s.xp;
            if (s.badges && s.badges.length !== undefined) __fbSeason1.badges = s.badges;
            if (typeof s.boost === 'number' && s.boost > __FB_SEASON_BOOST) __FB_SEASON_BOOST = s.boost;
            var maxT = Math.min(FB_SEASON1.tiers, Math.floor(__fbSeason1.xp / FB_SEASON1.xpPerTier));
            for (var t2 = 1; t2 <= maxT; t2++) __fbClaimSeasonTier(t2);
            __fbSaveSeason();
          }
        }
      } catch(e){}
    }).catch(function(){});
  } catch(e){}
}

/* ============================================================
   WEAPONS (⚔️) — the Armory
   Jeff's official roster. Damage multipliers stack multiplicatively
   with armor/season boosts. All-ages, fire-safety centered.
   This section READS bronze/silver/season state — never modifies it.
   ============================================================ */
var FB_WEAPONS = [
  { id:'weapon_hose_blaster_01', icon:'🔥', name:'High-Pressure Magma Hose', cls:'Blaze', mult:2.5, element:'Plasma Fire', effect:'Area-of-Effect Splash', desc:'Standard-issue heavy tactical launcher for front-line hero strikes.', unlock:'starter', unlockText:'Starter weapon — yours from the beginning!' },
  { id:'weapon_fist_gauntlets_02', icon:'🥊', name:'Soot-Crusher Power Fists', cls:'Ruko', mult:2.2, element:'Kinetic Impact', effect:'Armor-Piercing Slam', desc:'Heavy brass knuckles built for close-quarters brawling and smashing stone blocks.', unlock:'bronze_full', unlockText:'Full BRONZE set (all 4 pieces)' },
  { id:'weapon_ember_bow_04', icon:'🏹', name:'Spitfire Precision Bow', cls:'Sizzle', mult:2.0, element:'Ignition Arrow', effect:'Long-Range Pierce', desc:'Fires concentrated high-velocity fire bolts to pick off backline targets.', unlock:'season8', unlockText:'Season 1 — Tier 8' },
  { id:'weapon_scatter_bomb_05', icon:'💥', name:'Blue-Tip Spark Grenade', cls:'Cinder', mult:2.4, element:'Wild Chaos', effect:'Multi-Split Explosion', desc:'Bounces wildly and fragments into dozens of miniature burning sparks.', unlock:'season16', unlockText:'Season 1 — Tier 16' },
  { id:'weapon_anvil_drop_03', icon:'⚒️', name:'Magma Core Anvil', cls:'Boulder', mult:3.0, element:'Earth-Magma', effect:'Vertical Crush', desc:'Drops with maximum weight to flatten wood and metal enemy structures.', unlock:'silver', unlockText:'SILVER armor (all 250 tasks)' },
  { id:'weapon_inferno_staff_06', icon:'🔱', name:'Underworld Doom Rod', cls:'Lord Inferno', mult:3.5, element:'Dark Slag', effect:'Boss Terrain Scorcher', desc:'Elite weaponized staff wielded by boss-tier underworld entities.', unlock:'season20', unlockText:'Season 1 — Tier 20 (Ignition Crown)' }
];
var FB_WEAPON_STARTER = 'weapon_hose_blaster_01';

/* ----- weapon state (localStorage, fbWeapons_* namespace) ----- */
var __fbWeaponEquipped = FB_WEAPON_STARTER;
var __fbWeaponsUnlocked = [FB_WEAPON_STARTER];
var __fbWeaponsInitDone = false;

function __fbLoadWeapons(){
  if (__fbWeaponsInitDone) return;
  __fbWeaponsInitDone = true;
  try {
    var eq = localStorage.getItem('fbWeapons_equipped');
    if (eq) __fbWeaponEquipped = eq;
    var un = localStorage.getItem('fbWeapons_unlocked');
    if (un) { var u = JSON.parse(un); if (u && u.length !== undefined) __fbWeaponsUnlocked = u; }
    if (__fbWeaponsUnlocked.indexOf(FB_WEAPON_STARTER) < 0) __fbWeaponsUnlocked.unshift(FB_WEAPON_STARTER);
    if (__fbWeaponById(__fbWeaponEquipped) && __fbWeaponsUnlocked.indexOf(__fbWeaponEquipped) < 0)
      __fbWeaponEquipped = FB_WEAPON_STARTER;
  } catch(e){}
  try { __fbFetchWeapons(); } catch(e){}
  try { __fbCheckWeaponUnlocks(); } catch(e){}
}

function __fbSaveWeapons(){
  try {
    localStorage.setItem('fbWeapons_equipped', __fbWeaponEquipped);
    localStorage.setItem('fbWeapons_unlocked', JSON.stringify(__fbWeaponsUnlocked));
  } catch(e){}
}

function __fbWeaponById(id){
  for (var i = 0; i < FB_WEAPONS.length; i++) if (FB_WEAPONS[i].id === id) return FB_WEAPONS[i];
  return null;
}

/* equipped weapon's damage multiplier (1 = fallback, no weapon data) */
function __fbWeaponMult(){
  try {
    __fbLoadWeapons();
    var w = __fbWeaponById(__fbWeaponEquipped);
    if (w && __fbWeaponsUnlocked.indexOf(w.id) >= 0 && typeof w.mult === 'number') return w.mult;
  } catch(e){}
  return 1;
}

function __fbBronzeFull(){
  try {
    var a = (typeof __FB_ARMOR !== 'undefined') ? __FB_ARMOR : null;
    return !!(a && a.greaves && a.gauntlets && a.helmet && a.chestplate);
  } catch(e){ return false; }
}

/* is this weapon's unlock condition met right now? */
function __fbWeaponUnlockedBy(id){
  try {
    var w = __fbWeaponById(id);
    if (!w) return false;
    __fbLoadSeason();
    switch (w.unlock) {
      case 'starter': return true;
      case 'bronze_full': return __fbBronzeFull();
      case 'silver': return (typeof __FB_SILVER_UNLOCKED !== 'undefined' && !!__FB_SILVER_UNLOCKED);
      case 'season8': return !!(__fbSeason1.claimed && __fbSeason1.claimed[8]);
      case 'season16': return !!(__fbSeason1.claimed && __fbSeason1.claimed[16]);
      case 'season20': return !!(__fbSeason1.claimed && __fbSeason1.claimed[20]);
    }
  } catch(e){}
  return false;
}

/* unlock a weapon (idempotent) */
function __fbUnlockWeapon(id, silent){
  try {
    __fbLoadWeapons();
    if (__fbWeaponsUnlocked.indexOf(id) >= 0) return false;
    __fbWeaponsUnlocked.push(id);
    __fbSaveWeapons();
    try { __fbSyncWeapons(); } catch(e){}
    if (!silent) {
      var w = __fbWeaponById(id);
      try { if (typeof toast === 'function') toast('⚔️ WEAPON UNLOCKED: ' + (w ? w.icon + ' ' + w.name : id) + '!'); } catch(e){}
      try { if (typeof beep === 'function') { beep(523,0.12,'sine',0.07); setTimeout(function(){ beep(659,0.12,'sine',0.07); },120); setTimeout(function(){ beep(784,0.22,'sine',0.07); },240); } } catch(e){}
    }
    try { __fbRefreshArmoryUI(); } catch(e){}
    try { __fbRefreshVaultUI(); } catch(e){}
    return true;
  } catch(e){ return false; }
}

/* unlock anything newly qualified (retroactive-safe for already-claimed tiers) */
function __fbCheckWeaponUnlocks(){
  try {
    __fbLoadWeapons();
    var changed = false;
    for (var i = 0; i < FB_WEAPONS.length; i++) {
      var w = FB_WEAPONS[i];
      if (__fbWeaponsUnlocked.indexOf(w.id) < 0 && __fbWeaponUnlockedBy(w.id)) {
        if (__fbUnlockWeapon(w.id)) changed = true;
      }
    }
    return changed;
  } catch(e){ return false; }
}

function __fbEquipWeapon(id){
  try {
    __fbLoadWeapons();
    if (__fbWeaponsUnlocked.indexOf(id) < 0) return false;
    __fbWeaponEquipped = id;
    __fbSaveWeapons();
    try { __fbSyncWeapons(); } catch(e){}
    var w = __fbWeaponById(id);
    try { if (typeof toast === 'function') toast('⚔️ Equipped: ' + w.icon + ' ' + w.name + ' (' + w.mult + 'x damage)!'); } catch(e){}
    try { if (typeof beep === 'function') beep(660, 0.1, 'sine', 0.06); } catch(e){}
    try { __fbRefreshArmoryUI(); } catch(e){}
    try { __fbRefreshVaultUI(); } catch(e){}
    return true;
  } catch(e){ return false; }
}

/* ----- server sync (defensive: endpoint may not exist yet) ----- */
function __fbSyncWeapons(){
  var t = null;
  try { if (window.FireBabysAuth && window.FireBabysAuth.getToken) t = window.FireBabysAuth.getToken(); } catch(e){}
  if (!t) return;
  try {
    fetch('https://fire-babys-auth.jeffpruden288.workers.dev/profile/weapons', {
      method: 'POST', headers: { 'content-type': 'application/json' },
      body: JSON.stringify({ token: t, equipped: __fbWeaponEquipped, unlocked: __fbWeaponsUnlocked })
    }).catch(function(){});
  } catch(e){}
}

function __fbFetchWeapons(){
  var t = null;
  try { if (window.FireBabysAuth && window.FireBabysAuth.getToken) t = window.FireBabysAuth.getToken(); } catch(e){}
  if (!t) return;
  try {
    fetch('https://fire-babys-auth.jeffpruden288.workers.dev/profile/get', {
      method: 'POST', headers: { 'content-type': 'application/json' },
      body: JSON.stringify({ token: t })
    }).then(function(r){ return r.json(); }).then(function(j){
      try {
        if (j && j.ok && j.profile && j.profile.weapons) {
          var sw = j.profile.weapons;
          var set = {};
          __fbWeaponsUnlocked.forEach(function(id){ set[id] = 1; });
          if (sw.unlocked && sw.unlocked.forEach) sw.unlocked.forEach(function(id){ if (__fbWeaponById(id)) set[id] = 1; });
          __fbWeaponsUnlocked = Object.keys(set);
          if (sw.equipped && __fbWeaponById(sw.equipped) && __fbWeaponsUnlocked.indexOf(sw.equipped) >= 0)
            __fbWeaponEquipped = sw.equipped;
          __fbSaveWeapons();
        }
      } catch(e){}
    }).catch(function(){});
  } catch(e){}
}

/* ----- Armory UI (⚔️) ----- */
var __fbArmoryUIOpen = false;

function __fbArmoryHTML(){
  __fbLoadWeapons();
  __fbCheckWeaponUnlocks();
  var w = __fbWeaponById(__fbWeaponEquipped) || __fbWeaponById(FB_WEAPON_STARTER);
  var rows = '';
  FB_WEAPONS.forEach(function(wp){
    var un = __fbWeaponsUnlocked.indexOf(wp.id) >= 0;
    var eq = __fbWeaponEquipped === wp.id;
    rows += '<div class="fb-arm-card" data-wid="' + wp.id + '" style="padding:12px;margin-bottom:8px;border-radius:12px;cursor:' +
      (un && !eq ? 'pointer' : 'default') + ';background:' + (eq ? 'rgba(255,140,46,0.16)' : (un ? 'rgba(255,140,46,0.06)' : 'rgba(140,140,140,0.06)')) +
      ';border:2px solid ' + (eq ? '#ff8c2e' : (un ? 'rgba(255,140,46,0.45)' : 'rgba(160,160,160,0.3)')) + ';">' +
      '<div style="display:flex;align-items:center;gap:10px;">' +
        '<div style="font-size:32px;min-width:40px;text-align:center;' + (un ? '' : 'filter:grayscale(1);') + '">' + (un ? wp.icon : '🔒') + '</div>' +
        '<div style="flex:1;min-width:0;">' +
          '<div style="color:' + (un ? '#ffe9d6' : '#9a9a9a') + ';font-size:14px;font-weight:900;">' + wp.name +
            (eq ? ' <span style="color:#ff8c2e;font-size:11px;">✅ EQUIPPED</span>' : '') + '</div>' +
          '<div style="color:#ff9c4a;font-size:11px;font-weight:700;margin-top:2px;">' + wp.cls + ' &bull; ' + wp.element + ' &bull; <b>' + wp.mult + 'x damage</b></div>' +
          '<div style="color:#c9a882;font-size:11px;margin-top:2px;">' + wp.effect + '</div>' +
          (un
            ? '<div style="color:#8a8a8a;font-size:11px;margin-top:2px;">' + wp.desc + '</div>' + (eq ? '' : '<div style="color:#ff8c2e;font-size:12px;font-weight:800;margin-top:4px;">👆 Tap to equip</div>')
            : '<div style="color:#9a9a9a;font-size:11px;margin-top:4px;">🔒 ' + wp.unlockText + '</div>') +
        '</div>' +
      '</div>' +
    '</div>';
  });
  return '' +
  '<div style="width:100%;max-width:520px;max-height:85vh;display:flex;flex-direction:column;background:#1c1006;border:3px solid #ff8c2e;border-radius:20px;padding:20px;box-sizing:border-box;">' +
    '<div style="color:#ff9c4a;font-size:20px;font-weight:900;letter-spacing:2px;text-align:center;">⚔️ THE ARMORY</div>' +
    '<div style="color:#c9a882;font-size:12px;text-align:center;margin:6px 0 12px;">Choose your weapon — multipliers stack with your armor boosts.</div>' +
    '<div style="padding:10px;border-radius:12px;text-align:center;margin-bottom:12px;background:rgba(255,140,46,0.1);border:2px solid #ff8c2e;">' +
      '<div style="font-size:30px;">' + w.icon + '</div>' +
      '<div style="color:#ff9c4a;font-size:14px;font-weight:900;">WIELDING: ' + w.name + '</div>' +
      '<div style="color:#ffe9d6;font-size:12px;">' + w.mult + 'x damage &bull; ' + w.element + '</div>' +
    '</div>' +
    '<div id="fb-arm-list" style="flex:1;overflow-y:auto;min-height:150px;">' + rows + '</div>' +
    '<button id="fb-arm-close" style="margin-top:12px;padding:12px;font-size:16px;font-weight:900;border:none;border-radius:12px;cursor:pointer;color:#1c1006;background:linear-gradient(180deg,#ff9c4a,#ff8c2e);">CLOSE</button>' +
  '</div>';
}

function __fbShowArmory(){
  try {
    __fbLoadWeapons();
    var old = document.getElementById('fb-armory-ov');
    if (old) { old.parentNode.removeChild(old); __fbArmoryUIOpen = false; return; }
    __fbArmoryUIOpen = true;
    var ov = document.createElement('div');
    ov.id = 'fb-armory-ov';
    ov.style.cssText = 'position:fixed;inset:0;z-index:99990;display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:center;background:rgba(8,4,2,0.88);padding:16px;box-sizing:border-box;';
    ov.innerHTML = __fbArmoryHTML();
    ov.querySelector('#fb-arm-close').addEventListener('click', function(){ ov.parentNode.removeChild(ov); __fbArmoryUIOpen = false; });
    ov.addEventListener('click', function(e){
      if (e.target === ov){ ov.parentNode.removeChild(ov); __fbArmoryUIOpen = false; return; }
      var el = e.target;
      while (el && el !== ov) {
        if (el.className && typeof el.className === 'string' && el.className.indexOf('fb-arm-card') >= 0) {
          var wid = el.getAttribute('data-wid');
          if (wid && wid !== __fbWeaponEquipped) __fbEquipWeapon(wid);
          return;
        }
        el = el.parentNode;
      }
    });
    document.body.appendChild(ov);
  } catch(e){}
}

function __fbRefreshArmoryUI(){
  if (__fbArmoryUIOpen) {
    try {
      var ov = document.getElementById('fb-armory-ov');
      if (ov) { ov.parentNode.removeChild(ov); __fbArmoryUIOpen = false; }
      __fbShowArmory();
    } catch(e){}
  }
}

/* ----- weapons section for the Vault panel ----- */
function __fbVaultWeaponsHTML(){
  __fbLoadWeapons();
  var cards = '';
  FB_WEAPONS.forEach(function(wp){
    var un = __fbWeaponsUnlocked.indexOf(wp.id) >= 0;
    var eq = __fbWeaponEquipped === wp.id;
    cards += '<div style="flex:1 1 44%;min-width:128px;padding:10px;border-radius:12px;text-align:center;background:' +
      (eq ? 'rgba(255,140,46,0.14)' : (un ? 'rgba(255,140,46,0.06)' : 'rgba(140,140,140,0.06)')) + ';border:1px solid ' +
      (eq ? '#ff8c2e' : (un ? 'rgba(255,140,46,0.45)' : 'rgba(160,160,160,0.3)')) + ';">' +
      '<div style="font-size:28px;' + (un ? '' : 'filter:grayscale(1);') + '">' + (un ? wp.icon : '🔒') + '</div>' +
      '<div style="color:' + (un ? '#ffe9d6' : '#9a9a9a') + ';font-size:11px;font-weight:800;margin-top:4px;">' + wp.name + (eq ? ' ✅' : '') + '</div>' +
      '<div style="color:#ff9c4a;font-size:11px;margin-top:2px;">' + (un ? wp.mult + 'x damage' : wp.unlockText) + '</div></div>';
  });
  return '<div style="color:#ffd76a;font-size:13px;font-weight:900;letter-spacing:1px;margin:12px 0 8px;">⚔️ WEAPONS — THE ARMORY</div>' +
    '<div style="display:flex;flex-wrap:wrap;gap:8px;">' + cards + '</div>' +
    '<div style="color:#c9a882;font-size:11px;margin-top:6px;text-align:center;">Open the ⚔️ Armory to equip a weapon.</div>';
}

/* ============================================================
   THE VAULT (🗝️) — personal trophy room
   ============================================================ */
var __fbVaultUIOpen = false;

function __fbVaultEquipped(){
  try {
    if (typeof __FB_SILVER_UNLOCKED !== 'undefined' && __FB_SILVER_UNLOCKED)
      return { icon:'🥈', name:'SILVER ARMOR', desc:'Full set — +20% over everything', color:'#cceeff', glow:true };
    var a = (typeof __FB_ARMOR !== 'undefined') ? __FB_ARMOR : null;
    var n = 0, full = false;
    if (a) {
      n = (a.greaves?1:0) + (a.gauntlets?1:0) + (a.helmet?1:0) + (a.chestplate?1:0);
      full = (typeof __FB_ARMOR_FULL !== 'undefined') ? __FB_ARMOR_FULL : (n === 4);
    }
    if (full) return { icon:'🥉', name:'BRONZE FULL SET', desc:'Diamond chestplate — +10% over everything', color:'#ffd76a', glow:true };
    if (n > 0) return { icon:'🥉', name:'BRONZE ' + n + '/4', desc:'+' + (n * 2.5) + '% boost — finish the 2D stages!', color:'#c9a882', glow:false };
  } catch(e){}
  return { icon:'🔥', name:'NO ARMOR YET', desc:'Beat 2D stages to earn bronze pieces!', color:'#ffe9d6', glow:false };
}

function __fbVaultHTML(){
  var eq = __fbVaultEquipped();
  var a = (typeof __FB_ARMOR !== 'undefined') ? __FB_ARMOR : {};
  var pieces = [
    { k:'greaves',    e:'🥾', name:'Bronze Greaves',    req:'Beat 2D Stage 5' },
    { k:'gauntlets',  e:'🧤', name:'Bronze Gauntlets',  req:'Beat 2D Stage 10' },
    { k:'helmet',     e:'🪖', name:'Bronze Helmet',     req:'Beat 2D Stage 15' },
    { k:'chestplate', e:'🛡️', name:'Bronze Chestplate', req:'Beat 2D Stage 20' }
  ];
  var grid = '';
  pieces.forEach(function(p){
    var has = !!a[p.k];
    grid += '<div style="flex:1 1 44%;min-width:128px;padding:10px;border-radius:12px;text-align:center;background:' +
      (has ? 'rgba(255,215,106,0.12)' : 'rgba(140,140,140,0.07)') + ';border:1px solid ' +

      '<div style="font-size:30px;">' + (has ? p.e : '🔒') + '</div>' +
      '<div style="color:' + (has ? '#ffd76a' : '#9a9a9a') + ';font-size:12px;font-weight:800;margin-top:4px;">' + p.name + '</div>' +
      '<div style="color:#c9a882;font-size:11px;margin-top:2px;">' + (has ? '✅ +2.5% boost' : p.req) + '</div></div>';
  });
  var sDone = 0;
  try { sDone = (typeof __fbTasksCompletedCount === 'function') ? __fbTasksCompletedCount() : 0; } catch(e){}
  var sOn = (typeof __FB_SILVER_UNLOCKED !== 'undefined' && __FB_SILVER_UNLOCKED);
  var sPct = Math.min(100, Math.round(sDone / 250 * 100));
  var tier = __fbSeasonTier();
  var claimedN = Object.keys(__fbSeason1.claimed).length;
  var badgesN = __fbSeason1.badges.length;
  var seasonBoostPct = Math.round(__FB_SEASON_BOOST * 1000) / 10;

  '</div>';

function __fbShowVault(){
  try {
    __fbLoadSeason();
    if (typeof __fbLoadTasks === 'function') { try { __fbLoadTasks(); } catch(e){} }
    var old = document.getElementById('fb-vault-ov');
    if (old) { old.parentNode.removeChild(old); __fbVaultUIOpen = false; return; }
    __fbVaultUIOpen = true;
    var ov = document.createElement('div');
    ov.id = 'fb-vault-ov';
    ov.style.cssText = 'position:fixed;inset:0;z-index:99990;display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:center;background:rgba(8,4,2,0.88);padding:16px;box-sizing:border-box;';
    ov.innerHTML = __fbVaultHTML();
    ov.querySelector('#fb-vault-close').addEventListener('click', function(){ ov.parentNode.removeChild(ov); __fbVaultUIOpen = false; });
    ov.addEventListener('click', function(e){ if (e.target === ov){ ov.parentNode.removeChild(ov); __fbVaultUIOpen = false; } });
    document.body.appendChild(ov);
  } catch(e){}
}

function __fbRefreshVaultUI(){
  if (__fbVaultUIOpen) {
    try {
      var ov = document.getElementById('fb-vault-ov');
      if (ov) { ov.parentNode.removeChild(ov); __fbVaultUIOpen = false; }
      __fbShowVault();
    } catch(e){}
  }
}

   SEASON 1: IGNITION (🎫) — battle pass panel

var __fbSeasonUIOpen = false;

function __fbSeasonHTML(){
  var tier = __fbSeasonTier();
  var xp = __fbSeason1.xp;
  var maxXp = FB_SEASON1.tiers * FB_SEASON1.xpPerTier;
  var pct = Math.min(100, Math.round(xp / maxXp * 100));
  var rows = '';
  FB_SEASON1_REWARDS.forEach(function(r, i){
    var t = i + 1;
    var unlocked = xp >= t * FB_SEASON1.xpPerTier;
    var claimed = !!__fbSeason1.claimed[t];
    rows += '<div style="display:flex;align-items:center;gap:10px;padding:8px 10px;margin-bottom:6px;border-radius:10px;background:' +
      (claimed ? 'rgba(255,140,46,0.16)' : 'rgba(255,140,46,0.05)') + ';border:1px solid ' +
      (claimed ? '#ff8c2e' : 'rgba(255,140,46,0.25)') + ';">' +
      '<div style="color:#ffd76a;font-weight:900;font-size:12px;min-width:50px;">TIER ' + t + '</div>' +
      '<div style="font-size:24px;min-width:32px;text-align:center;">' + (claimed || unlocked ? r.e : '🔒') + '</div>' +
      '<div style="flex:1;min-width:0;"><div style="color:#ffe9d6;font-size:13px;font-weight:700;">' + r.name + '</div>' +
      '<div style="color:#c9a882;font-size:11px;">' +
        (claimed ? '✅ Claimed' : (unlocked ? 'Ready!' : (t * FB_SEASON1.xpPerTier) + ' XP')) +
      '</div></div></div>';
  });
  return '' +
  '<div style="width:100%;max-width:520px;max-height:85vh;display:flex;flex-direction:column;background:#1c1006;border:3px solid #ff8c2e;border-radius:20px;padding:20px;box-sizing:border-box;">' +
    '<div style="color:#ff9c4a;font-size:20px;font-weight:900;letter-spacing:2px;text-align:center;">🎫 SEASON 1: IGNITION</div>' +
    '<div style="color:#ffe9d6;font-size:13px;text-align:center;margin:8px 0;">Tier <b style="color:#ffd76a;">' + tier + '</b>/20 &nbsp;•&nbsp; ' + xp + ' XP</div>' +
    '<div style="height:10px;background:#3a2410;border-radius:5px;margin-bottom:6px;"><div style="height:100%;width:' + pct +
      '%;background:linear-gradient(90deg,#ff8c2e,#ffd76a);border-radius:5px;"></div></div>' +
    '<div style="color:#c9a882;font-size:11px;text-align:center;margin-bottom:10px;">Earn XP: KOs (+' + FB_SEASON1.xpFor.ko +
      ') • Tasks (+' + FB_SEASON1.xpFor.task + ') • Play time (+' + FB_SEASON1.xpFor.time_min + '/min)</div>' +
    '<div style="flex:1;overflow-y:auto;min-height:150px;">' + rows +
      '<div style="margin:10px 0 4px;padding:14px;border-radius:12px;text-align:center;background:rgba(136,153,255,0.07);border:1px dashed #8899ff;">' +
        '<div style="font-size:24px;">🔒</div>' +
        '<div style="color:#cceeff;font-size:14px;font-weight:900;letter-spacing:1px;">PREMIUM TRACK</div>' +
        '<div style="color:#c9a882;font-size:12px;margin-top:2px;">Coming soon — even more loot!</div>' +
      '</div>' +
    '</div>' +
    '<button id="fb-season-close" style="margin-top:12px;padding:12px;font-size:16px;font-weight:900;border:none;border-radius:12px;cursor:pointer;color:#1c1006;background:linear-gradient(180deg,#ff9c4a,#ff8c2e);">CLOSE</button>' +
  '</div>';
}

<script>
/* ==== BULLETPROOF START - v2 ==== 
   The button ALWAYS responds. Tapping hides the title immediately,
   then tries 3D init in a try/catch. If 3D fails, shows error. */
function startGame(){
  // STEP 1: Hide title IMMEDIATELY - this always works, no dependencies
  var t = document.getElementById('titleov');
  if(t) t.style.display = 'none';
  // STEP 2: Show loading indicator
  var l = document.getElementById('loadov');
  if(l) l.style.display = 'flex';
  // STEP 3: Init 3D after UI updates (setTimeout lets the browser paint first)
  setTimeout(function(){
    try {
      init();
      // SUCCESS: hide loading
      if(l) l.style.display = 'none';
      // Deep-link ?mode=splash/?mode=catch goes straight to SPLASH via the MP UI
      try { if(typeof catchDeepLink === 'function') catchDeepLink(); } catch(e){}
      // SPLASH ONLY (Jeff's order 2026-10-05): skip character creation entirely,
      // always go straight to the SPLASH flow via showHub()
      if(typeof showHub === 'function') {
        // Skip showHub if the deep-link already started the SPLASH flow
        try { if(typeof _catchDL === 'undefined' || !_catchDL) showHub(); } catch(e){ showHub(); }
      }
    } catch(e) {
      // FAIL: hide loading, show error (never hang on black screen)
      if(l) l.style.display = 'none';
      var er = document.getElementById('errov');
      var em = document.getElementById('errmsg');
      if(em) em.textContent = '3D ERROR: ' + (e && e.message ? e.message : 'unknown error');
      if(er) er.style.display = 'flex';
    }
  }, 50);
}
/* CSP hash conversion (gamer check 2026-10-12): the former inline onclick
   attributes on #startbtn and the error-overlay TRY AGAIN button were removed
   (hash-based script-src does not cover inline event-handler attributes);
   both are wired here instead. */
(function(){
  var sb = document.getElementById('startbtn');
  if(sb) sb.addEventListener('click', startGame);
  var rb = document.getElementById('errretrybtn');
  if(rb) rb.addEventListener('click', function(){ location.reload(); });
})();
</script>

/* ============================================================
   Fire Babys Style Studio + Store v2
   ------------------------------------------------------------
   FIXES vs v1:
   - Customize 3D preview now renders the WORKING DRAFT (v1 read the
     saved look, so the preview never changed when tapping options).
   - Preview render loop is cancelled on close; WebGL failure falls
     back to a 2D face render instead of a blank canvas.
   - Save applies immediately and re-dresses the player.

   ECONOMY v2 (three currencies):
   - COINS (🪙)  : earned by playing. 10/game, 5/KO, 2/extinguish.
   - GEMS  (💎)  : premium. 1 per game win, 1 per 5 KOs, or buy below.
   - REAL MONEY  : Stripe Payment Links open in a new tab (GEMS tab).
   One-time migration: old gem balance -> coins, starter 100c + 5g.
   ============================================================ */

/* ---------------- state ---------------- */
var DEF_LOOK = { c:0, e:'happy', h:'wild', a:'none' };
function loadJSON(k, fb){ try{ var v=localStorage.getItem(k); if(v) return JSON.parse(v); }catch(e){} return JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(fb)); }
function loadInt(k, fb){ try{ var v=parseInt(localStorage.getItem(k),10); if(!isNaN(v)&&v>=0) return v; }catch(e){} return fb; }
var FBC = {
  look: loadJSON('fb_customization', DEF_LOOK),
  coins: loadInt('fb_coins', 0),
  gems: loadInt('fb_gems', 0),
  kos: loadInt('fb_kos_total', 0),
  inv: loadJSON('fb_inventory', null) || { armor:[], weapons:['stick'], emotes:['wave'],
                                          eqArmor:null, eqWeapon:'stick', favEmote:'wave' }
};
function saveState(){
  try{
    localStorage.setItem('fb_customization', JSON.stringify(FBC.look));
    localStorage.setItem('fb_coins', String(FBC.coins));
    localStorage.setItem('fb_gems', String(FBC.gems));
    localStorage.setItem('fb_kos_total', String(FBC.kos));
    localStorage.setItem('fb_inventory', JSON.stringify(FBC.inv));
    var pal = FB_COLORS[FBC.look.c]||FB_COLORS[0];
    localStorage.setItem('fb3d_color', pal.body);
  }catch(e){}
}
/* one-time economy migration: v1 gems become coins, everyone starts 100c/5g */
(function fbMigrateEconomy(){
  try{
    if(localStorage.getItem('fb_economy_v2')) return;
    var oldGems = loadInt('fb_gems', 0);
    FBC.coins = 100 + oldGems;
    FBC.gems = 5;
    FBC.kos = 0;
    localStorage.setItem('fb_economy_v2', '1');
    saveState();
  }catch(e){}
})();
