function emoteById(id){ for(var i=0;i<FB_EMOTES.length;i++) if(FB_EMOTES[i].id===id) return FB_EMOTES[i]; return FB_EMOTES[0]; }
function weaponById(id){ for(var i=0;i<FB_STORE_WEAPONS.length;i++) if(FB_STORE_WEAPONS[i].id===id) return FB_STORE_WEAPONS[i]; return FB_STORE_WEAPONS[0]; }
function armorById(id){ for(var i=0;i<FB_ARMOR.length;i++) if(FB_ARMOR[i].id===id) return FB_ARMOR[i]; return null; }

/* ---------------- helpers ---------------- */
function $(id){ return document.getElementById(id); }
function beepOk(){ try{ if(typeof beep==='function') beep(700,0.08,'square',0.06); }catch(e){} }
function toastOk(m){ try{ if(typeof toast==='function') toast(m); }catch(e){} }
function esc(s){ return String(s).replace(/[<>&"]/g,''); }

/* ---------------- currency ---------------- */
function fbCoins(){ return FBC.coins; }
function fbGems(){ return FBC.gems; }
function fbAddCoins(n, msg, quiet){
  FBC.coins = Math.max(0, FBC.coins + n);
  saveState(); fbRefreshCurrencyUI();
  if(msg && !quiet) toastOk(msg);
}
function fbAddGems(n, msg, quiet){
  FBC.gems = Math.max(0, FBC.gems + n);
  saveState(); fbRefreshCurrencyUI();
  if(msg && !quiet) toastOk(msg);
}
function fbRefreshCurrencyUI(){
  var cc = $('fbcoincount'); if(cc) cc.textContent = FBC.coins;
  var gc = $('fbgemcount'); if(gc) gc.textContent = FBC.gems;
  var hub = $('hubgempill');
  if(hub) hub.innerHTML = '🪙 <b>'+FBC.coins+'</b> &nbsp; 💎 <b>'+FBC.gems+'</b>';
}

/* ---------------- hair styling (post-process on makeBaby licks) ---------------- */
function fbStyleHair(g, style){
  try{
    var u = g.userData, licks = u.licks, R = u.R || 1;
    if(!licks) return;
    for(var i=0;i<licks.length;i++){
      var L = licks[i], lu = L.userData;
      /* remember factory defaults once so 'wild' can truly reset */
      if(lu.dfp===undefined){ lu.dfp=L.position.clone(); lu.dfr=L.rotation.clone(); }
      if(lu.hsx===undefined){ lu.hsx=1; lu.hsy=1; lu.hsz=1; }
      L.visible = true;
      if(style==='spiky'){
        lu.hsx=0.7; lu.hsy=1.6; lu.hsz=0.7;
        L.position.set(lu.dfp.x, R*0.95, lu.dfp.z);
      } else if(style==='curly'){
        lu.hsx=1.15; lu.hsy=0.7; lu.hsz=1.15;
        var a=(i/licks.length)*Math.PI*2;
        L.position.set(Math.cos(a)*R*0.45, R*0.85, Math.sin(a)*R*0.45);
      } else if(style==='mohawk'){
        if(i<5){
          lu.hsx=0.8; lu.hsy=1.5; lu.hsz=0.8;
          L.position.set((i-2)*R*0.28, R*1.0, 0);
          L.rotation.set(0,0,0);
        } else { L.visible=false; }
      } else if(style==='flattop'){
        var a2=(i/licks.length)*Math.PI*2;
        lu.hsx=1.0; lu.hsy=0.55; lu.hsz=1.0;
        L.position.set(Math.cos(a2)*R*0.55, R*1.02, Math.sin(a2)*R*0.55);
      } else { /* wild: restore factory defaults */
        lu.hsx=1; lu.hsy=1; lu.hsz=1;
        L.position.copy(lu.dfp); L.rotation.copy(lu.dfr);
      }
    }
  }catch(e){}
}

/* ---------------- accessories (post-process meshes) ---------------- */
function fbBuildAccessory(g, accId){
  var grp = new THREE.Group(), R = (g.userData&&g.userData.R)||1;
  try{
    if(accId==='tiny_crown'){
      var gold = new THREE.MeshLambertMaterial({ color:0xffd76a, emissive:0x553a00 });
      var circ = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.CylinderGeometry(R*0.34,R*0.38,R*0.2,8), gold);
      circ.position.y = R*1.12; circ.rotation.z = 0.12; grp.add(circ);
      for(var s=0;s<4;s++){
        var sp = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.ConeGeometry(R*0.07,R*0.2,5), gold);
        var sa=(s/4)*Math.PI*2;
        sp.position.set(Math.cos(sa)*R*0.3, R*1.3, Math.sin(sa)*R*0.3);
        grp.add(sp);
      }
    } else if(accId==='star_glasses'){
      var sm = new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({ color:0xffd76a, side:THREE.DoubleSide });
      [-1,1].forEach(function(sg){
        var star = new THREE.Shape();
        for(var i=0;i<10;i++){
          var rr=(i%2===0)?R*0.22:R*0.09, aa=-Math.PI/2+i*Math.PI/5;
          var px=Math.cos(aa)*rr, py=Math.sin(aa)*rr;
          if(i===0) star.moveTo(px,py); else star.lineTo(px,py);
        }
        star.closePath();
        var m = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.ShapeGeometry(star), sm);
        m.position.set(sg*R*0.42, R*0.28, R*0.92); m.rotation.y = sg*0.15;
        grp.add(m);
      });
      var bridge = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(R*0.2,R*0.05,R*0.05), sm);
      bridge.position.set(0, R*0.3, R*0.92); grp.add(bridge);
    } else if(accId==='bow_tie'){
      var rm = new THREE.MeshLambertMaterial({ color:0xe8352c });
      [-1,1].forEach(function(sg){
        var tri = new THREE.Shape();
        tri.moveTo(0,0); tri.lineTo(sg*R*0.34, R*0.2); tri.lineTo(sg*R*0.34, -R*0.2); tri.closePath();
        var m = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.ShapeGeometry(tri), rm);
        m.position.set(0, -R*0.72, R*0.88); grp.add(m);
      });
      var knot = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.SphereGeometry(R*0.09,8,8), rm);
      knot.position.set(0, -R*0.72, R*0.9); grp.add(knot);
    } else if(accId==='headband'){
      var hm = new THREE.MeshLambertMaterial({ color:0x3ca9ff });
      var band = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.TorusGeometry(R*1.0, R*0.09, 8, 24), hm);
      band.rotation.x = Math.PI/2 - 0.3; band.position.y = R*0.5; grp.add(band);
    } else if(accId==='party_hat'){
      var pm = new THREE.MeshLambertMaterial({ color:0xff7ab8 });
      var cone = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.ConeGeometry(R*0.32, R*0.75, 10), pm);
      cone.position.set(R*0.25, R*1.35, 0); cone.rotation.z = -0.18; grp.add(cone);
      var pom = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.SphereGeometry(R*0.1,8,8),
        new THREE.MeshLambertMaterial({ color:0xffffff }));
      pom.position.set(R*0.32, R*1.72, 0); grp.add(pom);
    }
  }catch(e){}
  if(grp.children.length) g.add(grp);
  return grp;
}

/* ---------------- armor (post-process meshes) ---------------- */
var fbRainbowTimers = [];
function fbBuildArmor(g, armorId){
  var grp = new THREE.Group(), R = (g.userData&&g.userData.R)||1;
  try{
    if(armorId==='wooden_shield'){
      var wm = new THREE.MeshLambertMaterial({ color:0x8a5a2e });
      var ring = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.TorusGeometry(R*1.12, R*0.13, 8, 26), wm);
      ring.rotation.x = Math.PI/2; ring.position.y = -R*0.1; grp.add(ring);
    } else if(armorId==='iron_helm'){
      var im = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color:0x9aa2ad, roughness:0.35, metalness:0.8 });
      var cap = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.SphereGeometry(R*1.02, 18, 12, 0, Math.PI*2, 0, Math.PI/2.4), im);
      cap.position.y = R*0.28; grp.add(cap);
    } else if(armorId==='steel_boots'){
      var bm = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color:0x6a7078, roughness:0.4, metalness:0.7 });
      [-1,1].forEach(function(sg){
        var boot = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(R*0.5, R*0.4, R*0.7), bm);
        boot.position.set(sg*R*0.42, -R*1.25, R*0.08); grp.add(boot);
      });
    } else if(armorId==='lava_gauntlets'){
      var gm = new THREE.MeshLambertMaterial({ color:0xe8352c, emissive:0x7a150e });
      [-1,1].forEach(function(sg){
        var fist = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.SphereGeometry(R*0.3, 10, 8), gm);
        fist.position.set(sg*R*1.15, -R*0.15, 0); grp.add(fist);
      });
    } else if(armorId==='knight_armor'){
      var km = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color:0xb8bec8, roughness:0.3, metalness:0.85 });
      var chest = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.CylinderGeometry(R*0.85, R*1.0, R*0.9, 12), km);
      chest.position.y = -R*0.15; grp.add(chest);
      [-1,1].forEach(function(sg){
        var pad = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.SphereGeometry(R*0.34, 10, 8), km);
        pad.position.set(sg*R*0.95, R*0.42, 0); grp.add(pad);
      });
    } else if(armorId==='golden_crown'){
      var gm2 = new THREE.MeshLambertMaterial({ color:0xffd76a, emissive:0x664400 });
      var circ = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.CylinderGeometry(R*0.55,R*0.62,R*0.34,10), gm2);
      circ.position.y = R*1.04; grp.add(circ);
      for(var s=0;s<6;s++){
        var sp = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.ConeGeometry(R*0.12,R*0.4,6), gm2);
        var sa=(s/6)*Math.PI*2;
        sp.position.set(Math.cos(sa)*R*0.5, R*1.36, Math.sin(sa)*R*0.5);
        grp.add(sp);
      }
      var jewel = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.SphereGeometry(R*0.1,8,8),
        new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({ color:0xff2e5a }));
      jewel.position.set(0, R*1.04, R*0.6); grp.add(jewel);
    } else if(armorId==='crystal_armor'){
      var cm = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color:0x9ae2ff, transparent:true, opacity:0.35,
        roughness:0.1, metalness:0.2 });
      var shell = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.IcosahedronGeometry(R*1.5, 0), cm);
      shell.position.y = R*0.1; grp.add(shell);
    } else if(armorId==='frost_guard'){
      var fm = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color:0x3ca9ff, transparent:true, opacity:0.3,
        roughness:0.15, metalness:0.3, emissive:0x0e4a8a });
      var fshell = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.IcosahedronGeometry(R*1.45, 1), fm);
      fshell.position.y = R*0.1; grp.add(fshell);
      for(var f=0;f<6;f++){
        var ic = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.OctahedronGeometry(R*0.16), fm);
        var fa=(f/6)*Math.PI*2;
        ic.position.set(Math.cos(fa)*R*1.35, R*(0.1+((f%3)-1)*0.5), Math.sin(fa)*R*1.35);
        grp.add(ic);
      }
    } else if(armorId==='dragon_scales'){
      var dm = new THREE.MeshLambertMaterial({ color:0xa31621 });
      for(var k=0;k<5;k++){
        var spike = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.ConeGeometry(R*0.14, R*0.5, 6), dm);
        spike.position.set(0, R*(0.55+k*0.22), -R*(0.95-k*0.06));
        spike.rotation.x = -0.5; grp.add(spike);
      }
      var plate = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.TorusGeometry(R*1.05, R*0.16, 8, 22), dm);
      plate.rotation.x = Math.PI/2; plate.position.y = -R*0.35; grp.add(plate);
    } else if(armorId==='shadow_cloak'){
      var scm = new THREE.MeshLambertMaterial({ color:0x1a1a24, transparent:true, opacity:0.85 });
      var cloak = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.ConeGeometry(R*1.0, R*1.9, 10, 1, true), scm);
      cloak.position.set(0, -R*0.35, -R*0.55); cloak.rotation.x = 0.25; grp.add(cloak);
    } else if(armorId==='rainbow_aura'){
      var c = document.createElement('canvas'); c.width=c.height=64;
      var x = c.getContext('2d');
      var gr = x.createRadialGradient(32,32,4,32,32,30);
      gr.addColorStop(0,'rgba(255,255,255,0)'); gr.addColorStop(0.75,'rgba(255,255,255,0)');
      gr.addColorStop(1,'rgba(255,80,180,0.85)');
      x.fillStyle=gr; x.fillRect(0,0,64,64);
      var sm = new THREE.SpriteMaterial({ map:new THREE.CanvasTexture(c),
        blending:THREE.AdditiveBlending, depthWrite:false, transparent:true, opacity:0.9 });
      var spr = new THREE.Sprite(sm);
      spr.scale.set(R*5.2, R*5.2, 1); spr.position.y = R*0.2;
      grp.add(spr);
      grp.userData.rainbow = sm;
    } else if(armorId==='phoenix_wings'){
      var pwm = new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({ color:0xff8c2e, transparent:true, opacity:0.92,
        blending:THREE.AdditiveBlending, depthWrite:false, side:THREE.DoubleSide });
      [-1,1].forEach(function(sg){
        var wing = new THREE.Shape();
        wing.moveTo(0,0);
        wing.quadraticCurveTo(sg*R*1.2, R*0.9, sg*R*1.7, R*0.2);
        wing.quadraticCurveTo(sg*R*1.1, R*0.1, sg*R*0.9, -R*0.5);
        wing.quadraticCurveTo(sg*R*0.4, -R*0.3, 0, -R*0.6);
        wing.closePath();
        var wmsh = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.ShapeGeometry(wing, 8), pwm);
        wmsh.position.set(sg*R*0.5, R*0.35, -R*0.55);
        wmsh.rotation.y = sg*0.35;
        grp.add(wmsh);
      });
    }
  }catch(e){}
  if(grp.children.length) g.add(grp);
  return grp;
}

/* ---------------- weapon mesh (shown during attack) ---------------- */
function fbBuildWeaponMesh(weaponId, Rin){
  var grp = new THREE.Group(), w = weaponById(weaponId), R = (typeof Rin==='number'&&Rin>0)?Rin:1;
  try{
    var m = new THREE.MeshLambertMaterial({ color:new THREE.Color(w.col), emissive:new THREE.Color(w.col).multiplyScalar(0.35) });
    if(weaponId==='stick' || weaponId==='fire_baton'){
      var b = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.CylinderGeometry(R*0.07,R*0.09,R*1.1,8), m);
      b.rotation.z = 1.1; grp.add(b);
    } else if(weaponId==='wooden_sword'){
      var bl = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(R*0.13,R*1.25,R*0.06), m);
      bl.rotation.z = 0.9; grp.add(bl);
      var gd = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(R*0.38,R*0.09,R*0.11),
        new THREE.MeshLambertMaterial({color:0x5a3a1e}));
      gd.rotation.z = 0.9; gd.position.set(-R*0.27,-R*0.27,0); grp.add(gd);
    } else if(weaponId==='stone_club'){
      var hd = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.CylinderGeometry(R*0.07,R*0.08,R*0.9,8), m);
      hd.rotation.z = 1.1; grp.add(hd);
      var rock = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.DodecahedronGeometry(R*0.26), m);
      rock.position.set(R*0.42, R*0.32, 0); grp.add(rock);
    } else if(weaponId==='iron_axe'){
      var ha = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.CylinderGeometry(R*0.06,R*0.06,R*1.1,8), m);
      ha.rotation.z = 1.0; grp.add(ha);
      var ax = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(R*0.5,R*0.28,R*0.08), m);
      ax.position.set(R*0.42,R*0.35,0); grp.add(ax);
    } else if(weaponId==='water_pistol'){
      var bd = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(R*0.5,R*0.22,R*0.18), m); grp.add(bd);
      var nz = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.CylinderGeometry(R*0.07,R*0.07,R*0.35,8), m);
      nz.rotation.z = Math.PI/2; nz.position.x = R*0.4; grp.add(nz);
    } else if(weaponId==='lava_sword'){
      var lb = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(R*0.14,R*1.3,R*0.06), m);
      lb.rotation.z = 0.9; grp.add(lb);
      var lg = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(R*0.4,R*0.1,R*0.12),
        new THREE.MeshLambertMaterial({color:0x5a3a1e}));
      lg.rotation.z = 0.9; lg.position.set(-R*0.28,-R*0.28,0); grp.add(lg);
    } else if(weaponId==='star_wand'){
      var wd = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.CylinderGeometry(R*0.045,R*0.055,R*1.0,8), m);
      wd.rotation.z = 1.1; grp.add(wd);
      var star = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.OctahedronGeometry(R*0.2),
        new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({ color:0xfff2b0 }));
      star.position.set(R*0.45, R*0.35, 0); grp.add(star);
    } else if(weaponId==='ice_axe'){
      var ih = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.CylinderGeometry(R*0.06,R*0.06,R*1.1,8), m);
      ih.rotation.z = 1.0; grp.add(ih);
      var ix = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(R*0.5,R*0.28,R*0.08), m);
      ix.position.set(R*0.42,R*0.35,0); grp.add(ix);
    } else if(weaponId==='thunder_hammer'){
      var th = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.CylinderGeometry(R*0.06,R*0.07,R*1.0,8),
        new THREE.MeshLambertMaterial({color:0x5a3a1e}));
      th.rotation.z = 1.05; grp.add(th);
      var hm2 = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(R*0.55,R*0.35,R*0.3),
        new THREE.MeshLambertMaterial({ color:0xffe95c, emissive:0x7a5a00 }));
      hm2.position.set(R*0.4, R*0.32, 0); hm2.rotation.z = 0.15; grp.add(hm2);
    } else if(weaponId==='plasma_blaster'){
      var tb = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.CylinderGeometry(R*0.14,R*0.18,R*0.7,10), m);
      tb.rotation.z = Math.PI/2; grp.add(tb);
      var tip = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.ConeGeometry(R*0.14,R*0.3,10), m);
      tip.rotation.z = -Math.PI/2; tip.position.x = R*0.5; grp.add(tip);
    } else if(weaponId==='bubble_hose'){
      var bh = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.CylinderGeometry(R*0.13,R*0.16,R*0.65,10), m);
      bh.rotation.z = Math.PI/2; grp.add(bh);
      var bn = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.CylinderGeometry(R*0.06,R*0.09,R*0.3,8), m);
      bn.rotation.z = Math.PI/2; bn.position.x = R*0.45; grp.add(bn);
      var bb2 = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.SphereGeometry(R*0.17,10,8),
        new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({ color:0xcfeeff }));
      bb2.position.set(R*0.62, R*0.14, 0); grp.add(bb2);
      var bb3 = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.SphereGeometry(R*0.11,10,8),
        new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({ color:0xffd7f4 }));
      bb3.position.set(R*0.55, -R*0.12, R*0.1); grp.add(bb3);
    } else if(weaponId==='void_blade'){
      var vb = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(R*0.12,R*1.35,R*0.05),
        new THREE.MeshLambertMaterial({ color:0x2a2a3a, emissive:0x6a3aa0 }));
      vb.rotation.z = 0.9; grp.add(vb);
    } else {
      /* generic fallback: never render an invisible weapon */
      var fb = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.CylinderGeometry(R*0.07,R*0.09,R*1.1,8), m);
      fb.rotation.z = 1.1; grp.add(fb);
    }
  }catch(e){}
  grp.position.set(R*1.35, R*0.1, 0);
  grp.userData.baseY = R*0.1;
  grp.visible = true; /* weapons are always carried, not just flashed on attack */
  return grp;
}

/* dispose a dressed-on group (geometry+material; never shared textures) */
function fbDisposeObj(gr){
  if(!gr) return;
  try{ gr.traverse(function(o){ if(o.geometry) o.geometry.dispose(); if(o.material){ var ms=Array.isArray(o.material)?o.material:[o.material]; for(var i=0;i<ms.length;i++){ try{ms[i].dispose();}catch(e){} } } }); }catch(e){}
}
/* ---------------- dress a baby group with a look ---------------- */
function fbDressGroup(g, look, armorId, weaponId){
  if(!g || !g.userData || !g.userData.body) return;
  try{
    var pal = FB_COLORS[look.c] || FB_COLORS[0];
    var u = g.userData;
    if(u.body.material.map){ try{ u.body.material.map.dispose(); }catch(e){} }
    u.body.material.map = bodyTexture({ body:pal.body, dark:pal.dark, core:pal.core }, look.e);
    u.body.material.needsUpdate = true;
    if(typeof limbMat === 'function'){
      var am = limbMat(pal.body), dm = limbMat(pal.dark);
      if(u.armL){ u.armL.material=am; u.armR.material=am; u.legL.material=dm; u.legR.material=dm; }
    }
    if(u.fbAcc){ g.remove(u.fbAcc); fbDisposeObj(u.fbAcc); u.fbAcc = null; }
    if(u.fbArmor){ g.remove(u.fbArmor); fbDisposeObj(u.fbArmor); u.fbArmor = null; }
    fbStyleHair(g, look.h);
    u.fbAcc = fbBuildAccessory(g, look.a);
    if(armorId) u.fbArmor = fbBuildArmor(g, armorId);
    fbAttachWeapon(g, weaponId || 'stick');
  }catch(e){}
}
function fbDressPlayer(){
  try{
    if(typeof player==='undefined' || !player || !player.group) return;
    fbDressGroup(player.group, FBC.look, FBC.inv.eqArmor, FBC.inv.eqWeapon);
    fbRefreshWeapon();
  }catch(e){}
}
function fbAttachWeapon(g, weaponId){
  try{
    if(!g || !g.userData) return;
    var u = g.userData;
    if(u.fbWeapon){ g.remove(u.fbWeapon); if(typeof fbDisposeObj==='function') fbDisposeObj(u.fbWeapon); u.fbWeapon = null; }
    u.fbWeapon = fbBuildWeaponMesh(weaponId || 'stick', (u&&u.R)||1);
    g.add(u.fbWeapon);
  }catch(e){}
}
function fbRefreshWeapon(){
  try{
    if(typeof player==='undefined' || !player || !player.group) return;
    fbAttachWeapon(player.group, FBC.inv.eqWeapon || 'stick');
  }catch(e){}
}
function fbLookNet(){
  return { c:FBC.look.c, e:FBC.look.e, h:FBC.look.h, a:FBC.look.a,
           ar:FBC.inv.eqArmor, w:FBC.inv.eqWeapon };
}
function fbSendLook(){
  try{
    if(typeof net!=='undefined' && net && net.connected && typeof mpInGame!=='undefined' && mpInGame)
      net.sendAction({ kind:'look', data:fbLookNet() });
  }catch(e){}
}

/* ---------------- weapon swing (called from attack-anim timers) ---------------- */
function fbSwingWeapon(g, k){
  try{
    if(!g || !g.userData || !g.userData.fbWeapon) return;
    var w = g.userData.fbWeapon;
    var by = (w.userData && typeof w.userData.baseY==='number') ? w.userData.baseY : 0.1;
    if(k > 0.01){ w.rotation.x = -1.5 * k; w.position.y = by + 0.4 * k; }
    else { w.rotation.x = 0; w.position.y = by; }
  }catch(e){}
}

/* ---------------- extinguish smoke particles ---------------- */
function fbSpawnSmoke(pos, n){
  n = n || 12;
  try{
    if(typeof poolSpawn==='function') poolSpawn(pos, 0xdcdcdc, false, n, 0, true);
    else { /* fallback if game script pool missing */
      for(var i=0;i<n;i++){
        var s = new THREE.Sprite(new THREE.SpriteMaterial({ map:GLOW_TEX, color:0xdcdcdc,
          transparent:true, opacity:0.8, depthWrite:false }));
        s.position.copy(pos);
        s.position.x += (Math.random()-0.5)*1.4;
        s.position.z += (Math.random()-0.5)*1.4;
        var sc = 0.7+Math.random()*0.9; s.scale.set(sc,sc,1);
        s.userData = { vx:(Math.random()-0.5)*1.6, vy:1.6+Math.random()*2.4, vz:(Math.random()-0.5)*1.6,
          life:0.9+Math.random()*0.7, age:0, smoke:true };
        scene.add(s); particles.push(s);
      }
    }
  }catch(e){}
}

/* ---------------- fire-extinguish death poof ---------------- */
function fbExtinguishPoof(g){
  if(!g || !g.userData || g.userData.poofed) return;
  var u = g.userData; u.poofed = true;
  try{ u.poofGlow=(u.glow&&u.glow.material)?u.glow.material.opacity:0.85; }catch(e){}
  try{
    var pos = g.position.clone(); pos.y += 1.3;
    fbSpawnSmoke(pos, 14);
    if(u.glow && u.glow.material) u.glow.material.opacity = 0.06;
    u.poofLicks = [];
    if(u.licks) for(var i=0;i<u.licks.length;i++){
      try{
        var L = u.licks[i];
        u.poofLicks.push({ m:L.material, c:L.material.color.getHex(),
          e:(L.material.emissive ? L.material.emissive.getHex() : 0) });
        L.material.color.setHex(0x8c8c8c);
        if(L.material.emissive) L.material.emissive.setHex(0x000000);
      }catch(e){}
    }
    if(u.body && u.body.material && u.body.material.emissive) u.body.material.emissive.setRGB(0.03,0.03,0.04);
    try{ g.scale.multiplyScalar(0.88); }catch(e){}
  }catch(e){}
  fbSizzleSound();
}
function fbReignite(g){
  if(!g || !g.userData) return;
  var u = g.userData; u.poofed = false;
  try{ if(u.glow&&u.glow.material) u.glow.material.opacity=(typeof u.poofGlow==='number'?u.poofGlow:0.85); }catch(e){}
  try{
    g.scale.set(1,1,1);
    if(u.poofLicks) for(var i=0;i<u.poofLicks.length;i++){
      try{ var sv=u.poofLicks[i]; sv.m.color.setHex(sv.c); if(sv.m.emissive) sv.m.emissive.setHex(sv.e); }catch(e){}
    }
    u.poofLicks = null;
    if(u.body && u.body.material && u.body.material.emissive) u.body.material.emissive.setRGB(0,0,0);
    var pos = g.position.clone(); pos.y += 1.0;
    spawnBurst(pos, 0xff8c2e, 12, 4);
  }catch(e){}
  fbReigniteSound();
}
function fbSizzleSound(){
  try{
    if(typeof fbMuted!=='undefined' && fbMuted) return;
    if(!AC) AC = new (window.AudioContext||window.webkitAudioContext)();
    var t = AC.currentTime, dur = 0.4;
    var len = Math.floor(AC.sampleRate * dur), buf = AC.createBuffer(1, len, AC.sampleRate), d = buf.getChannelData(0);
    for(var i=0;i<len;i++){ d[i] = (Math.random()*2-1) * Math.pow(1-i/len, 2.2); }
    var src = AC.createBufferSource(); src.buffer = buf;
    var f = AC.createBiquadFilter(); f.type = 'lowpass';
    f.frequency.setValueAtTime(3200, t); f.frequency.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(350, t+dur);
    var gn = AC.createGain(); gn.gain.setValueAtTime(0.22, t); gn.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.001, t+dur);
    src.connect(f); f.connect(gn); gn.connect(AC.destination); src.start(t);
    var o = AC.createOscillator(); o.type = 'sawtooth';
    o.frequency.setValueAtTime(850, t); o.frequency.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(110, t+0.5);
    var g2 = AC.createGain(); g2.gain.setValueAtTime(0.05, t); g2.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.001, t+0.5);
    o.connect(g2); g2.connect(AC.destination); o.start(t); o.stop(t+0.55);
  }catch(e){}
}
function fbReigniteSound(){
  try{
    if(typeof fbMuted!=='undefined' && fbMuted) return;
    if(!AC) AC = new (window.AudioContext||window.webkitAudioContext)();
    var t = AC.currentTime;
    var o = AC.createOscillator(); o.type = 'triangle';
    o.frequency.setValueAtTime(180, t); o.frequency.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(720, t+0.28);
    var g = AC.createGain(); g.gain.setValueAtTime(0.07, t); g.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.001, t+0.3);
    o.connect(g); g.connect(AC.destination); o.start(t); o.stop(t+0.32);
  }catch(e){}
}

/* ---------------- remote look handling ---------------- */
var fbRemoteLooks = {};
function fbDressRemote(id){
  try{
    var rp = (typeof remotePlayers!=='undefined') ? remotePlayers[id] : null;
    var lk = fbRemoteLooks[id];
    if(!rp || !rp.group || !lk) return;
    var look = { c:(typeof lk.c==='number'?lk.c:0), e:lk.e||'happy', h:lk.h||'wild', a:lk.a||'none' };
    fbDressGroup(rp.group, look, lk.ar||null, lk.w||'stick');
  }catch(e){}
}

/* ---------------- emotes ---------------- */
var FB_EMOTE_DUR = {};
FB_EMOTES.forEach(function(e){ FB_EMOTE_DUR[e.id] = e.dur; });
function fbPlayEmote(id, g){
  if(!g || !g.userData) return;
  try{
    var u = g.userData;
    if(u.fbEmote) return; // one at a time
    if(u.fbKO) return;
    u.fbEmote = { id:id, t:0, dur:FB_EMOTE_DUR[id]||1.2, y0:g.position.y };
    beepOk();
  }catch(e){}
}
function fbEmoteFrame(g, dt){
  try{
    var u = g.userData, em = u.fbEmote;
    if(!em) return;
    em.t += dt;
    var k = Math.min(1, em.t/em.dur), done = em.t >= em.dur;
    if(em.id==='wave'){
      g.rotation.z = Math.sin(em.t*10)*0.25*(1-k);
      g.position.y = em.y0 + Math.abs(Math.sin(em.t*8))*0.12;
    } else if(em.id==='dance'){
      g.rotation.y += dt*7;
      g.position.y = em.y0 + Math.abs(Math.sin(em.t*10))*0.22;
    } else if(em.id==='spin'){
      g.rotation.y += dt*(Math.PI*2/em.dur);
    } else if(em.id==='clap'){
      g.rotation.z = Math.sin(em.t*16)*0.18*(1-k);
      g.position.y = em.y0 + Math.abs(Math.sin(em.t*16))*0.08;
    } else if(em.id==='flip'){
      g.rotation.x = (em.t/em.dur)*Math.PI*2;
    } else if(em.id==='backflip'){
      g.rotation.x = -(em.t/em.dur)*Math.PI*2;
      g.position.y = em.y0 + Math.sin(Math.min(1,em.t/em.dur)*Math.PI)*0.8;
    } else if(em.id==='fire_spin'){
      g.rotation.y += dt*12;
      var s = 1 + Math.sin(em.t*16)*0.1;
      g.scale.set(s, 2-s, s);
      u.flash = Math.max(u.flash||0, 0.45);
    } else if(em.id==='moonwalk'){
      g.rotation.x = -0.3;
      g.position.y = em.y0 + Math.abs(Math.sin(em.t*12))*0.07;
      g.rotation.z = Math.sin(em.t*6)*0.1;
    } else if(em.id==='levitate'){
      g.position.y = em.y0 + Math.sin(Math.min(1,em.t/em.dur)*Math.PI)*1.1;
      g.rotation.y += dt*4;
      u.flash = Math.max(u.flash||0, 0.4);
    } else if(em.id==='super_saiyan'){
      var s2 = 1 + Math.sin(em.t*20)*0.12;
      g.scale.set(s2, 2-s2, s2);
      g.rotation.y += dt*9;
      u.flash = Math.max(u.flash||0, 0.6);
    } else if(em.id==='taunt'){
      var s3 = 1 + Math.sin(em.t*14)*0.08*(1-k);
      g.scale.set(s3, 2-s3, s3);
      u.flash = Math.max(u.flash||0, 0.35);
    } else if(em.id==='victory'){
      g.rotation.y += dt*5;
      g.position.y = em.y0 + Math.sin(Math.min(1,em.t/0.5)*Math.PI)*0.9;
    }
    if(done){
      g.rotation.x = 0; g.rotation.z = 0; g.scale.set(1,1,1);
      if(typeof em.y0==='number') g.position.y = em.y0;
      u.fbEmote = null;
    }
  }catch(e){ try{ g.userData.fbEmote=null; }catch(e2){} }
}
function fbDoEmote(id){
  try{
    if(typeof player==='undefined' || !player || !player.group || player.dead) return;
    if(typeof mpKO!=='undefined' && mpKO) return;
    if(typeof mpAtkAnimT!=='undefined' && mpAtkAnimT>0) return;
    fbPlayEmote(id, player.group);
    try{
      if(typeof net!=='undefined' && net && net.connected && typeof mpInGame!=='undefined' && mpInGame)
        net.sendAction({ kind:'emote', data:{ id:id } });
    }catch(e){}
  }catch(e){}
}
function fbToggleEmotePop(){
  var p = $('fbemotepop'); if(!p) return;
  if(p.classList.contains('on')){ p.classList.remove('on'); return; }
  p.innerHTML = '';
  FBC.inv.emotes.forEach(function(id){
    var em = emoteById(id);
    var b = document.createElement('button');
    b.textContent = em.e; b.title = em.n;
    b.addEventListener('click', function(){ p.classList.remove('on'); fbDoEmote(id); });
    p.appendChild(b);
  });
  p.classList.add('on');
}

/* ---------------- per-frame hook (emotes, rainbow aura, weapon) ---------------- */
function fbBabyFrame(g, dt){
  fbEmoteFrame(g, dt);
  try{
    var u = g.userData;
    if(u.fbArmor && u.fbArmor.userData && u.fbArmor.userData.rainbow){
      var hue = (Date.now()/12)%360;
      u.fbArmor.userData.rainbow.color.setHSL(hue/360, 1, 0.6);
    }
    /* weapons stay visible at all times; swing is driven by attack-anim timers */
  }catch(e){}
}

/* ---------------- net sync ---------------- */
var fbNetWired = false;
function fbWireNet(){
  if(fbNetWired) return;
  try{
    if(typeof net==='undefined' || !net || typeof net.on!=='function') return;
    fbNetWired = true;
    net.on('action', function(msg){
      if(!msg || !msg.id) return;
      if(msg.kind==='look'){
        fbRemoteLooks[msg.id] = msg.data||{};
        fbDressRemote(msg.id);
      } else if(msg.kind==='emote'){
        try{
          var rp = remotePlayers[msg.id];
          if(rp && rp.group && !rp.ko) fbPlayEmote((msg.data&&msg.data.id)||'wave', rp.group);
        }catch(e){}
      } else if(msg.kind==='ko'){
        /* I scored the KO -> coins, gem every 5 KOs */
        try{
          if(msg.data && msg.data.by && typeof net!=='undefined' && msg.data.by===net.playerId){
            FBC.kos++; saveState();
            fbAddCoins(5, '+5 🪙 KO!');
            if(FBC.kos % 5 === 0) fbAddGems(1, '+1 💎 KO streak!', true);
          }
        }catch(e){}
      }
    });
    net.on('end', function(){
      fbAddCoins(10, 'Game complete! +10 🪙');
      fbAddGems(1, null, true); /* 1 gem per game played */
    });
  }catch(e){}
}

/* ---------------- 3D preview (FIXED: renders the working draft) ---------------- */
var fbPrev = null;
function fbPreviewEnsure(){
  if(fbPrev && fbPrev.r) return fbPrev;
  /* drop a dead renderer so we can retry cleanly */
  if(fbPrev && fbPrev.raf){ try{ cancelAnimationFrame(fbPrev.raf); }catch(e){} }
  fbPrev = null;
  try{
    var cv = $('custpreview');
    if(!cv) return null;
    var renderer = new THREE.WebGLRenderer({ canvas:cv, alpha:true, antialias:(typeof FBQ!=='undefined'?FBQ.antialias:true) });
    renderer.setSize(220,220);
    renderer.setPixelRatio(Math.min((typeof FBQ!=='undefined'?FBQ.pixelRatio:2),1.5));
    var scene = new THREE.Scene();
    var cam = new THREE.PerspectiveCamera(38, 1, 0.1, 100);
    cam.position.set(0, 1.4, 5.4); cam.lookAt(0, 0.7, 0);
    scene.add(new THREE.AmbientLight(0xffffff, 0.75));
    var dl = new THREE.DirectionalLight(0xffe0b0, 1.1);
    dl.position.set(3, 6, 4); scene.add(dl);
    fbPrev = { r:renderer, s:scene, c:cam, baby:null, raf:0 };
  }catch(e){ fbPrev = null; }
  return fbPrev;
}
/* 2D fallback when WebGL is unavailable: draw the face texture directly */
function fbPreviewFallback2D(look){
  try{
    look = look || fbCustDraft || FBC.look;
    var cv = $('custpreview');
    if(!cv || !cv.getContext) return;
    var ctx = cv.getContext('2d');
    var pal = FB_COLORS[look.c]||FB_COLORS[0];
    var t = bodyTexture({ body:pal.body, dark:pal.dark, core:pal.core }, look.e);
    ctx.clearRect(0,0,cv.width,cv.height);
    if(t && t.image) ctx.drawImage(t.image, 0, 0, cv.width, cv.height);
  }catch(e){}
}
/* FIX: dress from the passed look (the draft), not the saved FBC.look */
function fbPreviewDress(look){
  var P = fbPrev; if(!P) { fbPreviewFallback2D(look); return; }
  look = look || fbCustDraft || FBC.look;
  try{
    if(P.baby){ P.s.remove(P.baby); P.baby = null; }
    var pal = FB_COLORS[look.c]||FB_COLORS[0];
    var spec = { id:'preview', name:'You', body:pal.body, dark:pal.dark, core:pal.core, extra:null };
    var g = makeBaby(spec, look.e, 1);
    g.userData.baseY = 0.4;
    g.position.set(0, 0.4, 0);
    fbStyleHair(g, look.h);
    g.userData.fbAcc = fbBuildAccessory(g, look.a);
    /* show currently equipped armor in the preview too */
    try{
      if(FBC.inv.eqArmor) g.userData.fbArmor = fbBuildArmor(g, FBC.inv.eqArmor);
    }catch(e2){}
    /* show currently equipped weapon in the preview too */
    try{
      var _pw = (FBC.inv && FBC.inv.eqWeapon) || 'stick';
      if(typeof fbBuildWeaponMesh==='function') g.userData.fbWeapon = fbBuildWeaponMesh(_pw, 1);
    }catch(e3){}
    P.s.add(g); P.baby = g;
  }catch(e){ fbPreviewFallback2D(look); }
}
function fbPreviewShow(){
  var P = fbPreviewEnsure();
  var look = fbCustDraft || FBC.look;
  if(!P){ fbPreviewFallback2D(look); return; }
  fbPreviewDress(look);
  var ov = $('customizeov');
  function loop(){
    if(!ov || ov.style.display==='none'){ P.raf = 0; return; }
    try{
      var t = performance.now()/1000;
      if(P.baby){ P.baby.rotation.y = Math.sin(t*0.6)*0.35; updateBaby(P.baby, t, 0.016); }
      P.r.render(P.s, P.c);
    }catch(e){}
    P.raf = requestAnimationFrame(loop);
  }
  if(!P.raf) loop();
}

/* ---------------- customize UI ---------------- */
var fbCustDraft = null;
function fbOpenCustomize(){
  fbCustDraft = JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(FBC.look));
  var ov = $('customizeov'); if(!ov) return;
  if(typeof hideHub==='function') hideHub();
  ov.style.display = 'flex';
  fbBuildCustRows();
  fbPreviewShow();
}
function fbCloseCustomize(){
  var ov = $('customizeov'); if(ov) ov.style.display='none';
  try{ if(fbPrev && fbPrev.raf){ cancelAnimationFrame(fbPrev.raf); fbPrev.raf = 0; } }catch(e){}
  if(typeof showHub==='function') showHub();
}
function fbBuildCustRows(){
  var d = fbCustDraft;
  if(!d) return;
  var cc = $('custcolors'); cc.innerHTML='';
  FB_COLORS.forEach(function(pal, i){
    var b = document.createElement('button');
    b.className = 'swbtn'+(d.c===i?' sel':'');
    b.title = pal.n;
    b.innerHTML = '<div class="swdot" style="background:'+pal.body+'"></div>';
    b.addEventListener('click', function(){ d.c=i; beepOk(); fbBuildCustRows(); fbPreviewDress(d); });
    cc.appendChild(b);
  });
  var ce = $('custeyes'); ce.innerHTML='';
  FB_EYES.forEach(function(e){
    var b = document.createElement('button');
    b.className = 'swbtn'+(d.e===e.id?' sel':'');
    b.innerHTML = '<div style="font-size:24px">'+e.e+'</div><div style="font-size:10px">'+e.n+'</div>';
    b.addEventListener('click', function(){ d.e=e.id; beepOk(); fbBuildCustRows(); fbPreviewDress(d); });
    ce.appendChild(b);
  });
  var ch = $('custhair'); ch.innerHTML='';
  FB_HAIR.forEach(function(h){
    var b = document.createElement('button');
    b.className = 'swbtn'+(d.h===h.id?' sel':'');
    b.textContent = h.n;
    b.addEventListener('click', function(){ d.h=h.id; beepOk(); fbBuildCustRows(); fbPreviewDress(d); });
    ch.appendChild(b);
  });
  var ca = $('custacc'); ca.innerHTML='';
  FB_ACC.forEach(function(a){
    var b = document.createElement('button');
    b.className = 'swbtn'+(d.a===a.id?' sel':'');
    b.innerHTML = '<div style="font-size:24px">'+a.e+'</div><div style="font-size:10px">'+a.n+'</div>';
    b.addEventListener('click', function(){ d.a=a.id; beepOk(); fbBuildCustRows(); fbPreviewDress(d); });
    ca.appendChild(b);
  });
  /* WEAPON: pick from owned weapons (free Stick + store purchases) */
  var cw = $('custweapon');
  if(cw){
    cw.innerHTML='';
    var owned = (FBC.inv && FBC.inv.weapons) || ['stick'];
    var eqW = (FBC.inv && FBC.inv.eqWeapon) || 'stick';
    owned.forEach(function(wid){
      var w = weaponById(wid); if(!w) return;
      var b = document.createElement('button');
      b.className = 'swbtn'+(eqW===w.id?' sel':'');
      b.innerHTML = '<div style="font-size:24px">'+w.e+'</div><div style="font-size:10px">'+w.n+'</div>';
      b.addEventListener('click', function(){
        if(FBC.inv) FBC.inv.eqWeapon = w.id;
        saveState(); beepOk();
        fbBuildCustRows(); fbPreviewDress(d);
        if(typeof fbDressPlayer==='function') fbDressPlayer();
      });
      cw.appendChild(b);
    });
    /* hint to visit store for more weapons */
    var hint = document.createElement('div');
    hint.style.cssText = 'font-size:11px;opacity:0.7;margin-top:4px;width:100%;text-align:center';
    hint.textContent = 'More weapons in the STORE 🛒';
    cw.appendChild(hint);
  }
}
function fbSaveCustomize(){
  if(!fbCustDraft) return;
  FBC.look = JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(fbCustDraft));
  saveState(); beepOk();
  fbDressPlayer();
  fbSendLook();
  toastOk('🎨 Look saved!');
  fbCloseCustomize();
}

/* ---------------- store UI ---------------- */
var fbStoreTab = 'armor';
var fbConfirmId = null, fbConfirmTimer = 0;
function fbOpenStore(){
  var ov = $('storeov'); if(!ov) return;
  if(typeof hideHub==='function') hideHub();
  ov.style.display = 'flex';
  fbRefreshCurrencyUI();
  fbRenderStore();
}
function fbCloseStore(){
  var ov = $('storeov'); if(ov) ov.style.display='none';
  if(typeof showHub==='function') showHub();
}
function fbStoreItems(tab){
  if(tab==='armor') return FB_ARMOR;
  if(tab==='weapons') return FB_STORE_WEAPONS;
  if(tab==='emotes') return FB_EMOTES;
  return [];
}
function fbIsOwned(tab, id){
  if(tab==='armor') return FBC.inv.armor.indexOf(id)>=0;
  if(tab==='weapons') return FBC.inv.weapons.indexOf(id)>=0;
  if(tab==='emotes') return FBC.inv.emotes.indexOf(id)>=0;
  return false;
}
function fbIsEquipped(tab, id){
  if(tab==='armor') return FBC.inv.eqArmor===id;
  if(tab==='weapons') return FBC.inv.eqWeapon===id;
  if(tab==='emotes') return FBC.inv.favEmote===id;
  return false;
}
function fbPriceText(it){
  if(it.p===0) return 'FREE';
  return (it.cur==='gems' ? '💎 ' : '🪙 ') + it.p + (it.cur==='gems' ? ' GEMS' : ' COINS');
}
function fbCanAfford(it){
  if(it.p===0) return true;
  return (it.cur==='gems' ? FBC.gems : FBC.coins) >= it.p;
}
function fbRenderStore(){
  var grid = $('storegrid'); if(!grid) return;
  grid.innerHTML = '';
  fbRefreshCurrencyUI();
  if(fbStoreTab==='gems'){ fbStoreTab='armor'; } /* GEMS tab hidden until real payments exist */
  fbStoreItems(fbStoreTab).forEach(function(it){
    var owned = fbIsOwned(fbStoreTab, it.id), eq = fbIsEquipped(fbStoreTab, it.id);
    var card = document.createElement('div');
    card.className = 'sitem'+(owned?' owned':'')+(eq?' equipped':'')+(fbConfirmId===it.id?' confirm':'');
    var priceHtml;
    if(owned){
      priceHtml = eq ? '<div><span class="badge eq">EQUIPPED</span></div>'
                     : '<div><span class="badge ow">OWNED — TAP TO EQUIP</span></div>';
    } else {
      priceHtml = '<div class="pr'+(fbCanAfford(it)?'':' lock')+'">'+esc(fbPriceText(it))+'</div>';
      if(fbConfirmId===it.id)
        priceHtml = '<div class="pr" style="color:#ff5a3c">TAP AGAIN TO BUY</div>';
    }
    card.innerHTML = '<div class="em">'+it.e+'</div><div class="nm">'+esc(it.n)+'</div>'+
      '<div class="ds">'+esc(it.d)+'</div>'+priceHtml;
    card.addEventListener('click', function(){ fbStoreTap(it); });
    grid.appendChild(card);
  });
}
function fbStoreTap(it){
  beepOk();
  var tab = fbStoreTab;
  if(fbIsOwned(tab, it.id)){
    if(tab==='armor') FBC.inv.eqArmor = (FBC.inv.eqArmor===it.id) ? null : it.id;
    else if(tab==='weapons') FBC.inv.eqWeapon = it.id;
    else if(tab==='emotes') FBC.inv.favEmote = it.id;
    saveState(); fbDressPlayer(); fbSendLook(); fbRenderStore();
    toastOk('✅ Equipped: '+it.n);
    return;
  }
  if(!fbCanAfford(it)){
    toastOk(it.cur==='gems' ? '💎 Not enough gems! Win games or grab a pack.'
                            : '🪙 Not enough coins! Play games to earn more.');
    return;
  }
  if(fbConfirmId===it.id){
    fbConfirmId = null;
    if(it.cur==='gems') FBC.gems -= it.p; else FBC.coins -= it.p;
    if(tab==='armor'){ FBC.inv.armor.push(it.id); FBC.inv.eqArmor = it.id; }
    else if(tab==='weapons'){ FBC.inv.weapons.push(it.id); FBC.inv.eqWeapon = it.id; }
    else if(tab==='emotes'){ FBC.inv.emotes.push(it.id); FBC.inv.favEmote = it.id; }
    saveState(); fbDressPlayer(); fbSendLook(); fbRenderStore();
    toastOk('🎉 You got: '+it.n+'!');
  } else {
    fbConfirmId = it.id;
    clearTimeout(fbConfirmTimer);
    fbConfirmTimer = setTimeout(function(){ fbConfirmId=null; fbRenderStore(); }, 3000);
    fbRenderStore();
  }
}
/* ---- GEMS tab: real-money gem packs via Stripe Payment Links ---- */
function fbRenderGemPacks(grid){
  var wrap = document.createElement('div');
  wrap.style.cssText = 'grid-column:1/-1;text-align:center;color:#ffe9d6;padding:6px 4px';
  var html = '<div style="font-size:40px">💎</div>'+
    '<div style="font:900 18px system-ui;margin:6px 0">GEM PACKS</div>'+
    '<div style="font:400 12px system-ui;color:#d8b89a;margin-bottom:10px;line-height:1.6">'+
    'Gems unlock premium armor, weapons & emotes.<br>Earn free gems by winning games — or top up instantly.</div>'+
    '<div class="gempacks">';
  FB_GEM_PACKS.forEach(function(pk, i){
    html += '<div class="gpack">'+
      '<div class="gq">💎 '+pk.gems+'</div>'+
      '<div class="gt">'+pk.tag+'</div>'+
      '<button class="gbigbtn" data-pk="'+i+'">'+pk.usd+' — BUY</button>'+
      '</div>';
  });
  html += '</div>'+
    '<div style="font:400 11px system-ui;color:#a88a6a;margin-top:10px;line-height:1.6">'+
    'Secure checkout via Stripe. Gems are credited after payment completes.</div>';
  wrap.innerHTML = html;
  grid.appendChild(wrap);
  var btns = wrap.querySelectorAll('.gbigbtn');
  for(var i=0;i<btns.length;i++){
    (function(b){
      b.addEventListener('click', function(e){
        e.stopPropagation(); beepOk();
        fbBuyGemPack(parseInt(b.getAttribute('data-pk'),10));
      });
    })(btns[i]);
  }
}
function fbBuyGemPack(i){
  var pk = FB_GEM_PACKS[i]; if(!pk) return;
  try{
    /* opens Stripe checkout in a new tab; game stays open */
    window.open(pk.link, '_blank', 'noopener');
    toastOk('💎 Opening secure checkout…');
  }catch(e){}
}


/* ---------------- graphics quality settings ---------------- */
function fbOpenSettings(){
  try{
    if($('fb_setov')){ $('fb_setov').style.display='flex'; return; }
    var ov=document.createElement('div');
    ov.id='fb_setov';
    ov.style.cssText='position:fixed;inset:0;z-index:60;display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:center;background:rgba(5,2,1,.78);';
    var cur=(typeof FBQ!=='undefined')?FBQ.level:'high';
    function qbtn(q, label, desc){
      var on=(q===cur);
      return '<button data-q="'+q+'" style="display:block;width:100%;margin:8px 0;padding:14px 18px;border-radius:12px;cursor:pointer;'+
        'background:'+(on?'#ffb347':'#2a1408')+';color:'+(on?'#3a1c05':'#ffe9d6')+';'+
        'border:2px solid '+(on?'#fff':'#a05a20')+';font:900 16px system-ui;">'+label+
        '<div style="font:400 12px system-ui;opacity:.85;margin-top:4px;">'+desc+'</div></button>';
    }
    ov.innerHTML='<div style="background:#160a04;border:2px solid #ff8c2e;border-radius:18px;padding:26px 30px;max-width:86vw;width:340px;text-align:center;">'+
      '<div style="font:900 20px system-ui;color:#ffd76a;margin-bottom:6px;">⚙️ GRAPHICS</div>'+
      '<div style="font:400 12px system-ui;color:#ffb;border-bottom:1px solid #5a2c10;padding-bottom:10px;margin-bottom:6px;">Applies instantly (page reloads)</div>'+
      qbtn('high','🔥 HIGH','Best visuals — shadows, full particles')+
      qbtn('medium','⚡ MEDIUM','Balanced — smaller shadows, fewer particles')+
      qbtn('low','🌱 LOW','Fastest — no shadows, minimal particles')+
      '<button id="fb_setclose" style="margin-top:10px;padding:10px 22px;border-radius:999px;background:#3a1c05;color:#ffd;border:2px solid #a05a20;font:900 14px system-ui;cursor:pointer;">CLOSE</button></div>';
    document.body.appendChild(ov);
    ov.addEventListener('click', function(e){
      var q=e.target&&e.target.getAttribute?e.target.getAttribute('data-q'):null;
      if(q){ try{ localStorage.setItem('fb_quality', q); }catch(x){} location.reload(); return; }
      if(e.target===ov || (e.target&&e.target.id==='fb_setclose')) ov.style.display='none';
    });
  }catch(e){}
}

/* ---------------- hub integration ---------------- */
function fbAddHubButtons(){
  var wrap = $('hubbtns'); if(!wrap) return;
  if(!$('hubgempill')){
    var panel = wrap.parentElement;
    var gp = document.createElement('div');
    gp.id = 'hubgempill';
    gp.style.cssText = 'text-align:center;margin-bottom:10px;pointer-events:auto;background:rgba(30,14,6,.9);'+
      'border:2px solid #4de88a;border-radius:999px;padding:8px 18px;color:#ffe9d6;font:900 15px system-ui;';
    if(panel) panel.insertBefore(gp, wrap);
  }
  fbRefreshCurrencyUI();
  if(!$('fb_custbtn')){
    var b = document.createElement('button');
    b.className='modebtn'; b.id='fb_custbtn';
    b.innerHTML = '<div class="ic">🎨</div><div><div class="nm">CUSTOMIZE</div>'+
      '<div class="ds">Design your Fire Baby!</div></div>';
    b.addEventListener('click', function(){ beepOk(); fbOpenCustomize(); });
    wrap.insertBefore(b, wrap.firstChild);
  }
  if(!$('fb_storebtn')){
    var s = document.createElement('button');
    s.className='modebtn'; s.id='fb_storebtn';
    s.innerHTML = '<div class="ic">🛒</div><div><div class="nm">STORE</div>'+
      '<div class="ds">Armor, weapons, emotes, gems!</div></div>';
    s.addEventListener('click', function(){ beepOk(); fbOpenStore(); });
    var cb = $('fb_custbtn');
    wrap.insertBefore(s, cb ? cb.nextSibling : wrap.firstChild);
  }
  if(!$('fb_setbtn')){
    var st = document.createElement('button');
    st.className='modebtn'; st.id='fb_setbtn';
    st.innerHTML = '<div class="ic">\u2699\uFE0F</div><div><div class="nm">SETTINGS</div>'+
      '<div class="ds">Graphics quality</div></div>';
    st.addEventListener('click', function(){ beepOk(); fbOpenSettings(); });
    wrap.appendChild(st);
  }
}

/* ---------------- init: patches ---------------- */
function fbInit(){
  loadJSON('fb_customization', DEF_LOOK);
  try{
    if(typeof renderHub==='function'){
      var _rh = renderHub;
      renderHub = function(){ _rh(); try{ fbAddHubButtons(); }catch(e){} };
    }
  }catch(e){}
  try{
    if(typeof createSquad==='function'){
      var _cs = createSquad;
      createSquad = function(x,z){ _cs(x,z); fbDressPlayer(); };
    }
  }catch(e){}
  try{
    if(typeof updateBaby==='function'){
      var _ub = updateBaby;
      updateBaby = function(g,t,dt){ _ub(g,t,dt); fbBabyFrame(g,dt); };
    }
  }catch(e){}
  /* +10 coins, +1 gem on single-player victory */
  try{
    if(typeof showVictoryPanel==='function'){
      var _vp = showVictoryPanel;
      showVictoryPanel = function(a,b,c,d,e,f){
        _vp(a,b,c,d,e,f);
        fbAddCoins(10, 'Game complete! +10 🪙');
        fbAddGems(1, null, true);
      };
    }
  }catch(e){}
  /* +2 coins per local extinguish (multiplayer) */
  try{
    if(typeof mpSendExtinguish==='function'){
      var _me = mpSendExtinguish;
      mpSendExtinguish = function(x,z){ _me(x,z); fbAddCoins(2, null, true); };
    }
  }catch(e){}
  try{
    if(typeof mpSpawnRemote==='function'){
      var _sr = mpSpawnRemote;
      mpSpawnRemote = function(p){ _sr(p); try{ if(fbRemoteLooks[p.id]) fbDressRemote(p.id); }catch(e){} };
    }
  }catch(e){}
  try{
    if(typeof mpEnterGame==='function'){
      var _eg = mpEnterGame;
      mpEnterGame = function(){ _eg(); fbSendLook(); };
    }
  }catch(e){}
  if(!window._fbNetTimer){
    window._fbNetTimer = true;
    setInterval(function(){ fbWireNet(); }, 1000);
  }
  try{
    var eb = $('fbemotebtn');
    if(eb) eb.addEventListener('click', function(e){ e.preventDefault(); fbToggleEmotePop(); });
    if(!window._fbEmoteKeys){
      window._fbEmoteKeys = true;
      window.addEventListener('keydown', function(e){
        var k = (e.key||'').toLowerCase();
        if(k==='e'){
          var inGame = false;
          try{
            inGame = (typeof mpInGame!=='undefined' && mpInGame) ||
              ((typeof state!=='undefined') && (state==='EXPLORE'||state==='BATTLE'));
          }catch(e2){}
          if(inGame) fbDoEmote(FBC.inv.favEmote||'wave');
        }
      });
    }
    if(!window._fbEmoteUITimer){
      window._fbEmoteUITimer = true;
      setInterval(function(){
        try{
          var mc = $('mpcombat'), btn = $('fbemotebtn');
          if(btn) btn.classList.toggle('on', !!(mc && mc.classList.contains('on')));
        }catch(e){}
      }, 300);
    }
  }catch(e){}
  try{
    var cs=$('custsave'); if(cs) cs.addEventListener('click', fbSaveCustomize);
    var cc2=$('custclose'); if(cc2) cc2.addEventListener('click', fbCloseCustomize);
    var sc=$('storeclose'); if(sc) sc.addEventListener('click', fbCloseStore);
    var tabs=$('storetabs');
    if(tabs) tabs.addEventListener('click', function(e){
      var t = e.target && e.target.getAttribute ? e.target.getAttribute('data-tab') : null;
      if(!t && e.target && e.target.parentElement) t = e.target.parentElement.getAttribute('data-tab');
      if(t){ fbStoreTab=t; fbConfirmId=null; beepOk();
        var kids=tabs.children;
        for(var i=0;i<kids.length;i++) kids[i].classList.toggle('sel', kids[i].getAttribute('data-tab')===t);
        fbRenderStore();
      }
    });
  }catch(e){}
  /* cross-block API: these are called from outside this IIFE (combat anim, multiplayer) */
  window.fbSwingWeapon = fbSwingWeapon;
  window.fbAttachWeapon = fbAttachWeapon;
  window.fbDressPlayer = fbDressPlayer;
  window.fbDressRemote = fbDressRemote;
  window.fbExtinguishPoof = fbExtinguishPoof;
  window.fbReignite = fbReignite;
  window.FireBabysStyle = {
    look:function(){ return FBC.look; }, gems:fbGems, coins:fbCoins,
    addGems:fbAddGems, addCoins:fbAddCoins,
    grantEmote:function(id){ try{ if(id && FBC.inv && FBC.inv.emotes.indexOf(id)<0){ FBC.inv.emotes.push(id); saveState(); } }catch(e){} },
    emote:fbDoEmote, openStore:fbOpenStore, openCustomize:fbOpenCustomize
  };
}

fbInit();

})();


</script>

</body>
</html>
